### [1] [PhysX-Omni: Unified Simulation-Ready Physical 3D Generation for Rigid, Deformable, and Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21572)
*Ziang Cao,Yinghao Liu,Haitian Li,Runmao Yao,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysX-Omni是一个统一的物理3D资产生成框架，支持刚体、可变形体和铰接体等多种类型。它包含三个核心组件：面向VLM的高效几何表示方法、首个通用仿真就绪3D数据集PhysXVerse，以及覆盖6个关键属性的综合评估基准PhysX-Bench。实验表明该方法在生成和理解方面均表现优异，并验证了其在场景生成和机器人策略学习中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成方法要么忽略物理属性，要么局限于单一资产类别（刚体、可变形体或铰接体），缺乏统一的生成框架。为此，作者提出PhysX-Omni以实现跨多种物理资产类型的统一生成。

Method: 1) 提出一种面向VLM的新型高效几何表示方法，可直接编码高分辨率3D结构而无需压缩；2) 构建首个通用仿真就绪3D数据集PhysXVerse，覆盖多种室内外类别；3) 提出PhysX-Bench评估基准，涵盖几何、绝对尺度、材质、可交互性、运动学和功能描述六个关键属性。

Result: 在传统指标和PhysX-Bench上的大量实验表明，PhysX-Omni在生成和理解任务上均表现出色。额外研究验证了其在仿真就绪场景生成和机器人策略学习等应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: PhysX-Omni是一个统一的仿真就绪物理3D生成框架，可显著推动具身AI和基于物理的仿真等下游应用的发展。

Abstract: Simulation-ready physical 3D assets have emerged as a promising direction owing to their broad applicability in downstream tasks. However, most existing 3D generation methods either neglect physical properties or are limited to a single asset category, e.g., rigid, deformable, or articulated objects. To address these limitations, we introduce PhysX-Omni, a unified framework for simulation-ready physical 3D generation across diverse asset types. Specifically, we develop a novel and efficient geometry representation tailored for Vision-Language Models, which directly encodes high-resolution 3D structures without compression, significantly improving generation performance. In addition, we construct the first general simulation-ready 3D dataset, PhysXVerse, covering diverse indoor and outdoor categories. Furthermore, to comprehensively and flexibly evaluate both generative and understanding capabilities in the wild, we propose PhysX-Bench, which encompasses six key attributes: geometry, absolute scale, material, affordance, kinematics, and function description. Extensive experiments with conventional metrics and PhysX-Bench show that PhysX-Omni performs strongly in both generation and understanding. Moreover, additional studies further validate the potential of PhysX-Omni for applications in simulation-ready scene generation and robotic policy learning. We believe PhysX-Omni can significantly advance a wide range of downstream applications, particularly in embodied AI and physics-based simulation.

</details>


### [2] [Lens: Rethinking Training Efficiency for Foundational Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21573)
*Dong Chen,Fangyun Wei,Ziyu Wan,Dongdong Chen,Jiawei Zhang,Jinjing Zhao,Sirui Zhang,Yang Yue,Zhiyang Liang,Baining Guo,Chong Luo,Jianmin Bao,Ji Li,Lei Shi,Qinhong Yang,Xiuyu Wu,Xuelu Feng,Yan Lu,Yanchen Dong,Yitong Wang,Yunuo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lens是一个38亿参数的文本到图像生成模型，通过高密度数据训练（密集描述数据集、多分辨率多宽高比批次）、语义VAE和强语言编码器等架构优化实现高效训练，仅需约19.3%的训练计算量即可达到或超越60亿参数以上模型性能，并支持多语言提示词、任意宽高比和高分辨率生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高性能T2I模型通常参数量巨大（>6B），训练计算成本高昂。本文旨在通过紧凑模型设计和高效训练策略，在显著降低计算开销的同时达到与更大模型竞争甚至超越的性能，实现T2I模型的轻量化和高效化。

Method: (1) 数据层面：构建Lens-800M数据集，使用GPT-4.1生成平均109词的高密度描述；构建包含多分辨率、多样化宽高比的训练批次以增加有效视觉覆盖。(2) 架构层面：采用语义VAE提供更好的潜在表示，使用强语言编码器加速优化并实现多语言泛化。(3) 后训练：使用分类法驱动提示的RL（Lens-RL-8K）和结构化奖励评分来抑制伪影、提升视觉质量；训练无关的系统提示搜索模块对齐用户请求；蒸馏加速实现4步推理。

Result: Lens（3.8B）在多项基准测试中达到与超过60亿参数的模型竞争甚至超越的性能，仅需Z-Image约19.3%的训练计算。单个H100 GPU上生成1024²图像需3.15秒，蒸馏Turbo版本4步生成仅需0.84秒。支持1:2至2:1任意宽高比、最高1440²分辨率，以及多种常用语言提示。

Conclusion: 通过高密度数据训练策略、精心设计的架构选择以及后训练优化流程，可以在紧凑模型规模下实现与超大模型相当甚至更优的T2I生成质量，同时大幅降低训练和推理计算成本。这为高效、轻量化的文本到图像生成模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce Lens, a 3.8B-parameter T2I model that achieves performance competitive with, and in several cases surpassing, state-of-the-art models with more than 6B parameters across various benchmarks, while requiring significantly less training compute. For example, Lens requires only about 19.3% of the training compute used by Z-Image. The training efficiency of Lens stems from two key strategies beyond its compact model size. First, we maximize data information density per training batch by (i) training on Lens-800M, a dataset of 800M densely captioned image-text pairs whose captions are generated by GPT-4.1 and contain approximately 109 words on average, providing richer semantic supervision than conventional short captions, and (ii) constructing each batch from images with multiple resolutions and diverse aspect ratios, thereby enlarging the effective visual coverage of each optimization step. Second, we improve convergence speed through careful architectural choices, including adopting a semantic VAE that provides better latent representations and employing a strong language encoder that accelerates optimization while enabling multilingual generalization from English-only training data. After pre-training, we apply RL with taxonomy-driven prompts (Lens-RL-8K) and structured reward rubrics to suppress artifacts and improve visual quality, a reasoner module with training-free system prompt search to better align user requests with the model, and distillation-based acceleration for 4-step inference. Through efficient training and systematic optimization, Lens generalizes to arbitrary aspect ratios from 1:2 to 2:1 and resolutions up to 1440^2, and supports prompts in several commonly used languages. Thanks to its compact size, Lens generates a 1024^2 image in 3.15 seconds on a single NVIDIA H100 GPU, while its distilled turbo version performs 4-step generation in 0.84 seconds.

</details>


### [3] [GenEvolve: Self-Evolving Image Generation Agents via Tool-Orchestrated Visual Experience Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21605)
*Sixiang Chen,Zhaohu Xing,Tian Ye,Xinyu Geng,Yunlong Lin,Jianyu Lai,Xuanhua He,Fuxiang Zhai,Jialin Gao,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenEvolve是一个基于工具编排视觉经验蒸馏的自我进化框架，通过将图像生成建模为工具编排轨迹，并利用视觉经验蒸馏提供密集的token级监督，使智能体在搜索、知识激活、参考选择和提示构建等方面持续改进，在公开基准和自建基准上达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式图像生成已不再是简单的提示词到图像问题，高质量生成需要智能体结合模型内部生成能力与外部资源。随着请求日益多样化和高要求，现有方法主要依赖图像级标量奖励，缺乏有效的轨迹级学习机制，亟需开发一种能通过轨迹自我进化、更有效使用工具的通用图像生成智能体。

Method: GenEvolve将每次生成尝试建模为工具编排轨迹（tool-orchestrated trajectory），智能体依次执行证据收集、参考选择、生成技能调用和提示-参考程序组合。针对同一请求对比多条轨迹，将最好与最差轨迹差异抽象为结构化视觉经验，仅提供给特权教师分支。受在线策略自蒸馏启发，视觉经验蒸馏（Visual Experience Distillation）提供密集的token级监督，帮助学生智能体内化更好的搜索、知识激活、参考选择和提示构建能力。

Result: 在公开基准和自建的GenEvolve-Bench上，GenEvolve相比强基线取得了显著提升，达到当前图像生成框架中最先进的性能。

Conclusion: GenEvolve通过工具编排轨迹和视觉经验蒸馏实现了图像生成智能体的自我进化，验证了从轨迹对比中提取结构化视觉经验进行密集监督的有效性，为通用图像生成智能体的发展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Open-ended image generation is no longer a simple prompt-to-image problem. High-quality generation often requires an agent to combine a model's internal generative ability with external resources. As requests become more diverse and demanding, we aim to develop a general image-generation agent that can self-evolve through trajectories and use tools more effectively across varied generation challenges. To this end, we propose GenEvolve, a self-evolving framework based on Tool-Orchestrated Visual Experience Distillation. In GenEvolve, each generation attempt is modeled as a tool-orchestrated trajectory, where the agent gathers evidence, selects references, invokes generation skills, and composes them into a prompt-reference program. Unlike existing agentic generation methods that mainly rely on image-level scalar rewards, GenEvolve compares multiple trajectories for the same request and abstracts best-worst differences into structured visual experience, provided only to a privileged teacher branch. Inspired by on-policy self-distillation, Visual Experience Distillation provides dense token-level supervision, helping the student internalize better search, knowledge activation, reference selection, and prompt construction. We further construct GenEvolve-Data and GenEvolve-Bench. Experiments on public benchmarks and GenEvolve-Bench show substantial gains over strong baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance among current image-generation frameworks. Our website is as follows: https://ephemeral182.github.io/GenEvolve/

</details>


### [4] [MM-Conv: A Multimodal Dataset and Benchmark for Context-Aware Grounding in 3D Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21796)
*Anna Deichler,Jim O'Regan,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Lubos Marcinek,Anna Klezovich,Iolanda Leite,Jonas Beskow*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于动态3D环境的指代交流基准数据集（含6.7小时VR交互、4200+指代表达），以及一个将对话歧义消解与视觉定位解耦的两阶段流水线，该方法相比端到端方法在指代消解上提升11-22个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在静态图像任务上表现优异，但在动态、多轮对话中难以处理歧义指代表达，缺乏针对动态3D环境中自然对话指代交流的基准和方法。

Method: 构建了包含同步语音、动作、注视和3D场景几何的VR交互数据集（6.7小时，4200+指代表达）；提出两阶段流水线：先进行上下文改写消除对话歧义，再进行视觉定位（使用GroundingDINO）。

Result: 上下文改写方法在指代消解上平均提升11-22个百分点；GroundingDINO在代词指代上达到56.7%，几乎是最好端到端基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 将语言推理与视觉感知解耦比端到端方法在对话指代消解上更有效。

Abstract: Grounding language in the physical world requires AI systems to interpret references that emerge dynamically during conversation. While current vision-language models (VLMs) excel at static image tasks, they struggle to resolve ambiguous expressions in spontaneous, multi-turn dialogue. We address this gap by introducing (1) a benchmark for referential communication in dynamic 3D environments, built from 6.7 hours of egocentric VR interaction with synchronized speech, motion, gaze, and 3D scene geometry, and (2) a two-stage grounding pipeline that explicitly resolves conversational ambiguity before visual localization. The benchmark includes over 4,200 manually verified referring expressions spanning full, partitive, and pronominal types. Our contextual rewriting approach improves grounding performance by 11-22 percentage points on average, with a pure detector (GroundingDINO) reaching 56.7% on pronominals after rewriting, nearly double the best end-to-end baseline. Results demonstrate that decoupling linguistic reasoning from visual perception is more effective than end-to-end approaches for conversational grounding.

</details>


### [5] [Guided Trajectory Optimization with Sparse Scaling for Test-Time Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21907)
*Gang Dai,Yining Huang,Yiming Xia,Guohao Chen,Shuaicheng Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RTS是一种新颖的奖励引导轨迹缩放方法，通过两种核心创新（奖励引导的噪声优化策略和基于PCA曲率分析的稀疏测试时缩放框架）来提升扩散模型的生成性能，在GenEval Score和ImageReward Score上分别实现了15.6%和60.4%的提升，达到了新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时缩放方法局限于静态、预定义的噪声池，且在整个去噪轨迹中缺乏灵活的噪声探索能力，无法充分挖掘扩散模型的生成潜力。

Method: 1) 奖励引导的噪声优化策略，主动引导搜索朝向有前景的区域；2) 稀疏测试时缩放框架结合PCA驱动的曲率分析方案，优先处理关键中间步骤，压缩搜索空间。

Result: 在GenEval Score上超过基线15.6%，在ImageReward Score上提升60.4%，达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: RTS为扩散模型提供了更有效的测试时缩放实践指南，在扩散架构中实现了生成质量的显著提升。

Abstract: The efficient Test-Time Scaling (TTS) paradigm offers a promising perspective for enhancing the generation performance of diffusion models. However, current solutions are limited to a static, pre-defined noise pool and suffer from inflexible noise exploration across the denoising trajectory. To bridge this gap, we propose RTS, a novel Reward-guided Trajectory Scaling method to fully unlock the generative potential of diffusion models. Unlike existing methods, RTS facilitates the synthesis of refined, high-fidelity images via two core innovations: 1) a reward-guided noise optimization strategy to actively direct the search towards promising regions; and 2) a sparse test-time scaling framework together with a PCA-driven curvature analysis scheme to prioritize key intermediate steps in the entire denoising space, effectively compressing the search space. Experiments show our approach outperforms baselines by 15.6% across GenEval Score, and a 60.4% enhancement in ImageReward score, setting a new SOTA while providing a practical guideline for more effective test-time scaling across diffusion-specific architectures.

</details>


### [6] [MAVEN: A Multi-stage Agentic Annotation Pipeline for Video Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21917)
*Han Zhang,Wanting Jiang,Tomasz Kornuta,Tian Zheng,Vidya Murali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAVEN是一个多阶段智能体流水线，通过Chain-of-Thought推理轨迹将原始视频自动转换为多任务训练数据，在交通视频事件推理任务上微调Cosmos-Reason2-8B，在CCTV评估集上超越Gemini 2.5 Pro和3.1 Flash（MCQ准确率提升38.8个点），在AccidentBench上通过RL后训练全面超越两个Gemini基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频事件推理需要高质量的结构化标注（包含时间、空间、原因、结果等），但人工标注无法规模化。现有方法缺乏自动化生成多任务训练数据且能跨领域自适应的能力。

Method: 提出MAVEN流水线：(1) 合成多尺度时空事件描述(MSTED)，融合三个互补的标题级别；(2) 以MSTED为唯一输入，生成多格式问答对；(3) 智能体驱动的领域自适应，自动重设计所有提示；(4) 分层精炼循环，根据错误分类树追溯根因并定向修复。

Result: 标注5300+交通视频，微调Cosmos-Reason2-8B在CCTV评测集上超越Gemini 2.5 Pro和3.1 Flash（MCQ +38.8点）；AccidentBench上仅用CCTV数据训练提升+10.7点并匹配Gemini 2.5 Pro，加入自适应标注后接近Gemini 3.1 Flash，RL后训练全面超越两个基线。

Conclusion: MAVEN展示了智能体驱动的自动标注流水线能够高效生成高质量视频事件推理训练数据，并具备良好的跨领域适应性（仓库监控、公共安全视频）。

Abstract: Training Vision Language Models (VLMs) for video event reasoning requires high-quality structured annotations capturing not only what happened, but when, where, why, and with what consequence, at a scale manual labelling cannot support. We present MAVEN (Multi-stage Agentic Video Event aNnotation), a multi-stage agentic pipeline that turns raw videos into multi-task training data with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning traces, organized around a designated Event of Focus. At its core, MAVEN synthesizes a Multi-Scale Spatio-Temporal Event Description (MSTED) from three complementary caption levels; this explicit intermediate serves as the sole input to downstream Q&A generation across multiple task formats. Crucially, MAVEN supports agent-driven domain adaptation: given a new video dataset and target question examples, the agent redesigns all prompts top-down without manual re-engineering. A hierarchical refinement loop further classifies annotation errors against a taxonomy, traces root causes to the originating pipeline stage, and applies targeted edits that rewrite prompts or modify the pipeline structure itself, iteratively improving data quality. We apply MAVEN to label over 5,300 traffic videos and fine-tune Cosmos-Reason2-8B on the resulting data. On a private CCTV evaluation set, fine-tuning surpasses both Gemini 2.5 Pro and 3.1 Flash, including a $+38.8$-point gain in MCQ accuracy over zero-shot. On AccidentBench, CCTV-only training lifts Cosmos-Reason2 by $+10.7$ MCQ points and matches Gemini 2.5 Pro despite seeing no dashcam videos; adding agent-adapted dashcam annotations narrows the gap to Gemini 3.1 Flash, and RL post-training pushes overall performance past both Gemini baselines. Qualitative results on warehouse surveillance and public safety videos further show the agentic workflow readily adapts the pipeline to new domains.

</details>


### [7] [SDGBiasBench: Benchmarking and Mitigating Vision--Language Models' Biases in Sustainable Development Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21919)
*Zihang Lin,Huaiyuan Qin,Muli Yang,Hongyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SDGBiasBench基准套件（含50万道多选题和5万回归任务），用于评估视觉语言模型在SDG监测中的决策偏差和估计偏差，并提出了训练无关的即插即用去偏方法CADE（对比自适应去偏集成），可将多选准确率提升高达25%，回归MAE降低达12点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立评估定性判断或定量估计，无法揭示模型在SDG监测中用先验替代证据时产生的系统性偏差，因此需要一个大规模、综合性的基准来全面评估VLM在SDG导向的视觉语言推理中的偏差。

Method: 1）构建SDGBiasBench，包含50万道专家参与的多选题和5万回归任务，覆盖多步推理、视觉线索、情境知识和发展指标；2）提出CADE（对比自适应去偏集成），一种无需训练、即插即用的方法，利用模态特定的答案先验来缓解偏差。

Result: 当前VLM存在内在的SDG偏差，预测常由SDG特定先验而非可靠多模态线索驱动；CADE在多个VLM上将多选准确率提升高达25%，回归MAE降低达12点。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了VLM在SDG监测中的系统性偏差问题，并提供了有效的无训练去偏方法，旨在推动更公平、更可靠的面向可持续发展的AI系统开发。

Abstract: Assessing progress toward the Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) requires multi-step reasoning over visual cues, contextual knowledge, and development indicators, where incomplete evidence use and imperfect evidence integration can introduce hidden prediction biases. Real-world SDG monitoring further spans both qualitative judgments and quantitative estimation. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these aspects in isolation, obscuring systematic biases that emerge when models substitute priors for evidence. To address this gap, we propose SDGBiasBench, a large-scale benchmark suite for SDG-oriented vision-language reasoning. Spanning 500k expert-involved multiple-choice questions and 50k regression tasks, the benchmark enables comprehensive assessment of both decision-level and estimation-level bias in Vision--Language Models (VLMs). Evaluations on SDGBiasBench reveal an intrinsic SDG bias in current VLMs, where predictions are frequently driven by SDG specific priors rather than reliable multi-modal cues. To mitigate such bias, we propose CADE (Contrastive Adaptive Debias Ensemble), a training-free, plug-and-play method that leverages modality-specific answer priors. CADE yields significant gains on the proposed benchmark, improving multiple-choice accuracy by up to 25% and reducing regression MAE by up to 12 points across multiple VLMs. We hope our work can foster the development of more fair and reliable AI systems for sustainable development.

</details>


### [8] [Visual-Advantage On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21924)
*Ruiqi Liu,Xiaolei Lv,Gengsheng Li,Ximo Zhu,Zhiheng Wang,Zhengbo Zhang,Junkai Chen,Zhiheng Li,Bo Li,Jun Gao,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VA-OPD，一种利用视觉优势（VA）区分视觉关键令牌与语言令牌的在线知识蒸馏方法，在数学推理和视觉理解任务上一致优于标准在线蒸馏，且收益随教师规模和数据规模单调增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线蒸馏能提升学生模型输出质量，但未能增强其对视觉输入的依赖：在视觉关键令牌上，无论有无细粒度视觉细节，学生预测几乎不变，而教师预测高度依赖视觉输入。需要一种方法让视觉监督信号不被周围大量语言令牌稀释。

Method: 定义视觉优势（VA）为教师在有/无细粒度视觉细节条件下对学生生成rollout进行评分时的token级对数概率差。VA集中在少数令牌上，高VA令牌携带视觉监督信号。VA-OPD在两种粒度上使用VA：轨迹平均VA的rollout级重加权，以及高VA和低VA组分别计算token级KL散度。

Result: 在Geometry3K和ViRL39K上训练，在8个数学推理和视觉理解基准上评估，使用Qwen3-VL家族三个教师规模（4B、8B、32B）。VA-OPD在每个基准上均优于标准在线蒸馏，增益沿教师规模和数据规模单调增长。

Conclusion: VA-OPD通过区分视觉关键令牌与语言令牌，有效增强了VLM学生模型对视觉输入的依赖，且收益随教师规模和数据规模一致放大。

Abstract: On-policy knowledge distillation has proven effective for language models, yet its application to vision-language models (VLMs) remains underexplored. We observe that standard on-policy distillation can improve a student's output quality while failing to strengthen its reliance on visual input: on vision-critical tokens, the student's predictions remain largely unchanged whether or not fine-grained visual detail is present, even though the teacher's predictions depend heavily on it.To make this difference observable, we introduce visual advantage (VA), the token-level log-probability difference when the teacher scores a student-generated rollout with versus without access to fine-grained visual detail. VA is concentrated in a small minority of tokens, and these high-VA tokens are the ones that actually carry the visual supervision signal. This motivates a distillation objective that treats them differently from language scaffolding, so their contribution is not diluted by the abundant surrounding language tokens.We propose Visual-Advantage On-Policy Distillation (VA-OPD), which uses VA at two granularities: rollout-level reweighting by trajectory-averaged VA, and token-level KL averaged within high-VA and low-VA groups separately. We train on two math datasets (Geometry3K and ViRL39K) and evaluate on eight benchmarks covering both mathematical reasoning and visual understanding, across three teacher sizes (4B, 8B, and 32B) on the Qwen3-VL family. VA-OPD improves over standard on-policy distillation on every benchmark, with the gain growing monotonically along both the teacher-size and data-scale axes, suggesting that these factors compound consistently.

</details>


### [9] [EvoVid: Temporal-Centric Self-Evolution for Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21931)
*Shiqi Huang,Ziyue Wang,Zhongrong Zuo,Han Qiu,Qi She,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoVid提出了一个以时间为中心的自进化框架，使Video-LLMs能够直接从原始未标注视频中通过两个时间感知奖励（提问者奖励和解答者奖励）进行自我提升，在多个基准测试上取得与监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLMs的强化学习管道严重依赖人工标注的任务和解决方案，成本高昂且受限于人类专家知识；而自进化框架主要针对文本和图像等静态模态设计，无法捕捉视频推理中的时间动态。

Method: 提出EvoVid框架，包含两个互补的时间中心奖励机制：1) 时间感知的提问者奖励，通过时间扰动敏感性鼓励生成时间依赖性问题；2) 时间基元的解答者奖励，通过视频片段定位提供自动时间监督。

Result: 在4个基础模型和6个基准测试上进行了广泛实验，一致优于基础模型和现有自进化基线，达到了与监督方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 以时间为中心的自进化是视频理解与推理的一种有效且可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Recent Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in video reasoning through reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing RL pipelines rely heavily on human-annotated tasks and solutions, making them costly to scale and fundamentally constrained by human expertise. Self-evolving frameworks have recently emerged as a promising alternative through autonomous Questioner-Solver self-play. Unfortunately, these approaches are primarily designed for static modalities such as text and images, fundamentally failing to capture the temporal dynamics that are central to video reasoning. In this work, we propose $\textbf{EvoVid}$, a temporal-centric self-evolving framework that enables Video-LLMs to improve directly from raw, unannotated videos. Specifically, we introduce two complementary temporal-centric rewards: a temporal-aware Questioner reward that encourages temporally dependent question generation through temporal perturbation sensitivity, and a temporal-grounded Solver reward that provides automatic temporal supervision via inherent video segment localization. Extensive experiments across four base models and six benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over both base models and existing self-evolving baselines, achieving competitive performance with supervised methods. These results highlight temporal-centric self-evolution as an effective and scalable paradigm for video understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [10] [MLLMs Know When Before Speaking: Revealing and Recovering Temporal Grounding via Attention Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21954)
*Dazhao Du,Liao Duan,Jian Liu,Tao Han,Yujia Zhang,Eric Liu,Xi Chen,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大模型（MLLM）在视频时间定位任务中存在“感知-生成鸿沟”：预填充阶段注意力头（TG-Heads）能准确定位目标区间，但自回归解码时注意力偏移到视觉显著但无关区域。据此提出推理时“先读取后重新生成”框架，通过提取TG-Head注意信号裁剪视频上下文，无参训练即可提升VTG性能，在三个基准上最高提升+3.5 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视频时间定位任务中时间戳预测不可靠，已有方案要么需要昂贵的时序标注后训练，要么依赖粗糙的无训练启发式方法，缺乏高效且无需额外训练的解决方案。

Method: (1) 发现并定义TG-Heads（时间定位头），在预填充阶段其查询-视频注意力聚焦于真实区间；(2) 提出推理时先读取（将TG-Head预填充注意力转化为去偏帧级相关信号并提取高注意力区间）后重新生成（将视觉上下文裁剪到该区间，通过视频裁剪或注意力掩码抑制干扰）的框架。

Result: 在MiMo-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-8B和TimeLens-8B上，在三个VTG基准测试中一致提升，最高+3.5 mIoU，无需参数更新和架构变化。

Conclusion: 揭示并验证了MLLM在视频时间定位中的感知-生成鸿沟现象，提出的无训练推理框架有效弥合了这一鸿沟，为提升MLLM时序理解能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Video temporal grounding (VTG), which localizes the start and end times of a queried event in an untrimmed video, is a key test of whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) understand not only what happens but also when it happens. Although modern MLLMs describe video content fluently, their timestamp predictions remain unreliable, while existing remedies either require costly post-training on temporal annotations or rely on coarse training-free heuristics. In this work, we probe the cross-modal attention of MLLMs and uncover a perception-generation gap. Our key finding is that MLLMs often know the target interval during prefill, but lose this signal when generating the final answer. In the prefill stage, a sparse set of attention heads, which we call \emph{Temporal Grounding Heads} (TG-Heads), concentrates query-to-video attention on the ground-truth interval. During autoregressive decoding, however, the answer tokens shift attention away from this interval toward visually salient but query-irrelevant segments. This observation motivates an inference-time read-then-regenerate framework. We first convert TG-Head prefill attention into a debiased frame-level relevance signal and extract the high-attention interval it highlights. We then re-invoke the MLLM with visual context restricted to this interval, using video cropping or attention masking to suppress distractors. Without parameter updates and architectural changes, our framework consistently improves MiMo-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-8B, and TimeLens-8B on three VTG benchmarks, with gains of up to +3.5 mIoU. The project website can be found at https://ddz16.github.io/mllmsknowwhen.github.io/.

</details>


### [11] [Bounding-Box Trajectories Matter for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21957)
*Inpyo Song,Jangwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajVAD框架，通过利用边界框轨迹作为主要异常线索进行视频异常检测。其仅轨迹变体TrajVAD-T在ShanghaiTech上达到87.7% AP，超越所有基于姿态的方法；融合姿态信息的TrajVAD-P进一步提升至88.6% AUROC和90.9% AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于人体姿态的视频异常检测方法忽略了边界框轨迹这一在姿态管线中天然可用的信息，而轨迹本身可作为有效的异常线索。

Method: 提出TrajVAD框架，使用归一化流对多类边界框轨迹进行建模，以学习正常运动模式。包括仅轨迹变体TrajVAD-T和融合姿态信息的扩展变体TrajVAD-P。

Result: TrajVAD-T在ShanghaiTech上AP达87.7%，超越所有对比的基于姿态的方法；在MSAD上取得最佳结果。TrajVAD-P在ShanghaiTech上达到88.6% AUROC和90.9% AP。

Conclusion: 边界框轨迹是一种有效但被低估的视频异常检测模态，将其显式建模可显著提升检测性能。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection is critical for public safety and security, yet remains highly challenging despite extensive research due to large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and scene dynamics. Among existing approaches, human pose-based methods have emerged as a major line of research, showing strong performance since many anomalies in public datasets involve humans and pose representations are robust to appearance changes while providing compact motion descriptions. However, these methods often overlook bounding-box trajectories, although such information is inherently available in pose-based pipelines. In this paper, we explicitly leverage these trajectories as a primary anomaly cue. We present TrajVAD, a framework that models multi-class bounding-box trajectories using normalizing flows to learn normal kinematic patterns. Its trajectory-only variant (TrajVAD-T) eliminates pose estimation and surpasses all compared pose-based methods on ShanghaiTech in AP (87.7%), while achieving the best results on MSAD. An extended version (TrajVAD-P) incorporates pose information and further improves performance to 88.6% AUROC and 90.9% AP on ShanghaiTech, highlighting bounding-box trajectories as an effective yet underexplored modality for video anomaly detection.

</details>


### [12] [Video as Natural Augmentation: Towards Unified AI-Generated Image and Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21977)
*Zhengcen Li,Chenyang Jiang,Liangxu Su,Tong Shao,Shiyang Zhou,Ming Tao,Jingyong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现现有AI生成图像检测器在处理视频帧时性能显著下降，分析了跨模态差异的根源（视频处理偏移和生成器指纹），提出VINA框架——通过联合训练图像和视频数据，利用视频帧作为自然增强，结合跨模态对比学习对齐表征，在14个基准上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成内容检测器在跨模态（图像→视频帧）场景下性能崩溃，缺乏统一且鲁棒的检测框架。

Method: 提出VINA框架：(1) 联合图像与视频数据训练，将视频帧作为物理真实的自然增强；(2) 引入跨模态监督对比学习目标，对齐图像与视频在真/假决策边界下的表征。

Result: 在14个图像、视频及野外基准上，VINA在几乎所有评估设置中取得SOTA性能，实现双向收益，提升鲁棒性和迁移性，无需复杂增强或数据集特定调参。

Conclusion: 视频帧作为自然增强能够有效弥合图像与视频间的跨模态检测鸿沟，联合训练策略是提升AIGC检测器泛化性的有效途径。

Abstract: AI-generated content (AIGC) is rapidly improving, creating an urgent need for detectors that generalize across data sources, deployment pipelines, and visual modalities. A strongly generalizable detector should remain robust under distributional variations. However, we identify a consistent failure mode: SOTA AI-generated image detectors often collapse when applied to frames extracted from videos. Through systematic analysis, we show that this cross-modal gap arises from both entangled synthesis-agnostic video processing shifts, including color conversion, codec compression, resizing, and blur, and model-specific fingerprints introduced by modern video generators. Motivated by these findings, we propose VINA (Video as Natural Augmentation), a unified AIGC detection framework that jointly trains on image and video data. VINA uses video frames as physically grounded natural augmentations and further introduces a cross-modal supervised contrastive objective to align image and video representations under a shared real/fake decision boundary. Extensive experiments on 14 image, video, and in-the-wild benchmarks show that VINA delivers bidirectional gains, improves robustness and transferability, and achieves state-of-the-art performance across nearly all evaluated settings without complex augmentation or dataset-specific tuning.

</details>


### [13] [Interpreting and Enhancing Emotional Circuits in Large Vision-Language Models via Cross-Modal Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21980)
*Chengsheng Zhang,Chenghao Sun,Zhining Xie,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过构建基于引导向量的因果归因框架，揭示了LVLMs在描述性情感推理中"适应-聚合-执行"三阶段机制：中层通过情感特定注意力头聚合视觉情感线索，深层通过情感通用路径转化为叙事生成，并基于此发现通过推理时干预有效缓解情感幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在情感理解上表现出色，但其将抽象视觉刺激转化为连贯情感叙事的内部机制尚不明确，主要缺乏视觉反事实数据和情感表达的弥散性带来的研究困难。

Method: 提出基于引导向量的因果归因框架，构建专门数据集，发现"适应-聚合-执行"三阶段情感回路机制，并通过调控情感信息路由（增强注意力流和语义激活）进行推理时干预。

Result: 在MER-UniBench基准上，所提方法通过推理时干预显著提升性能，有效缓解情感幻觉，验证了所发现回路的因果保真度。

Conclusion: 揭示了LVLMs情感推理中功能解耦的特性（中层情感特异→深层情感通用），并证明了基于此机制的推理时干预可有效提升情感表达质量。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) represent a significant leap towards empathetic agents, demonstrating remarkable capabilities in emotion understanding. However, the internal mechanisms governing how LVLMs translate abstract visual stimuli into coherent emotional narratives remain largely unexplored, primarily due to the scarcity of visual counterfactuals and the diffuse nature of emotional expression. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing a steering-vector-based causal attribution framework tailored for descriptive emotional reasoning. To this end, we construct a specialized dataset to demystify the emotional circuits underlying the three-stage ``Adapt-Aggregate-Execute'' mechanism. Crucially, we discover a functional decoupling: visual emotional cues are aggregated in middle layers via sentiment-specific attention heads, but are subsequently translated into narrative generation in deep layers through emotion-general pathways. Guided by these insights, we regulate the emotional information routing to strengthen attention flow and amplify the semantic activation to consolidate expression. Extensive experiments on the comprehensive MER-UniBench demonstrate that our methods significantly improve performance via inference-time intervention, effectively mitigating emotional hallucinations and corroborating the causal fidelity of the discovered circuits.

</details>


### [14] [RiT: Vanilla Diffusion Transformers Suffice in Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21981)
*Le Zhang,Ning Mang,Aishwarya Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Representation Image Transformer (RiT)，利用预训练 DINOv2 特征空间的统计优势（高有效秩、条件数好、低峰度、低插值误差）进行 flow matching 的 x-prediction 训练，在 ImageNet 256×256 上以 676M 参数达到 FID 1.45（无 guidance）和 1.14（有 CFG），且仅需 5 步 Heun 即可达 FID 2.0，无需蒸馏或一致性训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练表示空间（如 DINOv2）相较于像素空间，是否因其更优的统计分布（而非仅因低维流形）更有利于 flow matching 的 x-prediction 学习，从而简化模型设计并提升生成效率。

Method: 1) 从四个几何维度（内在维度、有效秩、协方差条件数、超额峰度、流形上插值误差）系统比较像素、SD-VAE、DINOv2 特征空间；2) 提出 RiT：一个 vanilla Diffusion Transformer，在冻结的 DINOv2 特征上采用 x-prediction 训练，仅增加维度感知噪声调度和联合 [CLS]-patch 建模。

Result: DINOv2 相比像素空间：有效秩高 7.3×、协方差条件数好 35×、超额峰度低 11.5×、流形上插值误差低 1.7×，内在维度相近（~33）。RiT 在 ImageNet 256×256 上达到 FID 1.45（无 guidance）和 1.14（有 CFG），比 DiT^DH-XL 少 19% 参数（676M vs 839M）；5 步 Heun 达 FID 2.0，10 步达 1.25。

Conclusion: 预训练表示空间的统计特性（而非单纯低维流形）是 flow matching 高效学习的关键；RiT 以极简设计实现了 SOTA 性能与极快采样速度，无需特殊预测头或黎曼传输等复杂组件。

Abstract: Flow matching with $x$-prediction -- regressing the clean data point rather than the ambient velocity -- is known to exploit low-dimensional manifold structure effectively in pixel space \cite{li2025back}. We ask whether a pretrained representation space, while containing a low-dimensional data manifold of comparable intrinsic dimensionality, offers a distribution more favorable for flow-matching learning. Comparing pixel, SD-VAE, and DINOv2 features along four geometric axes, we find that pixel and DINOv2 share nearly identical intrinsic dimensionalities (both $\hat{d}\!\approx\!33$) yet DINOv2 exhibits $7.3\times$ higher effective rank, $35\times$ better covariance conditioning, $11.5\times$ lower excess kurtosis, and $1.7\times$ lower on-manifold interpolation error; SD-VAE latents are consistently intermediate, indicating that the advantage stems from representation-learning objectives rather than mere compression. These statistical properties render the flow-matching regression well-conditioned and remove the need for the specialized prediction heads or Riemannian transport used by prior DINOv2 diffusion methods. We propose the \emph{Representation Image Transformer} (RiT): a vanilla Diffusion Transformer trained by $x$-prediction on frozen DINOv2 features, augmented only by a dimension-aware noise schedule and joint \texttt{[CLS]}-patch modeling. On ImageNet $256{\times}256$, RiT attains FID 1.45 without guidance and 1.14 with classifier-free guidance, outperforming DiT$^\text{DH}$-XL with $19\%$ fewer parameters (676M vs.\ 839M). The resulting ODE is efficiently solvable at coarse discretizations: with classifier-free guidance, $5$ Heun steps already reach FID 2.0 and $10$ steps reach 1.25, without distillation or consistency training. Code at https://github.com/lezhang7/RiT.

</details>


### [15] [Learning Spatiotemporal Sensitivity in Video LLMs via Counterfactual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21988)
*Dazhao Du,Jian Liu,Jialong Qin,Tao Han,Bohai Gu,Fangqi Zhu,Yujia Zhang,Eric Liu,Xi Chen,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实关系策略优化（CRPO）框架，通过构建反事实视频（水平翻转、时间反转）并设计反事实关系奖励（CRR），解决视频大语言模型在RL后训练中依赖静态捷径而非追踪时空动态的问题。同时引入DyBench基准（含3,014个视频和成对准确率指标）进行评估。实验表明CRPO在改善时空敏感性方面优于先前RL方法，同时保持通用视频性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在RL后训练中，单纯以正确性为奖励会强化模型依赖单帧线索和语言先验的捷径策略，而非真正追踪时空动态变化，导致模型缺乏时空敏感性。

Method: 提出CRPO双分支RL框架：1）通过水平翻转和时间反转构建反事实视频；2）在原始和反事实两个分支上同时训练；3）引入反事实关系奖励（CRR），鼓励动态问题的答案应随视觉变化而改变，静态问题的答案保持不变。同时构建DyBench基准，包含可逆动态、移动方向和事件序列三类任务，采用成对准确率指标防止固定答案捷径。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上，CRPO比基础模型在DyBench P-Acc上提升7.7，在TimeBlind I-Acc上提升8.2，优于先前RL方法，且保持通用视频性能。

Conclusion: CRPO通过跨分支约束有效抑制了捷径策略，提升了模型的时空敏感性，证明了反事实关系约束是改进视频LLM动态理解能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video LLMs) achieve strong benchmark accuracy, yet often answer video questions through shortcuts such as single-frame cues and language priors rather than by tracking spatiotemporal dynamics. This issue is exacerbated in RL post-training, where correctness-only rewards can further reinforce shortcut policies that obtain high reward without tracking video dynamics. We address this by asking a controlled counterfactual question: if the visual world changed while the question remained fixed, should the answer change or stay the same? Based on this view, we propose \textbf{Counterfactual Relational Policy Optimization (CRPO)}, a dual-branch RL framework for improving \emph{spatiotemporal sensitivity}. CRPO constructs counterfactual videos through horizontal flips and temporal reversals, trains on both original and counterfactual branches, and introduces a \textbf{Counterfactual Relation Reward (CRR)} between their answers. CRR encourages answers to change for dynamic questions and remain unchanged for static questions. This cross-branch constraint makes it difficult for shortcut policies to be consistently rewarded across both branches. To evaluate this property, we introduce \textbf{DyBench}, a paired counterfactual video benchmark with 3,014 videos covering reversible dynamics, moving direction, and event sequence, together with a strict pair-accuracy metric that prevents fixed-answer shortcuts from inflating scores. Experiments show that CRPO outperforms prior RL methods on spatiotemporal-sensitive evaluations while maintaining competitive general video performance. On Qwen3-VL-8B, CRPO improves DyBench P-Acc by +7.7 and TimeBlind I-Acc by +8.2 over the base model, indicating improved spatiotemporal sensitivity rather than stronger reliance on static shortcuts. The project website can be found at https://ddz16.github.io/crpo.github.io/ .

</details>


### [16] [Virtual 3D H&E Staining from Phase-contrast Back-illumination Interference Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22000)
*Anthony Song,Boyan Zhou,Mayank Golhar,Marisa Morakis,Alex Baras,Nicholas Durr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HistoBIT3D——首个体素级配对的BIT与荧光标记细胞核数据集，并开发了一种无监督虚拟染色框架，能够将具有移变对比度的BIT体积数据转换为逼真的H&E染色体积，在零样本Cellpose评估下显著提升了3D细胞核分割精度和边界保持能力，建立了定量验证的3D虚拟H&E染色流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无染色的BIT显微技术可快速获取未处理组织的三维体积影像，但将其转化为临床可解释的H&E图像面临两个挑战：BIT成像的移变对比度问题，以及缺乏定量验证基准来评估虚拟染色的结构保真度。

Method: 1) 构建首个体素级配对的BIT与荧光标记细胞核数据集HistoBIT3D；2) 提出双向多尺度内容一致性（bidirectional multiscale content consistency）与跨域风格复用（cross-domain style reuse）的虚拟染色框架，处理BIT的移变对比度；3) 利用该数据集进行无监督虚拟染色的定量评估，验证与Ground-truth细胞核分布的结构保真度。

Result: 该方法在真实性指标上达到最优效果，同时在零样本Cellpose评估下显著提升了3D细胞核分割精度和边界保持能力。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了经过定量验证、结构保真、可扩展的3D虚拟H&E染色流水线，推动了无切片、体积计算组织病理学的发展。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) histopathology of unprocessed tissues has the potential to transform disease management by enabling volumetric characterization of tissue microarchitecture and in-vivo assessment. Back-illumination Interference Tomography (BIT) is a new phase microscopy technology that provides rapid, non-destructive volumetric imaging of unprocessed tissues. However, translating BIT volumes into clinically interpretable H&E images remains challenging, particularly due to shift-variant contrast and the absence of quantitative validation benchmarks. We introduce HistoBIT3D, the first voxel-wise paired BIT and fluorescence-labeled nuclei dataset, enabling quantitative evaluation of structural preservation in unsupervised virtual staining against ground-truth nuclear distributions. Using this dataset, we present a novel virtual staining framework that translates BIT volumes with shift-variant contrast into realistic H&E volumes by leveraging bidirectional multiscale content consistency and cross-domain style reuse to enhance structural fidelity and perceptual realism. Our method achieves state-of-the-art realism metrics while significantly improving 3D nuclei segmentation accuracy and boundary preservation under zero-shot Cellpose evaluation. Together, these contributions establish a quantitatively validated, structurally faithful, and scalable pipeline for 3D virtual H&E staining, advancing the paradigm of slide-free, volumetric computational histopathology. Our data and code are available at: https://github.com/aasong113/HistoBIT3D_VirtualStaining.

</details>


### [17] [PointLLM-R: Enhancing 3D Point Cloud Reasoning via Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22013)
*Chaoqi Chen,Qile Xu,Wenjun Zhou,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的框架，用于构建面向3D点云理解的大规模Chain-of-Thought监督数据。通过两阶段流水线（质量评估与参考引导的精炼 + 人工循环提示优化）构建了PoCoTI数据集（55K样本），并微调PointLLM得到PointLLM-R模型。该模型在生成式3D分类和描述任务上达到最先进水平，并能鲁棒泛化到真实扫描点云和多轮对话场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云数据的非规则结构使得语言理解具有挑战性，现有3D多模态模型缺乏显式推理能力。虽然CoT推理在LLM和图像MLLM中表现出色，但在3D理解领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出两阶段数据驱动框架：1) 通过VLM质量评估和参考引导精炼优化点-文本指令数据；2) 采用Human-in-the-Loop Prompt Optimization (HiLPO) 合成高质量推理路径。基于此构建PoCoTI数据集（55K样本），并微调PointLLM得到PointLLM-R模型。

Result: PointLLM-R在生成式3D分类和描述任务上达到最先进水平，并展现出对真实扫描点云和多轮对话场景的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的CoT监督能有效提升3D多模态语言模型的推理能力，为3D点云理解中的显式推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Understanding 3D point clouds through language remains a fundamental challenge in computer graphics and visual computing, due to the irregular structure of point cloud data and the lack of explicit reasoning in existing 3D multimodal models. While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has shown strong effectiveness in LLMs and image-based MLLMs, its extension to 3D understanding remains largely underexplored. In this paper, we propose a data-centric framework for constructing large-scale CoT supervision tailored to 3D point cloud understanding. Our framework consists of a two-stage pipeline that first refines point-text instruction data via vision-language-model-based quality evaluation and reference-guided refinement, and then synthesizes high-quality reasoning paths through Human-in-the-Loop Prompt Optimization (HiLPO). Using this approach, we build PoCoTI, a CoT-enhanced point-text instruction-following dataset containing 55K samples with explicit reasoning paths. Fine-tuning PointLLM on PoCoTI yields PointLLM-R, a reasoning-capable 3D multimodal language model. Extensive experiments on generative 3D classification and captioning demonstrate that PointLLM-R achieves state-of-the-art performance and generalizes robustly to real-world scanned point clouds and multi-turn dialogue scenarios.

</details>


### [18] [ORBIS: Output-Guided Token Reduction with Distribution-Aware Matching for Video Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22015)
*Hangyeol Lee,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORBIS提出了一种软硬件协同设计的视频DiT加速器，通过利用上一时间步的输出激活获得更准确的token间相似度，并提出分布感知的token匹配算法(DATM)及专用流水线硬件，相比SOTA方法AsymRnR实现了约2倍更高的token缩减率，相比NVIDIA A100 GPU实现高达4.5倍加速和79.3%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频DiT中3D时空注意力机制导致token长度随帧数线性增长，计算成本急剧上升。现有token缩减方法依赖不准确的相似度估计和轻量级匹配算法，匹配质量差且加速效果有限。

Method: 提出ORBIS软硬件协同加速器：(1)利用上一时间步输出激活获取更准确的token间相似度，提高匹配质量和缩减率；(2)提出分布感知token匹配算法(DATM)，捕获全局token分布并显式最小化token对损失；(3)设计深度流水线专用硬件，通过量化将硬件开销降至总面积的2.4%。

Result: 相比AsymRnR实现约2倍更高的token缩减率；相比NVIDIA A100 GPU实现高达4.5倍加速和79.3%能耗降低；专用硬件仅占总面积2.4%，精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: ORBIS通过软硬件协同设计有效解决了视频DiT中token缩减的匹配质量瓶颈，实现了显著的加速和能效提升，验证了利用时间步间激活信息指导token匹配的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformer (DiT) has emerged as a powerful model architecture for generating high-quality images and videos. In the case of video DiT, 3D Spatio-Temporal Attention increases token length in proportion to the number of frames, sharply increasing computational cost. Token reduction methods mitigate this cost by exploiting spatial redundancy, but existing approaches rely on inaccurate similarity estimates and lightweight matching algorithms, resulting in poor matching quality and only marginal acceleration.
  To overcome these limitations, we propose ORBIS, an SW-HW co-designed accelerator for video DiT. ORBIS leverages the output activation from the previous timestep to obtain more accurate inter-token similarity, substantially improving matching quality and enabling a higher token reduction ratio. We further introduce a Distribution-Aware Token Matching (DATM) algorithm that captures global token distribution and explicitly minimizes token-pair loss for additional gains. To fully hide DATM latency, we design specialized, deeply pipelined hardware and minimize its hardware cost through quantization, occupying only 2.4% of total area with negligible accuracy loss. Extensive experiments show that ORBIS achieves about 2x higher token reduction ratio than the state-of-the-art approach, AsymRnR, while delivering up to 4.5x speedup and 79.3% energy reduction compared to an NVIDIA A100 GPU.

</details>


### [19] [Diverse Yet Consistent: Context-Guided Diffusion with Energy-Based Joint Refinement for Multi-Agent Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22017)
*Lei Chu,Yuhuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的框架，通过历史轨迹的上下文信息引导和能量函数优化，实现了多智能体运动预测的多样性与交互一致性，在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型能捕捉多模态分布，但难以同时保证预测的多样性和多智能体间交互的一致性，且现有评估指标（单智能体边际指标）不足以反映联合动态。

Method: 基于扩散模型框架，通过历史轨迹的上下文信息引导（guidance mechanism）提升预测多样性与表现力；引入基于能量的公式（energy-based formulation）优化联合轨迹分布，同时保持单轨迹合理性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上持续优于现有方法；在ETH/UCY数据集上，相比强边际基线方法，显著提升了边际指标（ADE/FDE）和联合指标（JADE/JFDE）；相比现有联合预测方法，在边际指标上取得显著提升，同时保持有竞争力的联合性能。

Conclusion: 通过引导机制和能量约束，扩散模型能有效平衡多智能体运动预测中的多样性与交互一致性，联合优化策略不仅提升了联合指标，还反向改进了边际预测精度。

Abstract: Deepgenerative models havebecomeapromisingapproach for human motion prediction due to their ability to capture multimodal distributions and represent diverse human be haviors. However, generating predictions that are both di verse and jointly consistent among interacting agents re mains challenging. In addition, most existing approaches are primarily evaluated using single-agent (marginal) met rics, which fail to fully reflect the joint dynamics of multi agent interactions. We propose a diffusion-based frame work that improves multi-agent motion prediction by lever aging rich contextual information from historical trajecto ries. This information is incorporated through a guidance mechanism to enhance the diversity and expressiveness of predicted motions. To further enforce interaction consis tency, we introduce an energy-based formulation that re fines the joint trajectory distribution while preserving the plausibility of individual trajectories. Extensive experi ments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing methods. No tably, our approach substantially improves both marginal (ADE/FDE) and joint (JADE/JFDE) metrics on ETH/UCY over strong marginal baselines. Compared with prior joint prediction methods, it delivers significant gains in marginal metrics while maintaining competitive joint performance.

</details>


### [20] [FRED: A Multi-Modal Autonomous Driving Dataset for Flooded Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22018)
*Connor Malone,Sebastien Demmel,Sebastien Glaser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FRED（洪水道路环境数据集），据我们所知，这是首个专门针对道路涉水场景的多模态自动驾驶数据集，包含相机、LiDAR、IMU/GNSS数据，提供语义标签和两种数据格式，旨在支持涉水场景下的感知、定位和SLAM研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集缺乏针对道路积水/洪水场景的专门数据，而积水是自动驾驶面临的重要挑战之一。因此需要构建一个专门的多模态数据集来支持该场景下的算法研究和评估。

Method: 使用2.3 MP FLIR Blackfly相机、Ouster OS1-64 64线LiDAR、iXblue ATLANS-C IMU（经Geoflex RTK GNSS校正）在5个不同地点采集洪水期间及退水后的数据。提供KITTI格式和RTMaps格式两种数据发布格式，并提供了语义标签用于单传感器和传感器融合的水坑检测方法训练与评估。

Result: 构建了首个多模态道路涉水场景自动驾驶数据集，包含相机、LiDAR、IMU/GNSS数据，提供语义标签和位置/速度信息，同时包含干地条件下的对照数据。

Conclusion: FRED数据集填补了自动驾驶领域在涉水场景数据方面的空白，可支持水坑检测（单传感器和融合方法）、基于位置的水坑检测、定位和SLAM等多项任务的开发和评估。

Abstract: The Flooded Road Environments Dataset (FRED) is, to our knowledge, the first multi-modal autonomous driving dataset specifically targeting the collection of data from scenarios involving water hazards on the road. The dataset contains images from a 2.3 MP FLIR Blackfly USB3 camera, 64-beam 360$^\circ$ point clouds from an Ouster OS1-64 LiDAR, and data from an iXblue ATLANS-C IMU corrected by a Geoflex RTK GNSS, from five separate locations captured both during and after flooding events. The data has been released in two formats: a KITTI-style format for easy integration with existing data tools, and the RTMaps format for direct replay of the vehicle's data capture. We provide semantic labels to enable the training and evaluation of both single-sensor and sensor-fusion methods for water hazard detection. Position and velocity, as well as data captured under dry conditions, are provided to enable the development of location-based detection methods that may incorporate maps, and to evaluate other tasks such as localisation and SLAM.

</details>


### [21] [AVI-HT: Adaptive Vision-IMU Fusion for 3D Hand Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21714)
*Ziyi Kou,Ankit Kumar,Mia Huang,Taylor Niehues,Vatsal Mehta,Ergys Ristani,Li Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AVI-HT，一种自适应视觉-IMU融合方法，通过联合建模第一人称图像和手套上的6-DoF IMU信号来跟踪3D手部姿态。在DexGloveHOI数据集上，AVI-HT相比基线方法将关键点误差降低了16.1%（腕部对齐变体降低24.2%），尤其在严重视觉遮挡的手物交互场景中表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部姿态跟踪方法在严重视觉遮挡（如手物交互）场景下精度和可用性不足，单模态视觉方法受遮挡影响大，而简单多模态融合缺乏对传感器可信度的自适应调节能力。

Method: (1) 构建DexGloveHOI数据集，包含100K+同步的视觉-IMU样本及3D标注姿态；(2) 提出跨传感器深度注意力机制，自适应调节视觉和每个IMU传感器的信任权重，实现鲁棒融合。

Result: 相比基线方法，AVI-HT将平均关键点误差降低16.1%，腕部对齐变体降低24.2%。消融实验揭示了IMU传感器在不同活动类型中对手指的贡献，以及模型对IMU噪声和时间错位的敏感性。

Conclusion: AVI-HT通过自适应注意力融合视觉和IMU信号，在重遮挡HOI场景下显著提升了3D手部姿态跟踪的精度和鲁棒性，验证了跨传感器信任调制在多模态融合中的有效性。

Abstract: We present AVI-HT, an adaptive visual-IMU fusion approach for tracking 3D hand poses by jointly modeling the egocentric image with on-glove 6-DoF IMU signals. AVI-HT achieves significantly improved accuracy and availability, particularly in hand-object interaction (HOI) scenarios involving heavy visual occlusion. Two complementary ingredients underpin its success: (1) synchronized multi-modal training data pairing on-body vision-IMU sensor streams with ground-truth 3D hand poses from a motion-capture system, and (2) a cross-sensor deep attention mechanism that adaptively modulates the trust assigned to the vision and individual IMU sensors. To evaluate AVI-HT in real-world settings, we conduct extensive experiments on our DexGloveHOI dataset that consists of 100K+ pairwise vision-IMU samples with synchronized 3D annotated poses, in which users manipulate a variety of objects during daily tasks. We compare against multiple single- and multi-modal tracking approaches under two hand models (UmeTrack, MANO). The results show that AVI-HT reduces mean keypoint error by 16.1% and its wrist-aligned variant by 24.2% over the baselines. Ablation studies further reveal the per-finger contribution of IMU sensors across activity types, and the model's sensitivity to IMU noise and temporal misalignment in vision-IMU fusion.

</details>


### [22] [SO-Mamba: State-Ownership Mamba for Unrolled MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22031)
*Pengcheng Fang,Hongli Chen,Fangfang Tang,Feng Liu,Xiaohao Cai,Shanshan Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SO-Mamba，一种状态所有权Mamba正则化器，通过区分驻留载体证据与非驻留更新证据，利用状态所有权路由器（SOR）实现循环内容传递与状态接口调制，并引入两级外带泄漏诊断机制，在五个MRI重建基准上超越CNN、Transformer和Mamba基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据一致性耦合的展开求解器中，不同重建迭代阶段的驻留载体证据应与阶段依赖的非驻留证据区分对待，而现有Mamba统一处理这两类证据，导致循环内容路径混乱。

Method: 提出SO-Mamba正则化器，包含状态所有权路由器（SOR），将证据分为三类：循环驻留载体、状态接口访问和非状态输出校正。通过SOR构建驻留载体用于循环内容，非驻留证据通过仿射调制B/C状态接口和输出校正出口介入，不进入循环路径。另设计两级外带泄漏诊断分离隐藏状态存储与读出表达。

Result: 在涵盖不同解剖结构、采样模式和线圈配置的五个公开MRI重建基准上，SO-Mamba一致优于CNN、Transformer和Mamba基线，且计算效率具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 区分驻留与非驻留证据的显式所有权分配能有效提升Mamba在展开式MRI重建中的正则化效果，外带泄漏诊断机制为理解状态空间模型的行为提供了新工具。

Abstract: Accelerated MRI reconstruction requires recovering missing details while preserving anatomically coherent structures across large spatial regions. State-space models such as Mamba provide efficient long-range modeling, making them attractive learned regularizers for unrolled reconstruction. However, in a data-consistency-coupled unrolled solver, different stages operate on different reconstruction iterates, where the resident carrier should preserve coherent reconstruction content across stages while stage-dependent non-resident evidence is tied to the current update. Treating these roles uniformly can place persistent resident-carrier evidence and update-dependent non-resident evidence into the same recurrent content route. We therefore propose SO-Mamba, a state-ownership Mamba regularizer that assigns reconstruction evidence within each Mamba stage to recurrent residency, state-interface access, and non-state output correction. SO-Mamba implements this ownership rule with a State-Ownership Router (SOR), which constructs a resident carrier for recurrent content and routes non-resident evidence to affine modulation of the B/C state interfaces and an output correction outlet. The resident carrier supplies the Mamba content route, while the non-resident evidence stream adapts the state interfaces and contributes through the output outlet without entering the recurrent content route. We further introduce a two-level outer-band leakage diagnostic that separates hidden-state storage from readout expression by measuring outer-band energy in the selective-scan state trajectory and the post-scan Mamba readout. Experiments on five public MRI reconstruction benchmarks spanning diverse anatomies, sampling patterns, and coil configurations show that SO-Mamba consistently improves over CNN-, Transformer-, and Mamba-based baselines with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [23] [Improving 3D Labeling in Self-Driving by Inferring Vehicle Information using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21747)
*Steven Chen,Shivesh Khaitan,Nemanja Djuric*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用视觉语言模型（VLM）从图像裁剪中推断车辆品牌、型号和代际，并输出精确3D边界框尺寸的方法，用于提升自动驾驶中3D车辆标注效率和质量。实验表明，该方法在减少人工标注时间的同时提高了标注质量，尤其在车辆严重遮挡场景下优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中3D车辆标注通常依赖LiDAR辅助的人工标注，但该方法在车辆严重遮挡等情况下存在标注不准确的问题，且人工标注耗时耗力。作者希望通过引入VLM的零样本推理能力，提升标注效率和准确性。

Method: 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）对车辆图像裁剪进行推理，同时完成两项任务：(1) 识别车辆的品牌、型号和代际（VMMR）；(2) 输出精确的3D边界框尺寸作为人工标注的初始种子。通过迭代提示工程优化VLM输出，并比较不同VLM的效果。

Result: 与强基线方法相比，该方法不仅实现了高精度，还能在特定失败模式（如车辆严重遮挡）下提供比初始LiDAR辅助人工标注更准确的维度。在公开和私有数据集上的实验表明结论具有良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 将VLM整合到标注流程中可以减少人工标注时间，同时提升标注质量。

Abstract: We present an approach to improve 3D vehicle labeling in self-driving applications through zero-shot inference of vehicle information, leveraging Vehicle Make and Model Recognition (VMMR) methods. The proposed approach utilizes a Vision Language Model (VLM) to both infer a vehicle's make, model, and generation from image crops, and output accurate 3D bounding box dimensions to seed manual labeling. We evaluate the impact of iterative prompt engineering and the choice of different VLMs on both vehicle bounding box inference and make/model/generation recognition. When compared to strong baselines, the proposed approach not only shows high accuracy, but also excels in mitigating specific failure modes where VLMs provide better dimensions than initial lidar-aided human annotated labels (e.g., in cases of significant vehicle occlusion). Experiments on both public and proprietary data strongly suggest that our conclusions are generalizable across different labelers and datasets. The results demonstrate that integrating VLMs into the labeling process can reduce manual labeling time while increasing label quality.

</details>


### [24] [AgroVG: A Large-Scale Multi-Source Benchmark for Agricultural Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22034)
*Haocheng Li,Juepeng Zheng,Zenghao Yang,Kaiqi Du,Guilong Xiao,Gengmeng Pu,Haohuan Fu,Jianxi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgroVG，一个面向农业视觉定位的多源基准数据集，包含10,071个图像-查询对，覆盖6类农业目标（作物/杂草、果实、麦穗、害虫、植物病害、树冠），支持边界框和实例掩码两种定位任务，并涵盖单目标、多目标和目标缺失三种查询类型。对26种模型配置的零样本评估显示，最佳多目标Set-F₁仅0.35，最佳正查询掩码成功率(IoU@0.75)低于0.17，表明当前模型在农业视觉定位上仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业视觉定位任务中目标通常具有小、重复、遮挡、形状不规则等特点，且指令可能指向一个、多个或零个目标，现有基准缺乏对此类场景的系统评估。需要一种能同时测试定位精度、目标集完整性和存在感知能力的基准。

Method: 将农业视觉定位形式化为广义集合预测任务，构建包含10,071个图像-查询对的AgroVG基准，跨10个源数据集、6类农业目标，支持边界框定位（T1，全部6类）和实例掩码定位（T2，具有可靠像素级标注的源），查询涵盖单目标、多目标和目标缺失三种情况，并提供框集匹配和查询级掩码覆盖等任务特定评估协议。

Result: 对26种模型配置（闭源MLLM、开源VLM、专用定位系统）进行零样本评估，最佳多目标Set-F₁仅0.35，最佳正查询掩码成功率(IoU@0.75)低于0.17。

Conclusion: 当前模型在农业视觉定位任务上表现远不能满足实际需求，尤其在多目标定位和高IoU阈值下的掩码定位方面存在显著差距，AgroVG基准为推进该领域研究提供了重要评测平台。

Abstract: Visual grounding, the task of localizing objects described by natural-language expressions, is a foundational capability for agricultural AI systems, enabling applications such as selective weeding, disease monitoring, and targeted harvesting. Reliable evaluation of agricultural visual grounding remains challenging because agricultural targets are often small, repetitive, occluded, or irregularly shaped, and instructions may refer to one, many, or no objects in an image. Evaluating this capability therefore requires jointly testing localization accuracy, target-set completeness, and existence-aware abstention. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{AgroVG}, a multi-source benchmark that formulates agricultural grounding as generalized set prediction: given an image and a referring expression, a model must return all matching target instances or abstain when no target is present. AgroVG contains 10{,}071 annotation-grounded image-query pairs from ten source datasets across six target families: crop/weed, fruit, wheat head, pest, plant disease, and tree canopy. It supports bounding-box grounding (T1) across all six families and instance-mask grounding (T2) on sources with reliable instance-level pixel annotations, with queries covering single-target, multi-target, and target-absent regimes. AgroVG further provides task-specific protocols for box-set matching and query-level mask coverage. Zero-shot evaluation of 26 model configurations spanning closed-source MLLMs, open-source VLMs, and specialized grounding systems reveals persistent gaps: the best multi-target Set-$F_1$ reaches only 0.35, and the best positive-query mask success rate at IoU@0.75 remains below 0.17. Data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AgroVG-5172/ .

</details>


### [25] [SceneGraphGrounder: Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding via Structured Scene Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21788)
*Xuefei Sun,Xujia Zhang,Brendan Crowe,Doncey Albin,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneGraphGrounder将3D视觉定位重构为结构化图匹配问题，通过从2D视图中提取对象关系构建持久化3D场景图，实现零样本条件下基于自然语言查询的物体定位，在ScanRefer基准和真实机器人部署中取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的零样本3D视觉定位方法依赖视角相关的推理或隐式表示，在处理组合式查询时空间一致性和可解释性不足。

Method: 提出视觉标记提示策略，让VLM从2D视图中推断对象-对象关系，将其提升为包含空间和语义关系的持久化3D场景图；针对查询构建查询图，与场景图进行约束对齐以实现多视角一致和可解释推理。

Result: 在ScanRefer基准上取得了零样本方法中具有竞争力的性能（仅使用RGB-D输入），并在真实移动机器人上验证了长时域物理环境中的鲁棒空间推理能力。

Conclusion: 图匹配范式有效提升了零样本3D视觉定位的空间一致性和可解释性，适用于真实机器人部署。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D visual grounding requires localizing objects in unstructured environments from free-form natural language. Recent vision-language model (VLM) approaches achieve promising results but rely on view-dependent reasoning or implicit representations, limiting spatial consistency and interpretability for compositional queries. We propose SceneGraphGrounder, a framework that reformulates 3D grounding as structured graph matching over a reconstructed 3D scene graph. To enable this formulation, we introduce a visual marker prompting strategy that enables a VLM to infer object-object relationships from 2D views, which are subsequently lifted into a persistent 3D scene graph encoding both spatial and semantic relations. Given a query, we construct a query graph and perform constrained alignment with the scene graph, ensuring multi-view consistency and interpretable reasoning. Experiments on the ScanRefer benchmark demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance among zero-shot approaches, using only RGB-D inputs. We further validate our framework through real-world deployment on a mobile robot, demonstrating robust spatial reasoning in long-horizon physical environments. We will make our code publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [26] [GA-VLN: Geometry-Aware BEV Representation for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22036)
*Jiahao Yang,Zihan Wang,Xiangyang Li,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GA-BEV，一种将显式深度投影与隐式3D先验融合到BEV空间中的紧凑几何特征表示，用于提升VLN任务中MLLM的导航效率与性能，无需DAgger或混合VQA训练即达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法依赖密集RGB视频，产生过多patch token且缺乏显式空间结构，导致计算开销大、空间推理能力有限。

Method: 从RGB-D输入构建BEV空间地图：将视觉特征投影到3D空间并聚合为以智能体为中心的布局，保持几何一致性并减少token冗余；同时融入预训练3D基础模型的特征，注入来自大规模3D重建任务的结构先验，形成显式深度投影与隐式学习先验的互补。

Result: 仅使用导航数据（无DAgger增强或混合VQA训练）即达到SOTA结果，展现了鲁棒性和数据效率。

Conclusion: GA-BEV通过紧凑的3D几何特征表示有效解决了VLN中的token冗余与空间推理不足问题，验证了显式+隐式几何线索融合在MLLM导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Despite significant progress in Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), existing approaches still rely on dense RGB videos that produce excessive patch tokens and lack explicit spatial structure, resulting in substantial computational overhead and limited spatial reasoning. To address these issues, we introduce the Geometry-Aware BEV (GA-BEV) - a compact, 3D-grounded feature representation that integrates both explicit and implicit geometric cues into multimodal large language model (MLLM) - based navigation systems. We construct BEV spatial maps from RGB-D inputs by projecting visual features into 3D space and aggregating them into an agent-centric layout that preserves geometric consistency while reducing token redundancy. To further enrich geometric understanding, we incorporate features from a pretrained 3D foundation model into the BEV space, injecting structural priors learned from large-scale 3D reconstruction tasks. Together, these complementary cues - explicit depth-based projection and implicit learned priors - yield compact yet spatially expressive representations that substantially improve navigation efficiency and performance. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art results using only navigation data, without DAgger augmentation or mixed VQA training, demonstrating the robustness and data efficiency of the proposed GA-VLN framework.

</details>


### [27] [Diffusion-guided Generalizable Enhancer for Urban Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22420)
*Henry Che,Jingkang Wang,Yun Chen,Ze Yang,Sivabalan Manivasagam,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenRe 是一种基于扩散引导的通用增强器，能够快速修复预训练3D高斯表示在新视角下的缺陷，实现高效、鲁棒的城市场景重建，并泛化到未见过的视角（如车道变换），在质量和效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染方法在记录轨迹上表现良好，但在大视角偏移下质量严重下降；基于扩散模型的方法虽能改善质量，但需要昂贵的逐场景优化，且蒸馏后的表示脆弱，无法泛化到有限合成视角之外的场景。

Method: GenRe 提出一种扩散引导的通用增强器，输入任意预训练的3D高斯表示，通过跨场景学习蒸馏生成先验，在几分钟内修复新视角下的缺陷，产生鲁棒且高保真的表示。

Result: GenRe 在质量和效率上均优于现有方法，能够可靠地泛化到具有挑战性的未见视角（如车道变换），并有益于多种下游任务。

Conclusion: GenRe 实现了鲁棒且可扩展的自动驾驶传感器仿真，解决了现有方法在泛化性和效率上的关键局限。

Abstract: Urban scene reconstruction from real-world observations has emerged as a powerful tool for self-driving development and testing. While current neural rendering approaches achieve high-fidelity rendering along the recorded trajectories, their quality degrades significantly under large viewpoint shifts, limiting the applicability for closed-loop simulation. Recent works have shown promising results in using diffusion models to enhance quality at these challenging viewpoints and distill improvements back into 3D representations. However, they often require costly per-scene optimization, and the distilled representations remain fragile and fail to generalize beyond limited synthesized views. To address these limitations, we propose GenRe, a novel diffusion-guided generalizable enhancer for urban scene reconstruction. GenRe takes as input any pretrained 3D Gaussian representation and fixes the deficiencies within a few minutes. By learning to distill generative priors across diverse scenes, GenRe produces robust and high-fidelity representation efficiently that generalizes reliably to challenging unseen viewpoints (e.g., lane change). Experiments show that GenRe outperforms existing methods in both quality and efficiency and benefits various downstream tasks, enabling robust and scalable sensor simulation for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [28] [Broken Memories: Detecting and Mitigating Memorization in Diffusion Models with Degraded Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22050)
*Yuanmin Huang,Mi Zhang,Chen Chen,Feifei Li,Geng Hong,Xiaoyu You,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次发现扩散模型中的记忆化现象会导致内部数值不稳定并产生视觉"破碎"伪影，据此提出基于潜更新范数的经验稳定区域来量化稳定行为，并开发了逐步检测与自适应缓解的框架，无需修改提示或引导即可抑制记忆化，在Stable Diffusion 1.4上实现了AUC>0.999的检测性能和0.0%的记忆化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成高质量图像时存在记忆训练数据的隐私和版权风险，但此前缺乏有效的检测与缓解方法。

Method: 通过稳定性分析，定义基于潜更新范数的经验稳定区域；提出在线逐步骤检测与自适应缓解框架，在不改变提示或引导条件下抑制记忆化。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion 1.4上，检测AUC>0.999，缓解后记忆化率为0.0%，额外开销约0.01秒每张图像。

Conclusion: 所提方法能高效检测并缓解扩散模型的记忆化问题，同时保持语义保真度和图像质量。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel at generating high-quality images, their tendency to memorize training data poses significant privacy and copyright risks. In this work, we for the first time identify that memorization induces internal numerical instability, often manifesting as visually ``broken'' artifacts. Inspired by stability analysis in numerical methods, we introduce empirical stability regions based on latent update norms to quantitatively characterize stable behavior during generation. Leveraging this, we propose a principled, on-the-fly framework for step-wise detection and adaptive mitigation. Our approach suppresses memorization without altering prompts or guidance, thereby preserving semantic fidelity and image quality. Extensive experiments on Stable Diffusion 1.4 demonstrate that our method achieves an AUC $>0.999$ detection performance and a $0.0\%$ memorization rate after mitigation with negligible overhead ($\approx0.01$s per image).

</details>


### [29] [EasyVFX: Frequency-Driven Decoupling for Resource-Efficient VFX Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22051)
*Yue Ma,Xu Ye,Qinghe Wang,Yucheng Wang,Hongyu Liu,Yinhan Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Yuanpeng Che,Shanhui Mo,Paul Liang,Fangneng Zhan,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EasyVFX提出一种资源高效的视觉特效合成框架，通过频域分解将高维学习问题解耦为可管理的子任务，采用频率感知的混合专家架构（Freq-MoE）和测试时训练策略，在极低计算资源下实现了高质量VFX合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉特效合成需要大量数据集和 prohibitive 计算资源，因为空间纹理与时间动态之间存在复杂的耦合。作者希望降低VFX合成的资源门槛，实现VFX的民主化。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：1) 频率感知混合专家架构（Freq-MoE），通过软路由机制将不同频谱带分配给专门专家，分别学习外观先验和运动动态先验；2) 测试时训练策略，结合新型频率约束损失，在单GPU上约100步即可快速适应未见过的特效。

Result: EasyVFX能生成结构一致且视觉惊艳的特效，在极少GPU资源下达到专业级VFX质量。

Conclusion: 频率感知学习是降低VFX合成资源门槛的关键催化剂，证明了通过频域分解可以显著降低优化难度和数据依赖，推动专业级VFX的民主化。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity visual effects (VFX) typically demands massive datasets and prohibitive computational power due to the intricate coupling of spatial textures and temporal dynamics. In this paper, we introduce EasyVFX, a resource-efficient framework that achieves realistic VFX synthesis under stringent constraints. Our core philosophy lies in frequency-domain decomposition: we observe that the complexity of VFX can be significantly mitigated by decoupling high-frequency components, which represent intricate spatial appearances, from low-frequency components that encapsulate global motion dynamics. This spectral disentanglement transforms a high-dimensional learning problem into manageable sub-tasks, thereby lowering the optimization barrier and reducing data dependency. Building upon this insight, we propose a two-stage training paradigm. First, we design a Frequency-aware Mixture-of-Experts (Freq-MoE) architecture. By utilizing a soft routing mechanism, our model assigns specialized experts to distinct spectral bands, enabling them to cultivate robust priors for appearance and motion dynamics. This specialization allows the model to acquire foundational VFX knowledge with fewer GPU resources. Second, we introduce a Test-Time Training strategy powered by a novel Frequency-constraint Loss. This allows the pre-trained model to swiftly adapt to specific, unseen effects through localized optimizations, requiring only about 100 steps on a single GPU. Experimental results demonstrate that EasyVFX produces structurally consistent and visually stunning effects, proving that frequency-aware learning is a key catalyst for democratizing professional-grade VFX.

</details>


### [30] [Distributed Image Compression with Multimodal Side Information at Extremely Low Bitrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22061)
*Guojun Xu,Mingyang Zhang,Jianwen Xiang,Cheng Tan,Yanchao Yang,Junwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态分布式图像压缩框架（MDIC），首次将侧信息以多模态方式引入DIC范式，通过文本到图像的扩散解码器和特征掩码生成器，在极低码率（<0.1 bpp）下实现全局语义一致与局部细节保留，在KITTI Stereo和Cityscapes数据集上达到最先进感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式图像压缩方法在利用侧信息的全局上下文和物体级细节方面存在不足，导致重建图像出现局部模糊和精细细节损失，尤其是在极低码率场景下。

Method: 1）提出文本到图像扩散解码器，利用从相关图像提取的文本侧信息捕获共享全局语义；2）设计特征掩码生成器，通过多模态细粒度对齐任务监督，用于指导从无损传输侧信息中提取细节并调节VQ-VAE量化嵌入的聚类特征表示。

Result: 在KITTI Stereo和Cityscapes数据集上的大量实验表明，MDIC在极低码率下实现了最先进的感知质量。

Conclusion: 多模态方式利用侧信息能有效提升极低码率下分布式图像压缩的重建质量，兼顾全局感知质量与局部细节保留。

Abstract: Distributed Image Compression (DIC) is crucial for multi-view transmission, especially when operating at extremely low bitrates (< 0.1 bpp). Its core challenge is effectively utilizing side information to achieve high-quality reconstruction under strict bitrate budgets. However, existing DIC approaches struggle to exploit global context and object-level details from side information, leading to local blurring and the loss of fine details in the reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose a Multimodal DIC framework (MDIC), which, for the first time, leverages side information in a multimodal manner into the DIC paradigm, effectively preserving fine-grained local details and enhancing global perceptual quality in reconstructed images. Specifically, we introduce a text-to-image diffusion-based decoder conditioned on textual side information extracted from correlated images to capture shared global semantics. Moreover, we design a feature-mask generator, supervised by a multimodal fine-grained alignment task, to strengthen the exploitation of visual side information. The generated mask serves two purposes: first, it guides the extraction of fine-grained details from losslessly transmitted side information to preserve the semantic consistency of reconstructed details; second, it regulates the extraction of clustered feature representations from the quantized VQ-VAE embeddings, compensating for category information lost under the extreme compression of the primary image. Extensive experiments on the widely used KITTI Stereo and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that MDIC achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality at extremely low bitrates.

</details>


### [31] [COCOTree: A Dataset and Benchmark for Open Tree-Structured Visual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22068)
*Junhyub Lee,Seunghun Chae,Hyosu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化并实现开放树分解任务，利用LVLMs和SAM自动生成层次化视觉树结构，构建COCOTree基准数据集（2.1万图像，180万节点），并提出OTQ评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分割方法缺乏对层次化树状结构的支持，且手动标注层次化视觉组件的认知和物理成本极高，无法实现细粒度、灵活的视觉结构分解。

Method: 提出全自动生成流水线，结合LVLMs的语义推理能力和SAM的精确几何分割能力；构建COCOTree数据集；提出OTQ指标联合评估掩码精度、标签准确性和结构一致性。

Result: 构建了包含21K+图像和1.8M结构节点的COCOTree基准，覆盖3.5K+唯一标签的长尾分布；人工评估验证生成的标注与人类结构判断高度一致。

Conclusion: 开放树分解任务可行且有效，自动化流水线可替代人工标注，为层次化视觉理解提供了新范式和大规模基准。

Abstract: We formalize and enable the task of open tree decomposition, which segments an image into hierarchical trees of visual components with unconstrained granularity and flexibility. Specifically, we provide the foundation benchmark for this new paradigm with the following three key contributions. First, we overcome the prohibitively high cognitive and physical bottlenecks of manual annotation by developing a fully automated generation pipeline that synergizes the semantic reasoning of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) with the precise geometric grounding of SAM 3. Second, leveraging this pipeline, we construct COCOTree, a massive-scale benchmark featuring over 21K images and 1.8M structural nodes. By embracing an open-vocabulary space of over 3.5K unique labels, it successfully captures the long-tail distribution of complex physical assemblies. Notably, rigorous human evaluation confirms our generated annotations demonstrate strong alignment with human structural judgment. Third, we establish a standardized evaluation protocol by proposing the Open Tree Quality (OTQ) metric, which jointly assesses mask precision, label accuracy, and structural consistency. We release our dataset and benchmark code at https://github.com/melonkick3090/COCOTree.

</details>


### [32] [TWINGS: Thin Plate Splines Warp-aligned Initialization for Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22069)
*Hyeseong Kim,Geonhui Son,Deukhee Lee,Dosik Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TWINGS通过薄板样条（TPS）变形模型对齐反投影点与三角化控制点，为3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）提供快速且几何精确的初始点云，从而在稀疏视图输入下显著提升新视角合成的结构细节和色彩保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图输入下，高质量场景重建面临点云稀疏性的重大挑战，现有方法难以在有限视角下保持结构细节和色彩保真度。

Method: 利用薄板样条（TPS）作为平滑非刚性变形模型，通过最小化弯曲能量从控制点对应关系估计全局一致变形，将估计深度反投影的点与三角化3D控制点对齐，生成校准后的反投影点；在控制点附近采样这些校准点，为3DGS提供快速且几何准确的初始化。

Result: 在DTU、LLFF和Mip-NeRF360数据集上，TWINGS在稀疏视图场景下持续优于现有方法，重建结果具有更精细的结构细节和更准确的色彩。

Conclusion: TWINGS有效解决了3DGS在稀疏视图下的点稀疏性问题，通过TPS引导的几何对齐初始化显著提升了新视角合成质量。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse-view inputs poses a significant challenge in 3D computer vision, particularly for achieving high-quality scene reconstructions with limited viewpoints. We introduce TWINGS, a framework that enhances 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by directly addressing point sparsity. We employ Thin Plate Splines (TPS), a smooth non-rigid deformation model that minimizes bending energy to estimate a globally coherent warp from control-point correspondences, to align backprojected points from estimated depth with triangulated 3D control points, yielding calibrated backprojected points. By sampling these calibrated points near the control points, TWINGS provides a fast and geometrically accurate initialization for 3DGS, ultimately improving structural detail preservation and color fidelity in reconstructed scenes. Extensive experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF360 demonstrate that TWINGS consistently outperforms existing methods, delivering detailed and accurate reconstructions under sparse-view scenarios.

</details>


### [33] [JMed48k: A Multi-Profession Japanese Medical Licensing Benchmark for Vision-Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22080)
*Yue Xun,Junyu Liu,Qian Niu,Xinyi Wang,Zheng Yuan,Zirui Li,Zequn Zhang,Bowen Zhao,Shujun Wang,Irene Li,Kan Hatakeyama-Sato,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JMed48k，一个多职业日本医疗执照基准，包含48,862道考试题和20,142张图像，用于评估视觉语言模型。通过构建JMed48k-Eval子集（12,484道题）和配对图像移除审计，评估了21个模型，发现专有和开源模型从图像中获益显著，而医疗专用模型对视觉证据利用有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗视觉语言模型基准多集中于单一职业（如医生），缺乏跨职业、多模态的日本医疗执照评估基准，无法系统评估模型在不同医疗专业中的视觉推理能力。

Method: 1. 从日本厚生劳动省官方PDF中构建JMed48k语料库（48,862题，20,142张图像，8类视觉标注）；2. 提取近五年子集JMed48k-Eval（12,484道评分题）；3. 设计配对图像移除审计方法，比较有无图像时的答案变化，定义四种答案转移状态。

Result: 评估21个模型（专有、开源、医疗专用），专有和开源模型从图像中获益显著，医疗专用系统对视觉证据利用有限。图像移除效应在不同职业间差异达7倍（医师+5.7分 vs 公共卫生护士+39.8分）。

Conclusion: JMed48k支持可重复、按职业分层的视觉语言模型评估，揭示了当前模型在医疗执照场景中利用视觉信息的能力差异，尤其是医疗专用模型对视觉证据的利用不足。

Abstract: We introduce JMed48k, a multi-profession Japanese healthcare licensing benchmark for evaluating vision-language models. Built from official PDF materials released by the Japanese Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare, JMed48k contains 48,862 exam questions and 20,142 images from 11 national licensing examinations between 2005 and 2025, with visual content annotated under an 8-type taxonomy. From this corpus, we derive JMed48k-Eval, a recent five-year evaluation subset with 12,484 scored questions, including 9,905 text-only questions and 2,579 questions with images. We evaluate 21 proprietary, open-source, and medical-specific models, reporting text-only and with-image performance separately. Because these subsets contain different questions, we further introduce a paired image-removal audit that evaluates questions with images before and after removing visual content to explore four answer-transition states. The audit shows that proprietary and open source models gain substantially from images, whereas medical-specific systems show limited observable use of visual evidence, with many correct answers persisting after image removal. Even among proprietary models, the net image-removal effect varies sevenfold across professions, from +5.7 points on Physician questions to +39.8 points on Public Health Nurse questions. We release JMed48k to support reproducible, profession-stratified evaluation of vision-language models in medical licensing settings.

</details>


### [34] [GenHAR: Generalizing Cross-domain Human Activity Recognition for Last-mile Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22086)
*Zhiqing Hong,Zelong Li,Xiubin Fan,Guang Yang,Baoshen Guo,Haotian Wang,Tian He,Desheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenHAR是一个旨在解决传感器数据域迁移问题的框架，通过学习域不变的传感器表征，在仅使用源域数据的情况下提升目标域上的泛化能力。该方法通过对传感器数据进行token化并学习频域通道间相关性、引入选择性掩码和高效注意力机制，在真实数据集上相比SOTA方法准确率提升9.97%，计算量减少6.4倍，并在物流场景中检测了21.5亿次实时活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAR方法面临不同传感器数据域之间的分布偏移问题，导致在实际部署中性能显著下降。

Method: 1) 对传感器数据进行token化，学习频域传感器通道维度间的相关性；2) 引入选择性掩码和高效注意力机制提升计算效率。

Result: 相比SOTA方法准确率提升9.97%，浮点运算次数减少6.4倍；在4个城市部署，检测了21.5亿次实时活动。

Conclusion: GenHAR通过域不变表征学习和高效架构设计，有效解决了传感器数据的域迁移问题，在实际部署中表现出显著的性能优势和计算效率。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) has shown remarkable effectiveness in various applications, such as smart healthcare and intelligent manufacturing. However, a major challenge faced by HAR is the distribution shift across different sensor data domains, which often leads to decreased performance when deployed for real-world applications. To address this issue, this paper introduces GenHAR, a novel framework designed to mitigate the domain gap by learning domain-invariant sensor representations. GenHAR aims to enhance the generalization capabilities of HAR on target domains purely with data from the source domain. The key novelty of GenHAR lies in two aspects. Firstly, GenHAR tokenizes sensor data and learns correlations among frequency sensor channel dimensions to improve the robustness of HAR models. Secondly, GenHAR improves the efficiency via selective masking and an efficient attention mechanism. We conduct a systematic analysis of GenHAR by comparing it with state-of-the-art HAR methods on real-world human activity datasets. Results show that GenHAR outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 9.97% in accuracy, and reduces Floating Point Operations by 6.4 times. Moreover, we deploy GenHAR at a leading logistics company in 4 cities, and have detected 2.15 billion real-time activities. We release our code at: https://github.com/Sensor-FoundationModel/GenHAR.

</details>


### [35] [LVDrive: Latent Visual Representation Enhanced Vision-Language-Action Autonomous Driving Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22089)
*Xiaodong Mei,Diankun Zhang,Hongwei Xie,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LVDrive提出了一种基于潜空间视觉表征增强的VLA框架，通过在未来潜空间中进行场景预测和联合运动建模，替代了传统的稀疏动作监督和像素级图像重建，在闭环驾驶任务中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖稀疏的动作监督，未能充分利用其强大的场景理解与推理能力；而引入稠密视觉监督的世界模型方法又过度关注像素级图像重建，忽略了语义层面的场景表征学习。

Method: (1) 在VLA范式中引入未来场景预测任务，在高层级潜空间中进行未来表征学习，并由预训练视觉骨干提供辅助监督；(2) 摒弃低效的自回归生成方式，将未来场景预测与运动预测联合建模在统一嵌入空间中，通过单次前向传播实现未来感知推理；(3) 设计两阶段轨迹解码策略，显式利用学到的潜空间未来表征来优化轨迹生成。

Result: 在Bench2Drive基准测试上的大量实验表明，LVDrive在闭环驾驶性能上取得了显著提升，优于基于动作监督的方法和基于图像重建的世界模型方法。

Conclusion: 潜空间中的未来场景表征学习能够有效增强VLA模型的驾驶决策能力，既避免了像素级重建的低效，又弥补了稀疏动作监督的信息不足，为端到端自动驾驶提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising framework for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing VLAs typically rely on sparse action supervision, which underutilizes their powerful scene understanding and reasoning capabilities. Recent attempts to incorporate dense visual supervision via world modeling often overemphasize pixel-level image reconstruction, neglecting semantically meaningful scene representation learning. In this work, we propose LVDrive, a Latent Visual representation enhanced VLA framework for autonomous driving. LVDrive introduces a future scene prediction task into the VLA paradigm, where future representations are learned entirely in a high-level latent space under auxiliary supervision from a pretrained vision backbone. Departing from inefficient autoregressive generation, we jointly model future scene and motion prediction within a unified embedding space, processed in a single forward pass to conduct the future-aware reasoning. We further design a two-stage trajectory decoding strategy that explicitly leverages the learned latent future representations to refine trajectory generation. Extensive experiments on the challenging Bench2Drive benchmark demonstrate that LVDrive achieves significant improvements in closed-loop driving performance, outperforming both action supervised methods and image-reconstruction-based world model approaches.

</details>
