Abstract: Recent work has identified a counterintuitive phenomenon termed "Hyperfitting", where fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) to near-zero training loss on small datasets surprisingly enhances open-ended generation quality and mitigates repetition in greedy decoding. While effective, the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood, with the extremely low-entropy output distributions suggesting a potential equivalence to simple temperature scaling. In this work, we demonstrate that this phenomenon is fundamentally distinct from distribution sharpening; entropy-matched control experiments reveal that temperature scaling fails to replicate the diversity gains of hyperfitting. Furthermore, we falsify the hypothesis of static vocabulary reweighting, showing through ablation studies that hyperfitting relies on a dynamic, context-dependent rank reordering mechanism. Layer-wise analysis localizes this effect to a "Terminal Expansion" in the final transformer block, where a substantial geometric expansion of the feature space (Delta Dim approx +80.8) facilitates the promotion of deep-tail tokens. Additionally, we introduce Late-Stage LoRA, a targeted fine-tuning strategy that updates only the final 5 layers, yielding robust generation with minimal parameter updates

</details>


### [147] [Agentic CLEAR: Automating Multi-Level Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22608)
*Asaf Yehudai,Lilach Eden,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic CLEAR 是一个自动、动态且易用的评估框架，能在系统、轨迹和节点三个粒度上对智能体行为生成文本洞察，实验证明其与人工标注错误高度一致并能预测任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具要么局限于可观测性和基础评估能力，要么依赖静态、手工构建的错误分类体系，无法适应新领域，难以对越来越自主的智能体系统进行有效的监督和评估。

Method: 提出 Agentic CLEAR 框架，运行在可观测层之上，支持无缝集成，通过直观的 UI 在系统级、轨迹级和节点级三个粒度生成文本洞察，实现自动化和动态评估。

Result: 在四个基准、七种智能体设置和数万次 LLM 调用上的实验表明，Agentic CLEAR 能产生高质量、数据驱动、富有洞察力的反馈，与人工标注错误高度对齐，并能预测任务成功率。

Conclusion: Agentic CLEAR 填补了智能体评估工具的空白，提供了自动、动态、多粒度的行为洞察，是一种有效的智能体行为评估与监督方案。

Abstract: Agentic systems are becoming more capable: agents define strategies, take actions, and interact with different environments. This autonomy poses serious challenges for overseeing and assessing agent behavior. Most current tools are limited, focusing on observability with basic evaluation capabilities or imposing static, hand-crafted error taxonomies that cannot adapt to new domains. To address this gap, we present Agentic CLEAR, an automatic, dynamic, and easy-to-use evaluation framework. It produces textual insights into the agent behavior on three levels of granularity: system, trace, and node. Agentic CLEAR operates above the observability layer, enabling seamless integration and featuring an intuitive UI that makes agent evaluation highly accessible. In our experiments on four benchmarks, seven agentic settings, and tens of thousands of LLM calls, we show that Agentic CLEAR produces high-quality, data-driven, insightful feedback. Our analysis shows strong alignment with human-annotated errors and the ability to predict task success rate.

</details>


### [148] [Chinese sensorimotor and embodiment norms for 3,000 lexicalized concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22616)
*Jing Chen,Gábor Parti,Yin Zhong,Chu-Ren Huang,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含3,000个汉语词汇的11维度感知运动特征和单维度具身化评分的规范数据库（来自378名母语者），验证了感知运动信息对词汇加工的促进作用，并发现简单回归模型可从纯语言表征中部分恢复感知运动评分（平均Spearman r=.62），分布语言使用编码了具身概念结构的某些方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念知识如何植根于身体经验、机器系统能否在没有直接感知运动经验的情况下获得此类知识，是认知科学和具身人工智能的核心问题。大规模规范资源对此类实证研究至关重要，但非印欧语言的相关资源仍然匮乏。

Method: 收集378名普通话母语者对3,000个词汇的11维度感知运动评分和单维度具身化评分；通过感知运动具身强度（PSE）等指标进行词汇决策任务验证；使用简单回归模型从语言表征中恢复感知运动评分，并进行表征相似性分析。

Result: 评分具有高信度和良好交叉效度；PSE-Sensorimotor和Minkowski-3是词汇决策表现的最强预测因子；感知运动评分可从纯语言表征中部分恢复（平均Spearman r=.62），视觉和听觉维度恢复效果优于化学感觉维度；感知运动空间的几何关系也可部分恢复（r=.540）。

Conclusion: 分布语言使用中编码了具身概念结构的部分信息，表明在没有直接感知运动经验的情况下，机器系统仍可从语言分布中获取一定程度的具身知识。

Abstract: Understanding how conceptual knowledge is grounded in bodily experience, and to what extent machine systems can acquire such knowledge without direct sensorimotor experience, are central questions in both cognitive science and embodied artificial intelligence research. Large-scale normative resources are essential for investigating these questions empirically, yet such resources remain sparse for non-Indo-European languages. We present a novel normative database for 3,000 lexicalized concepts in Mandarin Chinese, comprising 11-dimensional sensorimotor ratings and unidimensional embodiment ratings collected from 378 native Mandarin speakers. The ratings demonstrate high reliability and strong cross-norm validity with existing Chinese resources, each of which covers fewer words and a subset of the 11 sensorimotor dimensions. In a validation study, we tested new variables derived from a theoretically motivated metric, Perceptual Strength of Embodiment (PSE) (Huang et al., 2025), together with seven common composite variables, on lexical decision tasks. The results suggest that PSE-Sensorimotor and Minkowski-3 are the strongest composite predictors of lexical decision performance, capturing the facilitatory effects of sensorimotor information on lexical processing. A further exploratory study showed that sensorimotor ratings are substantially recoverable from purely linguistic representations using simple regression models (mean Spearman r = .62 across dimensions), though recovery varied markedly: visual and auditory dimensions yielded higher correspondence than chemosensory ones. Representational similarity analysis further showed that the relational geometry of the sensorimotor space is also partially recoverable (r = .540), consistent with the view that distributional language use encodes aspects of embodied conceptual structure.

</details>


### [149] [More Context, Larger Models, or Moral Knowledge? A Systematic Study of Schwartz Value Detection in Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22641)
*Víctor Yeste,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在政治文本中检测Schwartz价值观时，上下文语境和显性道德知识检索的作用。结果表明，更长上下文并非总是更好：全文档上下文提升监督DeBERTa编码器3.8-4.8 macro-F1，但对零样本LLM帮助不稳定；检索道德知识在匹配比较中一致有效，但模型规模扩大并不保证性能提升；不同价值观对上下文和检索的敏感度各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治文本中Schwartz价值观检测面临隐含线索依赖周围论证、相邻价值观区分度精细等挑战，现有研究尚未系统评估上下文范围和外部道德知识对检测效果的影响。

Method: 采用ValuesML/Touché ValueEval格式，对比句子、窗口、全文档三种输入范围；无检索与基于道德知识库的检索增强两种设置；使用DeBERTa-v3-base/large监督编码器及12B-123B参数的零样本LLM，比较早期融合、晚期融合和交叉注意力等RAG变体。

Result: 全文档上下文提升监督DeBERTa 3.8-4.8 macro-F1，但对零样本LLM帮助不稳定；检索道德知识在早期融合下一致提升各模型家族和上下文条件；模型规模扩大不保证性能提升；早期融合优于晚期融合和交叉注意力RAG变体；上下文和检索对社交情境化或概念易混淆的价值观帮助最大。

Conclusion: 价值观敏感的自然语言处理应联合评估上下文、知识和模型家族，而非将更长输入或更大模型视为通用改进方案。

Abstract: Detecting Schwartz values in political text is difficult because implicit cues often depend on surrounding arguments and fine-grained distinctions between neighboring values. We study when context and explicit moral knowledge help sentence-level value detection. Using the ValuesML/Touch{é} ValueEval format, we compare sentence, window, and full-document inputs; no-RAG and retrieval-augmented settings with a curated moral knowledge base; supervised DeBERTa-v3-base/large encoders; and zero-shot LLMs from 12B to 123B parameters. The results show that more context is not uniformly better: full-document context improves supervised DeBERTa encoders by 3.8--4.8 macro-F1 points over sentence-only input, but does not consistently help zero-shot LLMs. Retrieved moral knowledge is more consistently useful in matched comparisons, improving each tested model family and context condition under early fusion. However, scaling from DeBERTa-v3-base to large and from 12B to larger LLMs does not guarantee gains, and simple early fusion outperforms the tested late-fusion and cross-attention RAG variants for encoders. Per-value analyses show that context and retrieval help most for socially situated or conceptually confusable values. These findings suggest that value-sensitive NLP should evaluate context, knowledge, and model family jointly rather than treating longer inputs or larger models as universal improvements.

</details>


### [150] [Boiling the Frog: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Agentic Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22643)
*Piercosma Bisconti,Matteo Prandi,Federico Pierucci,Federico Sartore,Enrico Panai,Laura Caroli,Yue Zhu,Adam Leon Smith,Luca Nannini,Marcello Galisai,Susanna Cifani,Francesco Giarrusso,Marcantonio Bracale Syrnikov,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"Boiling the Frog"基准测试，评估工具型AI模型在办公场景下对渐进式攻击的脆弱性。该基准通过多轮状态追踪，在持久化工作区中逐步引入风险操作，并在9个模型上测得平均攻击成功率44.4%，最高达92.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全基准仅评估语言模型生成的文本安全性，但当模型以智能体形式部署时，安全关注点从"系统说什么"转向"系统在环境中做什么"，仅评估模型在提示下的响应已不足以应对AI带来的安全挑战。

Method: 构建多轮状态追踪评估框架：每个场景从良性工作区编辑开始，逐步引入风险负载，通过持久化工作区暴露链，将风险负载放置在序列的受控位置，评估最终工件状态是否变为不安全。场景通过三级操作风险分类法组织，基于EU AI Act高风险上下文和GPAI行为准则。

Result: 在9个模型面板上，严格攻击成功率（ASR）平均为44.4%，模型级ASR范围从Claude Haiku 4.5的20.5%到Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite的92.9%，Seed 2.0 Lite也超过80%。Code of Practice失控场景的平均链类别级ASR达到93.3%。

Conclusion: 现有工具型AI模型在面对渐进式攻击时普遍脆弱，特别是针对失控场景的风险防范能力严重不足，亟需更有效的安全对齐策略。

Abstract: Background. Traditional safety benchmarks for language models evaluate generated text: whether a model outputs toxic language, reproduces bias, or follows harmful instructions. When models are deployed as agents, the safety-relevant object shifts from what the system says to what it does within an environment, and evaluating model responses under prompting is no longer sufficient to address the safety challenges posed by artificial intelligence. Recent developments have seen the rise of benchmarks that evaluate large language models as agents. We contribute to this strand of research. Approach. We introduce Boiling the Frog, a benchmark that evaluates whether tool-using AI models deployed in corporate and office settings are susceptible to incremental attacks. Each scenario begins with benign workspace edits and later introduces a risk-bearing request. The benchmark focuses on stateful multi-turn evaluation: chains expose a persistent workspace, place the risk-bearing payload at controlled positions in the turn sequence, and score whether the resulting artifact state becomes unsafe. Scenarios are organized through a three-level operational risk taxonomy grounded in the Boiling the Frog risks, the AI Act Annex I and Annex III high-risk contexts, and EU AI Act's Code of Practice on General-Purpose AI (GPAI). Results. Across a nine-model panel, aggregate strict attack success rate (ASR) is 44.4%. Model-level ASR ranges from 20.5% for Claude Haiku 4.5 to 92.9% for Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite, with Seed 2.0 Lite also above 80%. Average chain category-level ASR reaches 93.3% for Code of Practice loss-of-control scenarios.

</details>


### [151] [Seeing the Poem: Image-Semantic Detection of AI-Generated Modern Chinese Poetry with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22654)
*Shanshan Wang,Fengying Ye,Hanjia Lyu,Caiwen Gou,Junchao Wu,Jingming Yao,Chengzhong Xu,Jiebo Luo,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图像-语义引导的现代诗歌检测方法，通过引入与诗歌内容相关的图像信息，使LLM检测器性能超越传统文本检测方法和最佳传统检测器RoBERTa，Gemini检测器达到85.65%的Macro-F1分数，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明LLM不能有效用作检测器，但这些研究未涉及现代汉语诗歌领域，且尚无相关研究探索LLM在检测现代汉语诗歌方面的性能。

Method: 提出图像-语义引导的诗歌检测方法，创新性地引入反映诗歌内容的图像，通过示例驱动的方式将图像的语义、意象、情感等信息与诗歌文本形成互补判断。

Result: 基于该方法的LLM检测器优于基于纯文本的检测器及最佳传统检测器RoBERTa；Gemini检测器Macro-F1达85.65%，达到SOTA；不同LLM检测器在多个LLM生成数据上均有性能提升。

Conclusion: 图像-语义引导方法能有效提升LLM在现代汉语诗歌检测任务上的表现，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Previous detection studies have shown that LLMs cannot be effectively used as detectors, but these studies have not addressed modern Chinese poetry. Moreover, no relevant research has explored the performance of LLMs in detecting modern Chinese poetry. This paper evaluates and enhances the performance of LLMs as detectors for modern Chinese poetry, and proposes an image-semantic guided poetry detection method. Compared with traditional detection approaches, our method innovatively incorporates images that reflect the content of the poetry. Through example-driven approaches, our method effectively integrates information such as meaning, imagery, and feeling from the image, then forms a complementary judgment with the poem text. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM detectors based on our method outperform baseline detectors based on plain text, and even surpass the best-performing traditional detector, RoBERTa. The Gemini detector using our method achieves a Macro-F1 score of 85.65%, reaching the state-of-the-art level. The performance improvements of different LLM detectors on multiple LLMs-generated data prove the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [152] [Moral Semantics Survive Machine Translation: Cross-Lingual Evidence from Moral Foundations Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22660)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究以波兰语为测试案例，验证了基于LLM翻译的跨语言道德价值观分类方法的可行性。通过约5万条带道德标注的多主题社交媒体帖子，采用四阶段验证流程（LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度、CKA对齐、LLM裁判评估、深度学习分类器一致性测试），发现尽管在俚语、粗俗语和文化负载表达方面存在不足，直接翻译仍能很好保留道德线索，平均余弦相似度达0.86，各基础维度的AUC差异仅0.01-0.02，且在语言模型微调后进一步缩小。结果表明机器翻译是低资源语言道德价值观研究的实用且经济有效的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德语言具有微妙性和文化变异性，难以在不同语言间忠实翻译，而自动道德价值观分类依赖几乎仅存在于英语的特定语言标注语料库。为弥合这一差距，需要探索跨语言迁移的有效途径。

Method: 以波兰语为测试案例，使用约5万条来自多样主题的带道德标注社交媒体帖子，构建包含四阶段原则性验证流程：(1) LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度分析；(2) 中心核对齐(CKA)；(3) LLM作为裁判评估；(4) 深度学习分类器一致性测试。

Result: 尽管在俚语、粗俗语和文化负载表达方面存在不足，直接翻译仍能良好保留道德线索：平均余弦相似度达0.86，各基础道德维度的AUC差异仅为0.01-0.02，且在语言模型微调后差距进一步缩小。

Conclusion: 机器翻译是当前道德价值观研究中语言资源匮乏的语言的实用且经济有效的路径；以波兰语为代表斯拉夫语系验证了该方法的有效性，预期可推广至相关语言。

Abstract: Moral language is subtle and culturally variable, making it difficult to translate faithfully across languages. Idiomatic expressions, slang, and cultural references introduce hard-to-avoid translation artifacts. Yet automated moral values classification depends on language-specific annotated corpora that exist almost exclusively in English. We investigate whether LLM-based translation can bridge this gap, taking Polish as a test case. Using $\sim$50k morally-annotated social media posts from a diverse range of topics, we apply a principled four-method validation pipeline: LaBSE cross-lingual embedding similarity, Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), LLM-as-judge evaluation, and deep learning classifier parity tests. We show that despite shortcomings in handling slang, vulgarity, and culturally-loaded expressions, direct translation preserves subtle moral cues well enough to be harvested by cross-lingual machine learning -- with mean cosine similarity of 0.86 and AUC gaps of 0.01--0.02 across all foundations closing further under fine-tuning of language models. These results demonstrate that machine translation is a practical and cost-effective path to moral values research in languages currently under-resourced in this domain. We demonstrate this for Polish as a representative Slavic language, with expected generalisation to related languages.

</details>


### [153] [Self-Policy Distillation via Capability-Selective Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22675)
*Guangya Hao,Yitong Shang,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao,Hanxue Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自策略蒸馏（SPD），通过从模型自身梯度中提取低秩能力子空间，并在自生成过程中将KV激活投影到该子空间，无需任何外部信号即可实现可泛化的、面向特定能力的自蒸馏，在代码生成、数学推理和多选问答任务上比最先进的自蒸馏方法提升高达13%，比预训练基线提升高达16%，在域外泛化场景下提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法要么依赖昂贵且对前沿模型不可用的外部信号（如正确性过滤、执行反馈），要么不加筛选地训练所有原始输出（领域特定、难以泛化）。同时，自生成输出将任务相关能力与风格模式、格式伪影等无关特征纠缠在一起，稀释了目标能力的训练信号。

Method: SPD首先从模型在正确性定义token上的梯度中提取低秩能力子空间，然后在自生成阶段将KV激活投影到该子空间，最后对投影后的原始输出使用标准下一个token预测损失进行微调。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和多选QA任务上，SPD比无需外部信号的最先进自蒸馏方法提升高达13%，比预训练基线提升高达16%；在域外泛化设置下性能提升15%。

Conclusion: SPD通过能力子空间投影实现了无需外部信号、可泛化且能力选择性的自蒸馏，有效解耦了任务相关能力与无关特征，优于现有自蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Self-distillation bootstraps large language models (LLMs) by training on their own generations. However, existing methods either rely on external signals to curate self-generated outputs (e.g., correctness filtering, execution feedback, and reward search), which are costly and unavailable for the best-performing frontier models, or skip curation entirely and train on all raw outputs, an approach that is often domain-specific and hard to generalize. Both also share a deeper weakness that self-generated outputs entangle task-relevant capability with others, such as stylistic patterns, formatting artifacts, and model-specific errors, diluting the signal for the specific capability one aims to improve. In this paper, we propose Self-Policy Distillation (SPD), which achieves generalizable, capability selective without any external signal. Specifically, SPD extracts a low-rank capability subspace from the model's own gradients on correctness-defining tokens, projects key-value (KV) activations into this subspace during self-generation, and fine-tunes on the resulting raw outputs with standard next-token prediction loss. Through extensive experiments across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice QA, we show that SPD achieves up to 13% improvement over state-of-the-art self-distillation methods without external signals and up to 16% improvement over pre-trained baselines. Notably, SPD demonstrates superior generalizability, achieving 15% better performance under out-of-domain generalization settings.

</details>


### [154] [Tokenization with Split Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22705)
*Craig W. Schmidt,Michael Krumdick,Adam Wiemerslage,Seth Ebner,Varshini Reddy,Yuval Pinter,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToaST是一种基于分割树的新颖子词分词方法，通过递归推理和整数规划直接优化压缩率，在英语文本上相比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少11%以上的token数量，并在1.5B参数语言模型实验中取得最佳CORE分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的子词分词方法（如BPE、WordPiece、UnigramLM）在压缩效率和token数量方面仍有改进空间，作者希望通过直接优化压缩率来减少token数量，从而扩展模型的有效上下文长度。

Method: ToaST包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用预计算的字节n-gram频率，将每个预分词贪心地分裂成完整的二叉树；(2) 给定词表后，推理时递归下降每棵分割树，在每个路径上发射第一个在词表中的节点；(3) 词表选择被建模为整数规划问题，最小化所有分割树的总token数，其LP松弛在实践中接近整数解，可证明得到近似最优词表。

Result: 在词表大小超过40,960时，ToaST相比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少11%以上的token数量；较少使用单字节token，显著提升Renyi效率；在1.5B参数语言模型实验中，CORE分数超越基线2.6%-7.6%，在22个任务中的13个上取得最佳。

Conclusion: ToaST通过直接优化压缩率的词表学习方式，在token效率和模型性能上均优于现有主流分词方法，训练时间与分割树数量呈二次缩放关系，为提升大语言模型的有效上下文长度提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.

</details>


### [155] [ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22734)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Farzaneh Firoozbakht,Lukas Galke Poech,Jan Baumbach,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ChronoMedKG是一个包含460,497个带时间信息的三元组的生物医学知识图谱，覆盖13,431种疾病，通过多智能体流水线从文献中提取，并引入ChronoTQA基准测试，证明其在时序临床推理中显著优于现有知识图谱和LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱（如PrimeKG、Hetionet、iKraph）将疾病关联视为静态事实，缺乏时间信息（如症状在不同年龄段对应不同疾病），这限制了它们在纵向临床推理和检索增强中的实用性。

Method: 通过疾病自治的多智能体流水线构建：多个前沿LLM独立从PubMed/PMC文献中提取知识，保留支持多模型共识、通过可信度过滤和本体对齐的关系。每个关联包含发病窗口或进展阶段等时间成分，并附有PMID可追溯证据和多信号可信度评分。

Result: ChronoMedKG包含460,497个经证据链接的三元组（从1,300万原始提取中过滤），覆盖13,431种疾病，对Orphadata一致性达92.7%，为6,250种疾病添加了时间基础。在ChronoTQA基准上，前沿LLM从静态问题到时序问题性能下降约30个百分点，ChronoMedKG检索可挽救47-65%的长尾失败，而HPOA-RAG仅挽救17-29%。

Conclusion: ChronoMedKG为检索增强的临床系统提供了此前缺失的关键时间轴，显著提升了时序临床推理能力。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation.
  We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment.
  ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.

</details>


### [156] [Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22769)
*Pilchen Hippolyte,Fabre Romain,Signe Talla Franck,Perez Patrick,Grave Edouard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练数据顺序对大型语言模型时间敏感事实知识获取的影响，通过构建包含7000+时间问答的基准，对比了6B参数模型在时间顺序预训练与随机打乱预训练下的表现，发现顺序训练在保持通用语言能力的同时，能获得更及时、更精确的时间知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM通常在打乱语料上训练，导致模型知识冻结在训练时刻，对时间敏感事实知识的时间定位理解不足。作者希望探究预训练数据顺序对模型获取时间敏感事实知识的影响。

Method: 1) 构建了一个包含7000+时间接地问题的综合基准及评估协议；2) 在时间排序的Common Crawl快照上预训练6B参数模型，与标准随机打乱预训练进行对比分析。

Result: 顺序训练的模型在通用语言理解和常识知识上不逊于打乱基线，同时表现出更及时、更精确的时间知识。时间顺序预训练提升事实新鲜度，而打乱预训练在旧数据上表现更优（可能因事实重复增多）。

Conclusion: 时间顺序预训练能有效提升LLM的时间敏感事实知识准确性，为持续学习在LLM中的应用提供了基础，并开源了代码、模型和数据集。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [157] [Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22771)
*Long Phan,Devin Kim,Alexander Pan,Alice Blair,Adam Khoja,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在不同政治立场的对应话题上表现出系统性偏差，本文将其定义为“隐性政治偏见”，识别了7种操作技术，提出情感一致性和帮助一致性两项指标，并引入政治一致性训练（PCT）的强化学习方法以减轻此类偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注LLM的显性政治偏见，但忽视了模型在对应政治话题上不对称处理的隐性偏见现象，需系统定义、量化并缓解这一问题。

Method: 识别7类隐性政治偏见技术；提出情感一致性（衡量修辞与框架对称性）和帮助一致性（衡量回答深度与参与度对称性）两项指标；引入政治一致性训练（PCT），包含情感一致性训练和帮助一致性训练两种互补范式，基于强化学习进行优化。

Result: PCT在保持整体帮助性的同时，显著降低了隐性政治偏见，并在未参与训练的基准测试上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过RL训练方法可以在不牺牲模型有用性的前提下有效缓解LLM的隐性政治偏见，且该方法具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai

</details>


### [158] [Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22785)
*Mirac Suzgun,Emily Shen,Federico Bianchi,Alexander Spangher,Thomas Icard,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Jurafsky,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对六大AI聊天机器人（Gemini 3 Flash/Pro、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5、GPT-4o mini）进行了为期14天的评估，基于2100道源自BBC六种区域服务同日报道的事实问题。结果显示：最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，但自由回答下降11-13%；印地语准确率最低（79%），存在英语中心检索偏差；70%以上错误源于检索而非推理失败；当问题包含虚假前提时，准确率从88-96%骤降至19-70%，且存在检测-准确率悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人正迅速改变人们获取新闻的方式，但尚无研究系统性地评估这些带有专有搜索集成和检索-合成管道的系统在处理跨语言、跨区域新兴事实时的准确性。

Method: 在14天内（2026年2月9-22日），从BBC六种区域服务（美加、阿拉伯、非洲、印地、俄语、土耳其）同日报导中提取2100道事实性问题，对六种AI聊天机器人进行多项选择和自由回答两种评估，并测试包含虚假前提的问题以及检测能力。

Result: 最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，自由回答下降11-13%；印地语准确率最低（79%），存在英语中心检索偏差；70%以上错误由检索失败引起；虚假前提问题下准确率从88-96%降至19-70%；检测-准确率悖论存在。

Conclusion: 高准确率可能掩盖系统性区域不平等、对检索基础设施的近乎完全依赖以及对用户不完美查询的脆弱性。

Abstract: AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.

</details>


### [159] [Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22821)
*Jan Tempus,Philip Whittington,Craig W. Schmidt,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConvexTok，一种将分词器构建形式化为线性规划并用凸优化求解的新算法，相比BPE/Unigram等贪心算法，能持续改进内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte，且可证明与最优解差距在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的BPE和Unigram等分词算法都是贪心算法，只做局部最优决策而不考虑整体词汇表的质量，缺乏全局最优性保证。

Method: 将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题，使用凸优化工具求解，得到新算法ConvexTok。

Result: ConvexTok持续改进了内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte；在下游任务上也有提升但不太一致；经验上可证明与最优解的差距在1%以内。

Conclusion: ConvexTok提供了一种可认证最优性差距的分词器构建方法，为分词问题带来了全局优化的视角。

Abstract: Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.

</details>


### [160] [ACC: Compiling Agent Trajectories for Long-Context Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21850)
*Qisheng Su,Zhen Fang,Shiting Huang,Yu Zeng,Yiming Zhao,Kou Shi,Ziao Zhang,Lin Chen,Zehui Chen,Lijun Wu,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Agent Context Compilation (ACC)方法，将Agent轨迹（工具调用+环境反馈）转化为长上下文QA对，直接训练LLM的长距离推理能力，无需额外人工标注。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，MRCR提升18.1分达68.3，GraphWalks提升7.6分达77.5，接近Qwen3-235B-A22B的水平，且通用能力不受损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM长上下文推理训练需要昂贵的长文档标注或启发式上下文合成；标准Agent SFT掩码工具响应，只训练单轮工具选择，忽略了多轮轨迹中散落的关键证据，形成了监督盲区。

Method: 将搜索、软件工程、数据库查询等Agent的多轮轨迹转化为长上下文问答对：将原始问题与跨轮次收集的工具响应和环境观察拼接，训练模型直接回答问题而不依赖工具调用，使问题与证据之间的长距离依赖关系显式化。

Result: 在MRCR上Qwen3-30B-A3B达68.3（+18.1），GraphWalks上达77.5（+7.6），效果接近Qwen3-235B-A22B；在GPQA、MMLU-Pro、AIME、IFEval上通用能力保持；机制分析显示模型出现任务自适应注意力重组和专家分工。

Conclusion: ACC是一种简单有效的长上下文监督微调方法，可兼容现有长上下文扩展或训练方法，提供可扩展的SFT数据，填补了Agent训练中的监督盲区。

Abstract: Recent development of agents has renewed demand for long-context reasoning capacity of LLMs. However, training LLMs for this capacity requires costly long-document curation or heuristic context synthesis. We observe that agents produce massive trajectories when solving problems, invoking tools and receiving environment observations across many turns. The evidence needed to answer the original question is thus scattered throughout these turns, requiring integration of distant context segments. Nevertheless, standard agent SFT masks tool responses and only trains turn-level tool selection, creating a supervision blind spot where these scattered signals go unused. We propose Agent Context Compilation (ACC), which converts trajectories from search, software engineering, and database querying agents into long-context QA pairs that combine the original question with tool responses and environment observations gathered across multiple turns, training the model to answer directly without tool use. This makes the dependencies between the question and the evidence explicit, enabling direct supervision of long-context reasoning over distant segments without additional annotation. ACC is a simple but effective approach that can be combined with any existing long-context extension or training method, providing scalable supervised fine-tuning data. We validate ACC on long-range dependency modeling tasks through MRCR and GraphWalks, challenging benchmarks requiring cross-turn coreference resolution and graph traversal over extended contexts. Training Qwen3-30B-A3B with ACC achieves 68.3 on MRCR (+18.1) and 77.5 on GraphWalks (+7.6), results comparable to Qwen3-235B-A22B, while preserving general capabilities on GPQA, MMLU-Pro, AIME, and IFEval. Further mechanism analysis reveals that the ACC-trained model exhibits task-adaptive attention restructuring and expert specialization.

</details>


### [161] [From TF-IDF to Transformers: A Comparative and Ensemble Approach to Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22003)
*Dip Biswas Shanto,Mitali Yadav,Prajwal Panth,Suresh Chandra Satapathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种机器学习模型（朴素贝叶斯、逻辑回归、SVM、LightGBM、LSTM、RoBERTa、DistilBERT等）在IMDb电影评论情感分类任务上的表现，其中RoBERTa以93.02%的准确率最优，软投票集成方法进一步提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影评论中的情感分析面临ML模型难以准确理解上下文和元情感含义的挑战，本文旨在系统性地比较多种模型在这一任务上的表现，以寻找最优方案。

Method: 采用NLP方法进行数据预处理和模型评估，在IMDb数据集上评估了朴素贝叶斯、逻辑回归、SVM、LightGBM、LSTM、RoBERTa和DistilBERT等模型，并使用软投票集成策略融合所有模型。

Result: RoBERTa以93.02%的准确率在所有单模型中表现最佳；软投票集成方法进一步提升了分类性能。

Conclusion: RoBERTa在电影评论情感分类任务上显著优于传统ML模型和部分深度学习模型，模型集成策略能有效提升情感分析性能。

Abstract: Sentiment analysis, also referred to as opinion mining, primarily tries to extract opinion from any text-based data. In the context of movie reviews and critics, sentimental analysis can be a helpful tool to predict whether a movie review is generally positive or negative. It can be difficult for the ML models to understand the context or metaphysical sentiment accurately, as ML models rely largely on statistical word representations. The objective of this paper is to examine and categorise movie reviews into positive and negative sentiments. Diverse machine learning models are considered in doing so, and Natural Language Processing (NLP) methodologies are employed for data preprocessing and model assessment. The IMDb dataset is used. Specifically, Naive Bayes, Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machines (SVM), LightGBM, LSTM, and transformer-based models such as RoBERTa and DistilBERT were evaluated. After a lot of testing with accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC, RoBERTa performed better than all the other models, with an accuracy of 93.02%. A soft voting ensemble that combined all the models also improved classification performance, showing that model ensembling works well for sentiment analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Flying Together: Human-Guided Immersive Shared Control for Aerial Robot Teams in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21680)
*Lou De Bel-Air,Luca Morando,Ruitao Chen,Keru Wang,Benjamin Jarvis,Charbel Toumieh,Yang Zhou,Ken Perlin,Dario Floreano,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于虚拟现实的无人机编队共享控制框架，通过用户引导的运动基元规划器和导纳控制器，实现在未知受限环境中操作员与多机器人系统的协同导航与探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多机器人系统在非结构化环境中难以适应不可预见的情况并捕捉操作员驱动的目标，需要人机协同来弥补自主规划的不足。

Method: 采用VR-based共享控制框架，核心包括：(1) 用户引导的运动基元规划器，持续计算无碰撞轨迹并整合操作员输入；(2) 导纳控制器，使操作员灵活影响编队行为；(3) 双边VR界面，通过迁移点引导并实时反馈编队状态；(4) 支持混合现实操作（物理与仿真无人机）。

Result: 实验表明共享控制改善了障碍物规避能力，维持了无人机间距，并减少了操作员负担，验证了沉浸式人在回路多机器人导航的可行性与优势。

Conclusion: VR-based共享控制框架有效结合了人类直觉与自主规划能力，为复杂环境下的多无人机协同导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While autonomous multi-robots can achieve safe and coordinated navigation, they often struggle to adapt to unforeseen conditions and to capture operator-driven objectives in unstructured environments. We present a Virtual Reality (VR)-based shared control framework for teams of drones operating in constrained and unknown environments, enabling real-time, user-guided exploration. At the core of our approach is a novel, user-guided motion-primitive-based planner that computes continuous, collision-free trajectories while continuously integrating operator input. This planner is coupled with an admittance controller, allowing the operator to flexibly influence team behavior and guide drones toward regions of interest that autonomous planners may overlook. The system supports mixed-reality operations with both physical and simulated drones, and implements a bilateral VR-based interface, allowing the operator to guide the robot team via migration points while receiving immediate visual feedback of the team state. Experimental results show that shared control improves obstacle avoidance, maintains inter-agent spacing, and reduces operator effort, demonstrating the feasibility and advantages of immersive, human-in-the-loop multi-robot navigation.

</details>


### [163] [Distributed Multi-Coverage for Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21686)
*Mariem Guitouni,Aaron T. Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式多覆盖算法，使无人机群在局部感知和通信下无需全局协调即可实现资产的多重冗余覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中无人机群面临通信范围有限、机载计算受限、局部故障不可中断任务等约束，而现有集中式整数规划方法无法满足这些分布式需求。

Method: 提出分布式多覆盖算法，基于局部感知和局部通信，无需全局协调，使机群自主分配覆盖任务。

Result: 算法在局部条件下实现了多覆盖，满足不同资产重要性的冗余覆盖需求。

Conclusion: 该分布式方法解决了实际部署中通信、计算和容错的关键约束，适用于监视、环境监测和基础设施巡检等任务。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms deployed for surveillance, environmental monitoring, and infrastructure inspection must maintain reliable coverage of critical assets despite robot failures. This requires multicoverage: each asset must be observed by multiple robots for redundancy, with coverage requirements varying by asset importance. While recent work has solved the centralized problem optimally using integer programming, practical deployments face constraints that demand distributed solutions: robots operate with limited communication ranges, onboard computation restricts global planning, and partial system failures must not cause mission abort. We present a distributed multicoverage algorithm for robot swarms operating with local sensing, local communication, and no global coordination.

</details>


### [164] [Closed-Loop Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Deformable Microfiber Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21688)
*Alessandro Amici,Houari Bettahar,Veeti Jaakkola,Quan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种闭环 sim-to-real 强化学习方法，在简化无摩擦模拟器中训练策略，直接迁移至物理双夹持器微操作系统中，无需重新训练或域适应，在丝微纤维形状控制上实现亚毫米级精度（平均形状误差 270±80 μm），验证了简化模拟器+闭环视觉反馈可弥补未建模表面交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观尺度下表面和界面相互作用难以精确建模，限制了传统基于模型的控制和 sim-to-real 学习方法在自主接触式微操作中的应用。

Method: 在简化无摩擦模拟器中训练几何形状调节的强化学习策略，部署时利用实时视觉反馈闭环迭代修正未建模表面交互效应；策略直接迁移至 40 Hz 物理双夹持器系统，无需重新训练或域适应。

Result: 在丝微纤维上，24种不同初始配置下平均点状形状误差为 270±80 μm；覆盖3种纤维直径（50、80、120 μm）和3种操作长度（10、15、20 mm）的9种样本均实现亚毫米级最终形状误差，无需重新训练或调参。

Conclusion: 在简化模拟器中学习的策略，只要 sim-to-real 失配的任务相关效应在闭环反馈中可观测且可修正，就能在表面接触下实现可重复的真实世界微纤维形状调节。

Abstract: Autonomous contact-based micromanipulation is challenging because surface and interfacial interactions at the microscale are difficult to model accurately, limiting the use of conventional model-based control and sim-to-real learning. We present a closed-loop sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) approach for microfiber shape control on a surface. The central idea is to train geometric shape regulation in a simplified frictionless simulator and rely on real-time visual feedback during deployment to iteratively correct the observed effects of unmodeled surface interactions. An RL policy trained entirely in simulation is transferred directly to a physical dual-gripper micromanipulation system operating at 40 Hz, without retraining or domain adaptation. Using silk microfibers as a testbed, the policy achieves a mean point-wise shape error of 270 $\pm$ 80 $μ$m across twenty-four diverse initial configurations. Across nine specimens covering all combinations of three fiber diameters (50, 80, and 120 $μ$m) and three manipulated lengths (10 mm, 15mm, and 20 mm), the same policy achieves sub-millimeter final shape error without any retraining or retuning. These results show that a policy learned in a simplified simulator can achieve repeatable real-world microfiber shape regulation under surface contact, provided that the task-relevant effects of the sim-to-real mismatch remain observable and correctable within the closed feedback loop.

</details>


### [165] [Motion Design for Grasp-Based Dynamic Locomotion in Microgravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21704)
*Chaerim Moon,Joohyung Kim,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多足机器人在微重力环境下基于抓取的动力运动，提出了可参数化的运动规划框架，并通过两种四足形态的仿真实验，验证了增大可行接触力旋量空间和抑制冲击性全身动力学可提升运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境下的锚点稀疏且不规则，传统运动方式受限，需要多肢体抓取式运动；而动态运动需要同时协调锚点交互和全身动力学/运动学约束，现有研究对此缺乏系统性设计指导。

Method: 提出了可参数化的运动规划框架，支持调整步态模式、步幅、运动速度和标称姿态等设计参数；采用两种代表性四足形态，在物理仿真中评估运动稳定性和驱动力需求。

Result: 发现增大可行接触力旋量空间和抑制冲击性全身动力学可以有效改善运动性能。

Conclusion: 这些发现为微重力环境下多肢体系统的接触配置选择和全身协调策略提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Locomotion in microgravity often relies on sparsely and irregularly arranged anchors, motivating grasp-based mobility with multiple limbs. In this setting, dynamic locomotion is feasible only through deliberate regulation of both anchored interactions and whole-body coordination under coupled dynamic and kinematic constraints. This paper presents design insights for grasp-based dynamic locomotion with multi-limbed robotic systems in microgravity, targeting scenarios that require 6D limb manipulation to establish contacts with candidate anchors. The investigated design parameters include gait pattern, stride length, locomotion speed, and nominal posture. A parameterizable locomotion planning framework is proposed to support variations of these parameters and to evaluate the resulting locomotion performance in terms of stability and actuation demand. Two representative quadruped morphologies are adopted for evaluation in physics-based simulation. The results demonstrate that enlarging the feasible contact wrench space and attenuating impulsive whole-body dynamics improve locomotion performance. These findings inform strategies for contact configuration selection and whole-body coordination in microgravity locomotion with multi-limbed systems.

</details>


### [166] [PGDG: Physically Grounded Data Generation for Robust Bimanual Policy Learning from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21710)
*Cunxi Dai,Haoran Chang,Aditya Nisal,Rahul Kumar,Guofei Chen,Tao Chen,Yuzhe Qin,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGDG通过物理驱动采样和数据集策展，将单次演示扩展为包含多样化恢复行为的数据集，无需人工标注，显著提升双臂操作任务中的行为克隆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的双臂操作中，行为克隆面临两个挑战：(1) 收集多样化演示成本高昂；(2) 小扰动导致系统陷入无恢复监督的离流形状态。现有方法缺乏在不增加人工标注条件下生成多样化恢复行为的手段。

Method: 提出PGDG框架，包含两个迭代组件：(1) 物理驱动采样器：从更新后的分布中采样物理可行的 rollout 候选，保留成功轨迹；(2) 数据集策展器：选择信息量大、非冗余、可恢复的行为，更新采样分布以覆盖欠覆盖的恢复模式。同时，采用短视采样控制对选中的风险状态重新标注纠正动作。

Result: 在四个双臂操作任务中，PGDG一致优于纯空间增强方法。以RotateBox-Pitch为例，仿真成功率从38%提升至93%，真实世界从35%提升至82%。在GR00T基础模型微调中，成功率从46%提升至77%。

Conclusion: PGDG通过零样本策展将单条演示扩展为包含多样化恢复行为的数据集，有效解决了接触丰富双臂操作中行为克隆的离流形问题，在仿真和真实世界均取得显著提升，并能增强基础模型的微调效果。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation remains challenging because diverse demonstrations are expensive to collect, and even small disturbances can push the system into off-manifold states where no recovery supervision is available. We propose PGDG, a data generation framework with zero-shot curation that expands a single demonstration into a compact dataset of physically plausible, successful, and diverse recovery behaviors without additional human labeling. PGDG iterates between a physics-grounded sampler and a dataset curator, where the curator selects informative, non-redundant, and recoverable behaviors to update the sampling distribution toward under-covered recovery modes, and the sampler draws physically plausible rollout candidates from this updated distribution and retains successful trajectories. To further improve data quality, PGDG applies short-horizon sampling-based control to relabel selected risky states with corrective actions. Across four bimanual manipulation tasks, PGDG consistently outperforms spatial-only augmentation in both simulation and zero-shot real-world transfer. On RotateBox-Pitch, success improves from 38% to 93% in simulation and from 35% to 82% in the real world. PGDG also enables effective foundation models fine-tuning such as GR00T, increasing success from 46% to 77%. Additional results are available in our website: https://cunxid.github.io/PGDG/.

</details>


### [167] [Mind the Gaps: Multi-Robot Feedback-Driven Ergodic Coverage in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21719)
*Thales Costa Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实时环境模型反馈的自适应覆盖策略，通过在线更新的参数化模型构建目标空间信息分布，增强了传统遍历轨迹优化方法，解决了多机器人在未知先验分布环境中的自适应覆盖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遍历搜索方法虽然能优化机器人轨迹，使时间平均空间分布与环境信息分布对齐，但无法处理环境的未知先验分布，导致覆盖效率受限。

Method: 提出自适应覆盖策略，利用环境模型的实时反馈调整机器人采样行为；通过在线更新的参数化环境模型构建目标空间信息分布，增强传统遍历轨迹优化方法。

Result: 通过仿真验证了该方法在提升覆盖效率和资源分配方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能使机器人动态优先关注高兴趣区域，提高覆盖效率，为单个智能体合成有效控制策略，并在未知先验分布的环境中优化资源使用。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of multi-robot adaptive coverage, where teams of robots perform dynamic sampling by continuously adjusting their positions to collect data in an environment. This task can be challenging, particularly when robots must be efficiently allocated to new sampling locations over time. Ergodic search methods optimize robot trajectories by ensuring that the robots' time-averaged spatial distribution aligns with the spatial distribution of environmental information. While these methods promote effective exploration provided a target distribution, they often fail to account for unknown prior distributions of the environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an adaptive coverage strategy that utilizes real-time feedback from an environmental model to adjust robot sampling behavior in response to unknown conditions. Our approach enhances traditional ergodic trajectory optimization by constructing a target spatial information distribution based on parametric models of the environment, which are updated online. This strategy assumes that the environment is either static or changes slowly compared to the robot's motion. Our framework allows robots to dynamically prioritize regions of high interest, improving coverage efficiency, synthesizing effective control policies for individual agents, and optimizing resource use in settings with unknown prior distributions. We validate our approach through simulations, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing coverage and resource allocation.

</details>


### [168] [Safe and Steerable Geometric Motion Policies for Robotic Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21811)
*Albert Wu,Riccardo Bonalli,Thomas Lew,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafePBDS是一种几何一致性框架，通过拉回控制障碍函数和任务流形动作接口，在任意任务流形上计算最优且可证明安全的配置流形加速度，实现灵巧操作。在Franka Panda-Allegro Hand平台上，灵巧抓取成功率达92.5%，并实现了首个基于模型的全面驱动掌心向下手中重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧操作需要持续协调定义在异构几何空间上的目标和约束（如机器人在ℝ⁷配置流形上控制，同时需跟踪SE(3)上的末端执行器位姿并满足ℝ中的避障裕度），现有方法缺乏一个几何一致且可证明安全的统一框架来处理这种多几何空间协调问题。

Method: 1) 拉回控制障碍函数构造：将任务流形上的安全条件转换为配置流形加速度上的线性约束；2) 任务流形动作接口：允许高层策略注入低维残差运动，零输入恢复自治行为，任意输入下安全性保持不变。

Result: 在23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand平台上，灵巧抓取在20个家用物体、120次试验中成功率达92.5%；通过一维动作接口实现任意一个手指的排除，3指抓取在3个物体、36次试验中成功率达94.4%；实现了首个基于模型的全面驱动掌心向下手中重定向，在变负载和腕部运动下双向偏航旋转超过360°。

Conclusion: SafePBDS在几何一致性框架下实现了可证明安全的灵巧操作，同时保持了高效规划能力和任务适应性，为复杂机器人操作任务提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous manipulation requires continuously reconciling objectives and constraints defined on heterogeneous geometric spaces: a robot controlled on a $\mathbb{R}^7$ configuration manifold may need to track end effector poses on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ while satisfying obstacle avoidance margins in $\mathbb{R}$. We present Safe Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems (SafePBDS), a geometrically consistent framework that computes optimal, certifiably safe configuration manifold accelerations from objectives and safety requirements on arbitrary task manifolds. SafePBDS builds on prior work that combines predefined task manifold dynamical systems to produce autonomous motion. Its first innovation is a pullback control barrier function construction, which converts task manifold safety conditions into linear constraints on configuration manifold accelerations. The second innovation is a task manifold action interface that allows a high-level policy to inject low dimensional residual motions; zero input recovers the autonomous behavior, while safety is preserved under arbitrary inputs. This lets high-level policies efficiently steer exploration while leaving precise motion to the autonomous behavior. We validate SafePBDS in simulation and on a 23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand platform. On dexterous grasping, SafePBDS achieves a $92.5\%$ success rate across 20 household objects and 120 trials. Using the action interface, the method can exclude any one of the four fingers during grasping via a one-dimensional action, achieving $94.4\%$ 3-finger grasp success across 3 objects and 36 trials. The efficient planning and safety guarantee of SafePBDS also enables the first model-based, fully actuated palm-down in-hand reorientation, exceeding $360^\circ$ of yaw rotation in both directions under varying object weight and wrist motion. Demo video and details: https://tml.stanford.edu/safe-pbds

</details>


### [169] [EvoScene-VLA: Evolving Scene Beliefs Inside the Action Decoder for Chunked Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21862)
*Chushan Zhang,Ruihan Lu,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoScene-VLA提出了一种跨动作块持续更新的场景状态机制，通过循环场景前缀结合当前观测与上一动作块的场景更新，使VLM每次调用时都拥有反映近期动作和当前视觉的场景先验。在31个RoboTwin任务上，固定评估成功率从87.2%提升至89.1%，随机评估从86.1%提升至88.5%，真实机器人也优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分块VLA策略每个控制步仅依赖当前视觉观测，但机器人动作导致的接触、遮挡和物体运动会在下一次视觉更新前改变后续决策依赖的几何信息。空间VLA改进当前帧几何，时序VLA聚合历史帧，但都缺乏跨动作块的动作更新场景先验。

Method: 提出EvoScene-VLA，其核心是循环场景前缀机制：VLM将当前观测的视觉信息与上一动作块的场景更新结合，动作解码器同时输出下一动作块和紧凑场景更新。训练时使用Scene Predictor提供未来场景token目标，Geometric Anchor将场景槽与冻结的深度和3D教师对齐，部署时丢弃这两个模块。

Result: 在31个RoboTwin任务上，固定评估成功率从87.2%提升至89.1%，随机评估从86.1%提升至88.5%。在Galaxea R1-Lite真实机器人上优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 持续的动作更新场景先验能有效提升分块VLA策略在复杂操作任务中的表现，该方法在仿真和真实场景中均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Chunked vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict multi-step robot controls, conditioning each update on the current visual observation alone. Yet robot actions cause contact, occlusion, and object motion, and the geometry that later decisions depend on can change before the next visual update arrives. Spatial VLAs improve current-frame geometry. Temporal VLAs aggregate past frames. Neither maintains an action-updated scene prior across chunks. We argue for a persistent action-updated scene state across control calls, and introduce EvoScene-VLA. Its recurrent scene prefix carries a geometry-aware scene state across chunks. At each vision-language model (VLM) call, the VLM combines scene information from the current observation with the action-updated prior from the previous chunk; the action decoder outputs both the next action chunk and a compact scene update. This update becomes the next prior, which the VLM corrects against the new observation when the next call arrives. Each control call therefore starts from a scene prior that reflects both recent actions and fresh visual evidence. During training, \textbf{Scene Predictor} supplies future scene-token targets, and Geometric Anchor aligns scene slots with frozen depth and 3D teachers. We discard both modules at deployment. On 31 RoboTwin tasks, EvoScene-VLA raises average success from 87.2% to 89.1% in fixed evaluation and from 86.1% to 88.5% in randomized evaluation. On the Galaxea R1-Lite real robot, EvoScene-VLA outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [170] [Higher Order Reasoning for Collaborative Communicationless Mobile Robot Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21901)
*Jonathan Reasoner,Nicola Bezzo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态认知规划框架，使多机器人在无通信环境下通过高阶推理实现隐式协调与长时域规划，并在仿真和实物实验中相比一阶基线方法显著缩短任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无通信环境中，多机器人系统无法依赖常规的信息交换进行协调，现有策略难以实现有效的隐式协作与长期规划。

Method: 提出动态认知规划框架，包含：高阶信念粒子形成与传播、贝叶斯推断更新世界信念、基于行为树的动作选择以预测队友决策、结合时域感知的MPPI控制器将推理融入底层执行。

Result: 在仿真和实物实验中，该方法相比一阶基线方法持续缩短任务完成时间，验证了认知逻辑在通信受限环境下协调的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 认知逻辑可作为通信受限领域中弹性协调的鲁棒基础。

Abstract: In communicationless environments, multi-robot systems must operate without the constant information exchange that many coordination strategies typically assume. This paper presents a novel dynamic epistemic planning framework that enables implicit coordination and long horizon planning through higher-order reasoning among robots. With our approach, robots form and propagate higher-order belief particles, update world beliefs using Bayesian inference, and select actions via a behavior tree that anticipates teammates' likely decisions. A temporally aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller integrates this reasoning into low-level execution, allowing robots to plan intercepts and adapt trajectories under partial observability. The proposed framework is evaluated in both simulations and physical experiments, where it consistently reduces task completion time compared to a first-order baseline, demonstrating that epistemic logic can serve as a robust foundation for resilient coordination in communication-restricted domains.

</details>


### [171] [Non-Contact Vibration-Based Damage Detection of Civil Structures Using a Cost-Effective Autonomous UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21914)
*Javier Becerril,Maximiliano Vargas,Jennifer Herrera,Joanna Gutierrez,Jorge Rios,Mohsen Amjadian,Constantine Tarawneh,Jinghao Yang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低成本自主无人机(UAV)的非接触式振动结构损伤检测方法，通过视觉运动跟踪从视频中提取振动信号，识别固有频率变化以检测结构损伤。实验验证了智能手机、USB摄像头和定制无人机平台的有效性，无人机虽误差略高(达5.7%)但能可靠检测损伤，且成本远低于商业系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触式结构健康监测在难以布设传感器或GPS受限等场景下存在局限性，需要一种低成本、灵活且可扩展的非接触式检测方案。

Method: 利用无人机搭载摄像头进行视频录制，通过视觉运动跟踪提取位移时间序列，在频域中分析固有频率变化，并与接触式加速度计和有限元模型参考值对比验证。

Result: 所有平台均成功捕捉到固有频率及其损伤导致的频移；无人机平台误差略高(最高5.7%)，但能可靠检测损伤；低成本无人机性能与商业系统相当。

Conclusion: 低成本自主无人机为结构健康监测提供了实用、灵活且可扩展的解决方案，尤其适用于接触式传感不可行的场景，并支持多无人机协同部署以增强检测覆盖和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a non-contact approach for vibration-based structural damage detection using an autonomous and customized cost-effective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Vibration signals are extracted from video recordings through vision-based motion tracking to identify shifts in natural frequencies indicative of structural degradation. A laboratory-scale frame structure is evaluated under healthy and simulated-damage conditions. The proposed system is validated through an experimental study involving two smartphones, a USB camera, and a custom-built low-cost UAV equipped with an onboard camera and an autonomous alignment system for operation in GPS-denied environments. The displacement time is extracted and analyzed in the frequency domain and compared to reference measurements from contact accelerometers and a finite element model. Experimental results show that all platforms successfully capture the fundamental frequency and its shift due to damage. Although the UAV exhibits slightly higher errors (up to 5.7%) due to platform-induced disturbances and sensing limitations, it reliably detects damage-induced frequency changes. Compared to commercial UAV systems, the proposed platform achieves comparable inspection performance at significantly lower cost. These results demonstrate that low-cost autonomous UAVs provide a practical, flexible, and scalable solution for structural health monitoring, particularly in scenarios where contact-based sensing is impractical. The findings also support the potential for the deployment of multiple cooperative UAVs to further enhance inspection coverage and robustness.

</details>


### [172] [Auction-Consensus Algorithm with Learned Bidding Scheme for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21932)
*Jose Rodriguez,Constantine Tarawneh,Sven Koenig,Wenjie Dong,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习增强的拍卖-共识框架，用训练好的神经出价策略取代CBBA的贪婪启发式出价机制，结合集中训练与分散执行范式，通过强化学习（PPO）优化出价策略，实验表明该方法相比传统CBBA提升了多机器人任务分配的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBBA等拍卖-共识算法依赖手工设计的贪心评分函数，在多机器人任务分配中往往导致次优解，需要一种能学习更优出价策略的方法来提升分配质量。

Method: 提出学习增强的拍卖-共识框架：用强化学习（PPO）训练的神经出价策略替代CBBA的确定性出价机制，采用集中训练与分散执行范式，智能体基于局部观测计算任务出价，保留标准拍卖和共识阶段实现分散协调；使用混合整数线性规划得到的全局最优解来设计奖励函数。

Result: 在不同规模的机器人编队上，学习得到的出价策略在解质量上优于传统CBBA，同时保持了分散执行的能力。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与经典分散协调算法相结合是提升多机器人任务分配质量的可行途径，为可扩展的高质量分散式MRTA提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-Robot Task Allocation (MRTA) is a central challenge in decentralized multi-agent systems, where teams of robots must cooperatively assign and execute tasks under limited communication while optimizing global performance objectives. Auction-consensus algorithms, such as the Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA), provide scalable decentralized coordination with provable convergence, but rely on hand-crafted greedy scoring functions that often lead to suboptimal task allocations. This paper proposes a learning-enhanced auction-consensus framework in which CBBA's deterministic bidding mechanism is replaced by a neural bidding policy trained using reinforcement learning. Under a centralized training and decentralized execution paradigm, agents learn to compute task bids from partial local observations while retaining the standard auction and consensus phases for decentralized coordination. The learned bidding policy is trained using Proximal Policy Optimization with rewards shaped by proximity to globally optimal solutions obtained via mixed-integer linear programming. Multiple neural architectures are evaluated, including a Neural Additive Model, the Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model, and the Set Transformer Model. Experimental results across varying swarm sizes demonstrate that learned bidding policies can improve solution quality over classical CBBA while preserving decentralized execution. The proposed approach highlights the effectiveness of integrating reinforcement learning with classical distributed coordination algorithms, offering a scalable pathway toward higher-quality decentralized multi-robot task allocation.

</details>


### [173] [Learning to Evolve: Multi-modal Interactive Fields for Robust Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21935)
*Peifeng Jiang,Hong Liu,Jin Jin,Wenshuai Wang,Xia Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态交互场(MIF)，一种面向人形机器人的闭环感知-适应系统，融合置信度感知的语义3D高斯泼溅、差异触发的空间记忆更新和任务驱动几何重建，在动态环境中将重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，语义内存占用减少91.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在安全导向操控导航中需要可靠的场景记忆，但现有语义建图和场景图系统难以应对运动导致的感知失真、环境变化以及交互级几何安全约束，因为它们通常假设稳定的相机轨迹、静态环境或粗略的物体几何。

Method: MIF耦合三个场：不确定性感知的3DGS外观场(抑制步态模糊)、空间场(维护拓扑记忆)、几何场(支持操控交互位姿安全)。引入差异检测分数区分运动导致的假阳性变化与持久变化，仅更新局部不一致区域。

Result: 在Unitree-G1人形机器人真实动态办公室环境中，相比静态场景图记忆，非静态环境重定位成功率从12%提升至94%，通过特征蒸馏将语义内存占用减少91.4%。

Conclusion: MIF有效解决了人形机器人在动态环境中的场景记忆问题，实现了高可靠性和低内存占用的在线操作。

Abstract: Safe manipulation-oriented navigation for humanoid robots requires scene memory that remains reliable under locomotion-induced perceptual distortion, environmental changes, and interaction-level geometric safety constraints. Existing semantic mapping and scene-graph systems are difficult to deploy directly in this setting because they often assume stable camera trajectories, static environments, or coarse object geometry. We introduce the Multi-modal Interactive Field (MIF), a humanoid-oriented system that integrates confidence-aware semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting, discrepancy-triggered spatial memory updates, and task-driven geometric reconstruction within a closed-loop perception-adaptation pipeline. MIF couples three fields: an uncertainty-aware 3DGS Appearance Field that suppresses gait-induced blur, a Spatial Field that maintains topological memory, and a Geometry Field that supports Interaction Pose Safety (IPS) before manipulation. A discrepancy detection score is introduced to separate locomotion-induced false-positive changes from persistent changes and updates only locally inconsistent regions. On a Unitree-G1 humanoid in a real dynamic office, MIF improves relocation success in non-static environments from 12% to 94% compared with static scene-graph memory, while reducing semantic memory footprint by 91.4% through feature distillation for practical online operation. Project page and code: https://ziya-jiang.github.io/MIF-homepage/

</details>


### [174] [Industrial Dual-Arm Box Handling via Online Inertial Estimation and Convex Wrench Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22021)
*Kenzhi Iskandar Wong,Lin Yang,Qian Ying Lee,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种摩擦感知的双臂箱体操作框架，通过在线估计未知惯性参数（质量和质心），并利用二阶锥规划（SOCP）在椭圆摩擦极限曲面约束下计算摩擦可行的接触力与扭转力矩，同时加入离线轨迹优化以减少不必要的物体-环境接触，最终在真实双臂机器人系统上验证了对未知惯性属性物体的稳定抓取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人搬运箱体时，质量和质心未知会导致力-力矩平衡不稳定，进而引发滑移、掉落、方向偏差或过度挤压等问题，现有方法缺乏对摩擦可行性和接触力分配的统一处理。

Method: 1) 在线估计：基于测量的接触力计算物体质量和质心位置；2) 摩擦可行性约束：构建椭圆摩擦极限曲面（FLS），通过二阶锥规划（SOCP）求解摩擦可行的接触力和扭转力矩；3) 离线轨迹优化：减少几何约束下物体与环境的非预期接触；4) 将摩擦可行性作为硬约束，在可行域内最小化接触力。

Result: 在真实双臂机器人系统上，针对不同质心配置的未知惯性属性物体进行了实验，证明了该方法能够实现稳定抓取并维持摩擦接触的稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过在线惯性参数估计与摩擦可行接触力优化，有效解决了未知惯性属性物体的稳定搬运问题，避免了将防滑与防过度挤压作为独立目标分别调参的繁琐过程。

Abstract: Industrial robotic object handling often involves boxes and packages whose mass and center of mass are not known in advance. These uncertainties affect the force--moment balance required for stable lifting, and improper regulation of contact wrenches can lead to slip, object drop, orientation deviation, or excessive squeezing. This paper presents a friction-aware dual-arm box-handling framework for objects with unknown inertial properties. The proposed approach estimates the object mass and center of mass online from measured contact wrenches, and computes friction-feasible contact forces and torsional moments through a second-order cone program (SOCP) under ellipsoidal friction-limit-surface constraints. An offline trajectory refinement stage is also included to reduce undesired object--environment contact when geometric constraints are present. By enforcing friction feasibility as a hard constraint and minimizing contact effort within the feasible region, the framework achieves stable lifting without treating slip avoidance and excessive squeezing as separately tuned objectives. Experiments on a real dual-arm robotic system under different center-of-mass configurations demonstrate that the method lifts objects with unknown inertial properties while maintaining stable frictional contact.

</details>


### [175] [CoRMA: Contrastive RMA for Contact-Rich Meta-Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22082)
*Wentian Wang,Chutong Wen,Hongxu Ma,Wuhao Wang,Zhexiong Xue,Abdul Haseeb Nizamani,Dandi Zhou,Xinhai Sun,Jianqiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRMA是一个基于对比学习的机器人运动自适应框架，通过紧凑的6维语义接触上下文替代原始模拟器参数，利用可部署的因果Transformer在线推断接触状态，实现了无需演示、特权信息或梯度更新的片内自适应，在装配任务中比FORGE基线具有更高的实际硬件成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMA方法依赖原始模拟器参数自适应，难以直接推广到力占主导的精密装配任务；同时FORGE等基线在仿真中表现良好但在真实硬件上性能大幅下降，需要一种无需演示、特权信息或梯度更新的高效自适应方法。

Method: 1) 提出6维语义接触上下文（接触起始、横向啮合、导向过渡、接触方向、卡滞）代替原始模拟器参数；2) 使用因果Transformer适配器，基于力、本体感知和动作历史，通过语义回归和力域对比学习目标在线推断该上下文；3) 部署时移除oracle上下文，替换为推断上下文，实现片内自适应。

Result: 在PegInsert、GearMesh和NutThread三个装配任务以及真实Marvin机械臂上评估，相比FORGE基线在仿真中成功率高但硬件上显著下降的问题，CoRMA在受控目标位姿噪声下保持了更高的真实硬件成功率。

Conclusion: 语义接触推断可作为相关装配任务家族内可复用的自适应接口，而更广泛的未见任务泛化和Real2Sim校准留作未来工作。

Abstract: We present CoRMA(Contrastive Robotic Motor Adaptation), a context-based meta-adaptation framework that modifies RMA for force-dominant assembly. CoRMA replaces raw simulator-parameter adaptation with a compact 6D simulator-only semantic contact context describing contact onset, lateral engagement, guided transition, contact direction, and jamming. A deployable causal Transformer adapter infers this context online from force, proprioceptive, and action histories using semantic regression and a force-regime contrastive objective. At deployment, oracle context is removed and replaced by the inferred context, enabling within-episode adaptation without demonstrations, privileged inputs, or gradient updates. We evaluate CoRMA on PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread in Isaac Lab / Isaac Sim~5.0 and on a real Marvin arm. Compared with FORGE baselines that achieve high simulation success but degrade substantially on hardware, CoRMA retains higher verified real success under controlled target-pose noise. These results support semantic contact inference as a reusable adaptation interface within a related assembly task family, while broader unseen-task generalization and Real2Sim calibration remain future work.

</details>


### [176] [Beyond Pixels: Learning Invariant Rewards for Real-World Robotics From a Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22123)
*Tengye Xu,Yangting Sun,Ziju Shen,Guanqi Chen,Zhen Fu,Chen yizhou,Hua Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从少量演示中学习不变符号奖励函数的框架，通过发现行为不变量而非拟合视觉特征，实现奖励函数对位置、视角、物体变化的零样本泛化，在Meta-World和Franka操作任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的奖励模型倾向于记忆特定像素分布，无法泛化到实验室环境之外的真实世界操控问题，同一任务在不同物体实例、位置和视角下会出现大量变体。

Method: 提出一个双组件框架：(1) 结构奖励公式，编码任务级策略和物理约束，保持最优策略不变性；(2) 混合符号-数值过程，从演示中提取行为不变量，无需在线交互。

Result: 在8个Meta-World任务和3个Franka操作任务上，该方法在过程对齐和策略 rollout 排序能力上优于基线，加速了下游策略学习。三个真实世界OOD实验表明，学习到的奖励零样本泛化到位置、视角和物体变化。

Conclusion: 从视觉特征拟合转向行为不变量发现是实现奖励函数跨变体泛化的有效途径，单个奖励表示可在不同任务变体中重复使用，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Designing reward functions that generalize beyond controlled laboratory settings remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning for robotics. In open-world manipulation problems, a single task can appear in numerous variants through different object instances, positions, and camera viewpoints. Recent vision-based reward models tend to memorize specific pixel distributions and fail to generalize beyond their training conditions. To address this, we propose a framework that learns invariant symbolic reward functions from as few as five demonstrations. The insight is to shift from visual feature-fitting to the discovery of behavioral invariants: task-level properties that remain constant across diverse visual instantiations. The framework has two coupled components: a structural reward formulation that encodes task-level strategies and physical constraints while preserving optimal policy invariance, and a hybrid symbolic-numerical procedure that distills these invariants from demonstrations without online interaction. Experiments on eight Meta-World tasks and three Franka manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method achieves stronger process alignment and policy rollout ranking abilities compared to baselines, accelerating downstream policy learning. Three real-world out-of-distribution experiments further show that the same learned reward generalizes zero-shot to position, viewpoint, and object variations, enabling a single reward representation to be reused across diverse task variants in practice.

</details>


### [177] [Action with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22183)
*Weilong Guo,Yuchen Wang,Renping Zhou,Yunfeng Zhang,Rui Fang,Yue Meng,Wenda Xu,Yuan He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVP是一种端到端架构，通过让VLM推断下一阶段目标并生成视觉基元令牌来条件化流匹配动作专家，从而解耦认知感知与运动控制，在通用拾取放置任务上成功率比pi_0.5提升27.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA架构将指令理解、空间场景感知和运动控制耦合在单一学习目标中，导致动作专家需要隐式重新学习预训练VLM已具备的认知和感知能力，限制了学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: 提出AVP架构，VLM推断下一阶段目标并发射视觉基元令牌，以此条件化流匹配动作专家，动作监督来自末端执行器运动学。

Result: 在通用拾取放置任务的真实机器人实验中，AVP相比pi_0.5提升27.61%的成功率，在数据效率、空间组合泛化和物体级迁移方面均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 解耦认知感知与运动控制的视觉基元接口设计能有效提升VLA模型的学习效率和泛化性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. A common design in current architectures maps language instructions and visual observations to actions in a single forward pass. While conceptually simple, this formulation entangles instruction comprehension, spatial scene understanding, and motor control within a single learning objective. As a result, the action expert must implicitly relearn cognitive and perceptual capabilities already present in the pretrained VLM, which can limit both learning efficiency and generalization. We introduce AVP (Action with Visual Primitives), an end-to-end architecture that implements this visual-primitive-centric interface: the VLM infers the next-stage target and emits visual-primitive tokens that condition a flow-matching action expert, with supervision derived from end-effector kinematics. Real-robot experiments on general pick-and-place tasks show that AVP improves the success rate by 27.61% over pi_0.5 and outperforms other recent methods, with consistent gains in data efficiency, spatial-compositional generalization, and object-level transfer.

</details>


### [178] [Learning A Unified Risk Map for Autonomous Driving in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22189)
*Jie Jia,Yaofeng Su,Zeyu Bao,Yun Hong,Bingzhao Gao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向部分可观测环境的统一风险地图建模与学习框架，通过时空建模整合交通流风险与碰撞风险，并利用扩散模型生成对抗性遮挡场景，在Waymo数据集上显著提升了自动驾驶在遮挡场景下的安全规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遮挡感知方法要么基于可达状态高估风险，要么在高遮挡不确定性下难以预测准确轨迹，缺乏对遮挡诱导风险的细粒度评估手段，且遮挡交互场景数据稀缺。

Method: 1) 通过时空建模统一整合交通流风险与碰撞风险，实现遮挡诱导危害的细粒度评估；2) 提出基于扩散模型的场景生成框架，生成真实且具有对抗性的遮挡交互场景；3) 将统一风险地图的建模与学习集成到支持部分可观测性下风险感知规划的框架中。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，相较于最先进的遮挡感知基线，最小碰撞时间提升0.78倍，平均碰撞时间提升1.67倍。

Conclusion: 该方法为部分可观测环境下的风险感知规划提供了一种全面且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Occlusion-aware prediction remains a critical challenge in autonomous driving due to the inherent uncertainty of unobserved regions. Existing approaches either overestimate risk based on reachable states or struggle to predict accurate trajectories under high occlusion uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified risk map modeling and learning framework for partially observable environments. Our method integrates traffic flow risk and collision risk through spatiotemporal modeling, enabling fine-grained assessment of occlusion-induced hazards. To address the scarcity of scenarios involving occluded interactions, we introduce a diffusion-based scenario generation framework that produces realistic yet adversarial scenarios. We integrate the modeling and learning of a unified risk map into a framework that supports risk-aware planning under partial observability. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset show that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art occlusion-aware baseline, improving minimum time-to-collision by 0.78 times and average time-to-collision by 1.67 times. The proposed framework offers a comprehensive and practical solution for risk-aware planning in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [179] [Imagine2Real: Towards Zero-shot Humanoid-Object Interaction via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22272)
*Jiahe Chen,ZiRui Wang,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Xiaojie Niu,Weishuai Zeng,Tianfan Xue,Xiaowei Zhou,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Imagine2Real框架，通过统一4D点轨迹表示和稀疏关键点跟踪，无需几何先验和重定向，实现零样本全身人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人机交互受限于高质量3D数据的稀缺，现有方法存在表征不对齐（依赖几何先验）和重定向复杂（形态不匹配、变形成本高）两大瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将机器人和物体运动统一为4D点轨迹，消除表征不对齐；2) 仅跟踪基础、手部和物体的稀疏关键点，完全绕过重定向过程；3) 利用行为基础模型的潜在空间作为搜索域，维持自然步态；4) 采用渐进式训练策略，基于简单跟踪奖励学习鲁棒行为。

Result: 实现零样本物理部署，可在运动捕捉系统中直接运行。

Conclusion: Imagine2Real通过稀疏跟踪和潜在空间搜索，有效解决了表征不对齐和重定向复杂问题，为零样本全身人机交互提供了灵活、无需几何先验的方案。

Abstract: Whole-body Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D data. While video generative priors offer a promising alternative, existing methods suffer from \textit{Representation Misalignment} due to their reliance on geometric priors (e.g., explicit CAD models), and \textit{Retargeting Complexity} arising from intensive morphing and morphological mismatch. We propose Imagine2Real, a zero-shot HOI framework for flexible, geometry-free interaction. To resolve misalignment, we formulate robot and object motions as unified 4D point trajectories. To overcome retargeting complexity, our Keypoints Tracker tracks only sparse critical points (base, hands, and object), entirely bypassing the error-amplifying retargeting process. To maintain natural gaits despite these sparse signals, we utilize the latent space of a Behavior Foundation Model (BFM) as the tracker's search domain. Using a progressive training strategy, Imagine2Real learns robust behaviors with simple tracking rewards, enabling zero-shot physical deployment within a motion capture(mocap) system.

</details>


### [180] [Spatial Memory for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22283)
*Pengteng Li,Weiyu Guo,He Zhang,Tiefu Cai,Xiao He,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOMA是一个为视觉-语言-动作模型配备持久空间记忆的框架，通过多视角扫描构建空间语义表征，使模型能够在目标物体超出视野时仍能进行有效的操作推理，在五项真实世界任务中显著提升了成功率并实现了近单次抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型隐式假设任务相关物体始终可见，当目标超出相机视野时会产生脆弱和反应式的行为，缺乏对视野外物体的推理能力。

Method: SOMA包含三个组件：空间记忆构建（通过可移动头部相机进行多视角扫描聚合角度感知观测为统一空间语义表示）、动态记忆精炼（维护全局时间一致性）、以及上下文记忆检索（在操作过程中激活与指令相关的空间线索）。

Result: 在五项具有挑战性的真实世界视野外操作任务中，SOMA不仅提高了任务成功率，还产生了更快的目标定位、减少视角搜索、以及在部分可观测条件下实现近乎单次抓取等定性不同的操作行为。在RoboCasa GR1和SimplerEnv上的实验也验证了其在全可观测设置下的有效性。

Conclusion: SOMA通过持久空间记忆机制有效解决了VLA模型在目标物体超出视野时的操作问题，提升了操作行为的鲁棒性和效率，且在全可观测场景下同样有效。

Abstract: We introduce SOMA, the Spatial Memory framework for Out-of-Vision Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Most existing VLAs implicitly assume that task-relevant objects are always visible, leading to brittle and reactive behaviors when targets fall outside the camera's field of view. SOMA addresses this limitation by equipping VLAs with a persistent spatial memory constructed from multi-view observations acquired via a movable head camera, enabling reasoning beyond the current visual frustum. The framework consists of three components: Spatial Memory Construction, which aggregates angular-wise observations into a unified spatial-semantic representation through scanning; Dynamic Memory Refinement, which maintains global consistency over time; and Contextual Memory Retrieval, which activates instruction-relevant spatial cues during manipulation. We evaluate SOMA on five challenging real-world out-of-vision manipulation tasks, including multi-step and dual-arm scenarios where target objects are initially invisible. Experimental results show that SOMA not only improves task success rates, but also induces qualitatively different manipulation behaviors, with faster target localization, reduced viewpoint search, and near one-shot grasping under partial observability. Additional experiments on RoboCasa GR1 and SimplerEnv further validate the effectiveness of SOMA's memory design under conventional fully observable settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [181] [How can reasoning capability empower the AI copilot robot in endoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22322)
*Guankun Wang,Long Bai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了推理能力在基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的AI副手手术机器人中的应用潜力，指出推理驱动能够将机器人从被动执行者转变为认知协作者，提升内镜手术的精准度、安全性和可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理能力在复杂逻辑推理和机器人决策中已取得显著进展，但在内镜手术场景下的AI副手机器人（基于VLA模型）中尚未被探索，而手术环境中的多模态信息整合、手术意图理解及隐藏组织动力学推断等需求亟待解决。

Method: 提出通过推理能力增强VLA模型，使其能够整合多模态线索、解读手术意图、推断隐藏组织动力学，从而实现从被动执行到认知协作的转变。

Result: 推理驱动的自主性有望将AI副手机器人从反应式执行器转变为认知协作者，提升临床实践中的精准度、安全性和可持续性。

Conclusion: 推理能力的引入能够有效缓解术中不确定性及外科医生的认知负担，推动AI副手机器人成为真正的认知协作伙伴。

Abstract: Reasoning capability has significantly advanced complex logical inference and robotic decision-making in general domains. However, its potential in the Artificial Intelligence (AI) copilot robot-particularly implemented based on the Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model-remains unexplored in endoscopic surgery. Effective reasoning should enable AI copilot robots to integrate multimodal cues, interpret surgical intent, and infer hidden tissue dynamics, thereby alleviating intraoperative uncertainty and cognitive burden on surgeons. Properly implemented, reasoning-driven autonomy can transform AI copilot robots from reactive executors into cognitive collaborators, enhancing precision, safety, and sustainability in clinical practice.

</details>


### [182] [Real-Time Auto-Optimization in Unknown Environments via Structure-Exploiting Dual Control for Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22431)
*Shiying Dong,Haoyang Yang,Qiwei Liu,Wen-Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对未知环境下的自动优化问题，提出了一种快速数值双控探索与利用（DCEE）方法。通过挖掘DCEE中奖励函数固有的凸-非线性结构，开发了一种结构利用型数值方法——仅线性化非线性残差映射而保留凸外层损失，将子问题转化为可可靠求解的结构化凸形式。所提广义Gauss-Newton Hessian近似仅依赖一阶导数且半正定，支持快速在线计算。在车辆巡航自动优化问题上的仿真和硬件在环实验表明，该方法在典型车辆嵌入式CPU上以83 μs的微秒级最大计算时间实现了约一个数量级的加速，并提升了控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCEE方法在主动学习中计算负担大，且主要依赖标准优化包或显式梯度类更新律，未能充分利用DCEE的数值结构。

Method: 揭示DCEE奖励函数具有凸-非线性结构，将利用项与探索项统一为带凸外层损失的非线性残差映射；仅线性化非线性残差映射而保留凸外层损失，将子问题转化为结构化凸形式；采用广义Gauss-Newton Hessian近似（仅一阶导数、半正定），实现快速在线计算。

Result: 在车辆巡航自动优化问题上，相比现有方法，所提方法提升了控制性能，实现了约一个数量级的加速，在典型车辆嵌入式CPU上最大计算时间仅为83 μs。

Conclusion: 所提结构利用型数值方法有效解决了DCEE的计算瓶颈，兼具理论上的凸优化可靠性与实际部署的实时性要求，适用于未知环境下的自动优化问题。

Abstract: This paper develops a fast numerical dual control for exploration and exploitation (DCEE) method to address auto-optimization problems in unknown environments. In auto-optimization problems, the optimal operating condition is unknown a priori and may vary with the environment. As in classical dual control techniques, computational burden remains a major concern in DCEE for active learning. Existing DCEE methods provide a principled exploration-exploitation objective, but mainly realized through standard optimization packages or explicit gradient-type update laws, where the numerical structure of the DCEE has not been fully exploited. This paper shows that the reward function in DCEE has an inherent convex-over-nonlinear structure, where the exploitation and exploration terms form a unified nonlinear residual map equipped with a convex outer loss. Benefiting from this structure, a structure-exploiting numerical method is developed by linearizing only the nonlinear residual map while preserving the convex outer loss. Thus, each subproblem is transformed into a structured convex form that can be solved reliably. The resulting generalized Gauss-Newton Hessian approximation is positive semidefinite and depends only on first-order derivatives, thereby supporting fast online computation. The proposed method is evaluated on a vehicle cruising auto-optimization problem and compared with existing methods. Simulation and hardware-in-the-loop experimental results show that the proposed method improves control performance and achieves a speedup of approximately one order of magnitude, with a microsecond-level maximum computation time of only 83 μs on a typical vehicle embedded CPU.

</details>


### [183] [Terminal Constraint Model Predictive Control for Image-Based Visual Servoing of UAVs with Kalman Filter-Based Moment Loss Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22443)
