*X. Wang,Y. Cao,W. L. W. Leong,Y. R. Tan,S. Huang,S. H. R. Teo,C. Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于终端约束模型预测控制（TC-MPC）和卡尔曼滤波的视觉伺服控制框架，解决了传统IBVS在无人机控制中存在的稳定性丧失和视觉特征丢失问题，并通过实时实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBVS控制器存在两个关键问题：一是由于输入和状态约束，在目标附近会失去闭环稳定性；二是在剧烈运动下，基于矩的视觉特征间歇性丢失导致控制失败。

Method: 提出终端约束模型预测控制（TC-MPC）框架，显式引入终端状态约束和终端代价函数到IBVS误差动力学中，确保递推可行性和闭环稳定性；同时集成基于卡尔曼滤波的状态预测机制，在视觉退化期间预测图像矩的时间演化以维持控制连续性。

Result: 通过实时无人机视觉伺服实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TC-MPC结合卡尔曼滤波能够有效解决IBVS在约束下的稳定性问题和视觉特征丢失时的控制失败问题。

Abstract: Image-Based Visual Servoing (IBVS) provides an efficient vision-guided control paradigm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) by directly regulating image-space errors. However, conventional IBVS controllers are vulnerable to two critical issues: loss of closed-loop stability near the target due to input and state constraints, and control failure caused by intermittent loss of moment-based visual features under aggressive motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a terminal-constraint model predictive control (TC-MPC) framework for IBVS, integrated with a Kalman filter (KF)-based state-prediction mechanism. The TC-MPC explicitly incorporates terminal-state constraints and a terminal cost into the IBVS error dynamics, ensuring recursive feasibility, improved convergence behavior, and closed-loop stability under control and state constraints. In parallel, the Kalman filter predicts the temporal evolution of image moments during short-term visual degradation, enabling the controller to preserve control continuity when moment measurements are partially unavailable. The proposed approach is validated through real-time UAV visual servoing experiments.

</details>


### [184] [Steins;Gate Drive: Semantic Safety Arbitration over Structured Futures for Latency-Decoupled LLM Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22456)
*Anjie Qiu,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SteinsGateDrive提出了一种延迟解耦的规划器-运行时架构，通过世界线隐喻让LLM在最终控制时刻之前选择反事实驾驶未来，并将推理延迟从+3.07秒降低至-0.01秒，同时保持无碰撞安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端LLM驱动的智能体推理延迟超过车辆步进控制窗口，而学习的世界模型通常将未来生成和动作选择耦合在大型循环中，导致无法满足实时性要求。

Method: 提出SteinsGateDrive架构，包含：(1) 世界线生成器构建三种角色（alpha标称自我条件未来、beta交互反事实、gamma危险应激未来）；(2) 选中的分支成为带有时限、有效性/中止条件、回退和权威的StrategicForecast；(3) 运行时仅在安全合约有效时复用选中的预测，通过原子谓词运行时检查而非漂移分数来保障安全。

Result: 在匹配种子正常高速公路协议下（10种子，20步），GPT-5.4 mini将有效延迟从1秒视野下的+3.07秒降低到4秒视野下的-0.01秒，同时保持了无碰撞安全边界。

Conclusion: 架构的安全贡献来自原子谓词运行时检查，而非漂移分数（漂移分数仅作为刷新频率旋钮），证明了延迟解耦架构在LLM驱动的自动驾驶中的可行性。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted LLM driver agents provide useful semantic judgments, but their inference latency exceeds stepwise vehicle-control windows. Learned world models predict futures, but they usually keep future generation and action selection inside large coupled loops. We present SteinsGateDrive, a latency-decoupled planner-runtime architecture in which the worldline metaphor from the eponymous story names one plausible consequence of an intervention: the LLM selects counterfactual driving futures before the final control instant, and a runtime reuses the selected forecast only while safety contracts remain valid. The generator builds three world-line roles: alpha nominal ego-conditioned futures, beta interaction counterfactuals around nearby vehicles, and gamma hazard-stress futures such as braking, cut-ins, or blocked corridors. The selected branch becomes a typed StrategicForecast with horizon, validity/abort conditions, fallback, and authority. On a within-subject, matched-seed normal-highway protocol with 10 seeds and 20 steps, GPT-5.4 mini reduces effective lag from +3.07 s at 1-second horizon to -0.01 s at 4-second horizon while preserving the measured no-collision safety boundary. The architecture's safety contribution comes from the atom-predicate runtime check, not from the drift score, which functions as a refresh-frequency knob.

</details>


### [185] [Quantifying Full-Body Immersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22521)
*Alihan Bakir,Ekrem Yüksel,Fabio Zuliani,Neil Chennoufi,Francesco Bruno,Jamie Paik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模块化机器人表面单元的全身体沉浸（FBI）框架，通过三个层次的沉浸（视听、物理、全身体）实现虚拟与现实世界的深度融合，支持从个人到多用户的可扩展、动态交互体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前虚拟现实体验主要局限于视听层面，缺乏真正意义上的全身体互动与物理反馈。作者旨在打破虚实界限，通过可扩展的模块化物理平台实现用户与虚拟环境的全身动态交互，推动沉浸式技术从感官模拟向人机共生演进。

Method: 提出三层次沉浸范式：视听沉浸（感官真实感）、物理沉浸（触觉反馈）和全身体沉浸（FBI，动态身体互动）。核心是基于自然自适应设计启发的模块化机器人表面单元，构成可扩展、可分布的平台，能够实时响应交互并动态调整力、形状和运动反馈，覆盖从个人到多用户的各种规模。

Result: 实现了可适应任意规模的沉浸式环境渲染系统，支持实时动态物理反馈（力、形状、运动），使用户能够通过全身运动与虚拟空间进行共生式互动，显著提升了传统VR的沉浸深度。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了真实与虚拟世界之间的鸿沟，重新定义了人类与虚拟环境的共存方式，为沉浸式技术及人机环境融合新时代奠定了基础。

Abstract: Humanity is at the forefront of yet another digital revolution, where the lines between real and virtual worlds are dissolving, reshaping how we perceive and interact with our surroundings. In this context, we introduce a transformative paradigm for immersive virtual experiences centered around whole-body kinetic interactions. Our approach redefines immersion through three distinct levels: audio-visual immersion, capturing sensory realism; physical immersion, delivering haptic feedback; and full-body immersion (FBI), where dynamic bodily interaction integrates seamlessly with virtual environments. At the core of this innovation lies a scalable, distributable platform based on modular robotic surface units inspired by the adaptive designs of nature. These units enable the rendering of immersive environments at any scale, from intimate personal experiences to expansive multi-user settings, dynamically adapting to interactions in real-time. The modular system distributes force, shape, and motion feedback throughout entire spaces, replicating the physical characteristics of the environment and enabling new depth of engagement through FBI. By combining scalability, adaptability, and dynamic physical engagement, this framework bridges the gap between real and virtual worlds. It offers an unprecedented level of immersion where users can engage their entire bodies in symbiotic interactions with the virtual space. This work not only advances immersive technology but also redefines how humans and virtual environments coexist, setting a foundation for a new era of human-environment synthesis.

</details>


### [186] [SE3Kit: A Lightweight Python Library for Specialized Geometric Primitives in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22633)
*Daniyal Maroufi,Omid Rezayof,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SE3Kit是一个轻量级、纯Python的SE(3)和SO(3)李群运算库，填补了现有机器人学库在轻量性与数学严谨性之间的空白，适用于嵌入式部署、快速原型设计和教育场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python机器人学领域缺乏既轻量又数学严谨的刚体变换库：现有框架要么依赖繁重（如SpatialMath、PyPose），要么缺乏机器人学专用功能（如SciPy）。

Method: 采用纯Python、仅依赖NumPy的实现方式，提供SE(3)和SO(3)李群运算，不引入深度学习或可视化软件的额外开销。

Result: 成功构建了一个在轻量性与数学严谨性之间取得平衡的机器人学库，支持嵌入式部署、快速原型设计和教育用途。

Conclusion: SE3Kit填补了现有机器人学Python库在轻量级与数学严格性之间的空白，为机器人学社区提供了一个实用的工具。

Abstract: The Python robotics ecosystem faces a challenge: while many libraries exist for rigid body transformations, few are both lightweight and mathematically strict. This paper introduces SE3Kit, a lightweight Python library efficient operations on the Special Euclidean Group SE(3) and the Special Orthogonal Group SO(3). Unlike established frameworks that require heavy dependencies (e.g., SpatialMath, PyPose) or general tools that lack robotics-specific features (e.g., SciPy), SE3Kit targets the gap between these extremes. It is designed for embedded deployment, rapid prototyping, and education while providing rigorous mathematical implementation. It provides a pure-Python, NumPy-only implementation of Lie Group operations, without the overhead of deep learning or other visualization software.

</details>


### [187] [Symmetries Here and There, Combined Everywhere: Cross-space Symmetry Compositions in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22639)
*Loizos Hadjiloizou,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出跨空间对称性组合框架，通过学习在构型空间和任务空间上联合等变的机器人策略，利用正向运动学的微分几何结构实现对称性的下降与提升，在双臂机器人仿真和真实实验中验证了多重对称性联合利用能提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常孤立处理机器人系统中的多重对称性，未能充分利用其组合潜力，导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 引入跨空间对称性组合框架，基于正向运动学映射的微分几何结构，将对称性从构型空间下降至任务空间，同时从任务空间提升至构型空间，在统一表示空间内实现对称性组合。

Result: 在双臂机器人的仿真和真实实验中验证了该方法的有效性，表明联合利用多重对称性相比单独使用能显著提升泛化性能。

Conclusion: 跨空间对称性组合是提升机器人策略泛化能力的有效途径，未来可推广至更复杂的机器人系统与任务场景。

Abstract: Robots exhibit a rich variety of symmetries arising from their mechanical structure and the properties of their tasks. Although many robotics problems exhibit several symmetries simultaneously, existing approaches typically treat them in isolation, failing to exploit their combined potential. This paper introduces cross-space symmetry compositions, a framework for learning robot policies that are jointly equivariant to multiple symmetries across configuration and task spaces. Leveraging the differential-geometric structure of the forward kinematics map, we both descend symmetries from configuration to task space and lift symmetries from task to configuration space, enabling their composition within a unified representation space. We validate our framework on simulated and real-world experiments on a dual-arm robot, demonstrating that jointly leveraging multiple symmetries yields improved generalization.

</details>


### [188] [Superhuman Safe and Agile Racing through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22748)
*Ismail Geles,Leonard Bauersfeld,Markus Wulfmeier,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明多智能体强化学习（MARL）能为现实世界交互提供必要的安全保障。以高速四旋翼无人机竞速为高难度测试平台，通过联赛自对弈训练智能体处理复杂空气动力学交互和战术机动，在超22m/s速度下超越人类冠军级飞行员，同时相比单智能体基线降低50%碰撞率，并实现零样本泛化到更安全的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主系统在隔离或模拟环境中表现超人类，但在共享动态的现实空间中仍显脆弱，根本原因在于物理应用中占主导地位的单智能体范式将其他参与者忽略或视为环境噪声，无法实现有效协调。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习，以高速四旋翼无人机竞速为测试平台，通过联赛自对弈（league-based self-play）训练智能体，使其学会处理可变数量竞速者之间的复杂空气动力学交互（如下洗气流）和战略机动（如主动避碰、超车）。

Result: 在超过22m/s速度的多机竞速中，智能体超越人类冠军级飞行员，同时相比最先进的单智能体基线降低50%碰撞率。此外，使用多样化人工智能体训练能零样本泛化到更安全的人机交互。

Conclusion: 实现鲁棒机器人共存的路径不在于孤立的单智能体安全约束，而在于多智能体交互的严苛训练要求。

Abstract: Autonomous systems have achieved superhuman performance in isolation or simulation, yet they remain brittle in shared, dynamic real-world spaces. This failure stems from the dominant single-agent paradigm for physical applications, where other actors are ignored or treated as environmental noise, preventing effective coordination. Here we show that multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the essential safety scaffolding required for real-world interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a high-stakes testbed, we train agents to navigate complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvering with a variable number of racers. Through league-based self-play, agents evolve sophisticated anticipatory behaviors, including proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and handling multi-agent physical interactions, including aerodynamic downwash. Our agents outperform a champion-level human pilot in multi-player races at speeds exceeding 22 m/s, while simultaneously reducing collision rates by 50 % compared to state-of-the-art single-agent baselines. Crucially, training with diverse artificial agents enables zero-shot generalization to safer human interaction. These results suggest that the path to robust robotic co-existence lies not in isolated safety constraints, but in the rigorous demands of multi-agent interaction. Multimedia materials are available at: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl

</details>


### [189] [GesVLA: Gesture-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model Embedded Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22812)
*Wenxuan Guo,Ziyuan Li,Meng Zhang,Yichen Liu,Yimeng Dong,Chuxi Xu,Yunfei Wei,Ze Chen,Erjin Zhou,Jianjiang Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GesVLA模型，通过将手势作为并行的指令模态融入视觉-语言-动作框架，采用双VLM架构和手势数据生成管线，有效解决了复杂场景中多相似物体导致的空间歧义问题，在多任务真实机器人实验中提升了目标定位精度和人机交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖文本指令，在包含多个相似物体的复杂场景中难以解决空间歧义问题，无法精确定位用户意图所指的物体。

Method: 1) 手势特征编码到潜在空间，参与高层推理和底层动作生成；2) 双VLM架构实现手势表示与动作策略的紧耦合；3) 可扩展的手势数据生成管线：将手部模型渲染到真实场景图像上；4) 两阶段训练策略，分别赋予模型手势感知和动作预测能力。

Result: 在多个真实机器人任务（积木操作、商品挑选等）上，引入手势一致性地提升了目标定位精度和人机交互效率，尤其在复杂杂乱环境中效果显著。

Conclusion: 手势作为并行指令模态能有效弥补文本指令在空间歧义上的不足，GesVLA为更直观、高效的人机交互机器人操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation by unifying perception and action. However, existing VLA systems primarily rely on textual instructions and struggle to resolve spatial ambiguity in complex scenes with multiple similar objects. To address this limitation, we introduce gesture as a parallel instruction modality and propose a Gesture-aware Vision-Language-Action model (GesVLA). Our approach encodes gesture features directly into the latent space, enabling them to participate in both high-level reasoning and low-level action generation, and adopts a dual-VLM architecture to achieve tight coupling between gesture representations and action policies. At the data level, we construct a scalable gesture data generation pipeline by rendering hand models onto real-world scene images. This reduces the sim-to-real visual gap while producing rich data with diverse motion patterns and corresponding pointing annotations. In addition, we employ a two-stage training strategy to equip the model with both gesture perception and action prediction capabilities. We evaluate our approach on multiple real-world robotic tasks, including a controlled block manipulation task for validation and more practical scenarios such as product and produce selection. Experimental results show that incorporating gesture consistently improves target grounding accuracy and human-robot interaction efficiency, especially in complex and cluttered environments. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/.

</details>


### [190] [AwareVLN: Reasoning with Self-awareness for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22816)
*Wenxuan Guo,Xiuwei Xu,Yichen Liu,Xiangyu Li,Hang Yin,Huangxing Chen,Wenzhao Zheng,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AwareVLN通过自感知推理机制（结构推理模块+自动数据引擎）赋予VLN模型对智能体状态与任务进度的端到端理解，在Habitat模拟器中显著超越此前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法要么依赖端到端VLM但缺乏对智能体-指令-场景关系的显式、可解释理解，要么构建场景地图进行启发式规划但依赖额外3D传感器且阻碍大规模视觉-语言预训练。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出AwareVLN框架，包含两大创新：(1) 结构推理模块——促进空间和任务导向的自感知；(2) 自动数据引擎——通过进度划分实现高效训练。整体为完全端到端、数据驱动的方式。

Result: 在Habitat模拟器的多个数据集上，AwareVLN显著优于此前最先进的视觉-语言导航方法。

Conclusion: 自感知推理机制能有效提升VLN智能体的导航能力，且无需额外3D传感器即可实现可解释的端到端规划。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to ground language instructions to its own movement within a visual environment. While state-of-the-art methods leverage the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for end-to-end action prediction, they often lack an explicit and explainable understanding of the relationships between the agent, the instruction, and the scene. Conversely, explicitly building a scene map for heuristic planning is intuitively appealing but relies on additional 3D sensors and hinders large-scale vision-language pre-training. To bridge this gap, we propose AwareVLN, a novel framework that equips the navigation model with a self-aware reasoning mechanism, enabling it to understand the agent's state and task progress in a fully end-to-end and data-driven manner. Our approach features two key innovations: (1) a structural reasoning module that fosters spatial and task-oriented self-awareness, and (2) an automatic data engine with progress division for effective training. Extensive experiments on various datasets in Habitat simulator show our AwareVLN significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOOD基准测试，系统研究LLM监控管道能否检测分布外（OOD）的对齐失败。通过将守卫模型与OOD检测器（马氏距离+困惑度）结合，召回率从39%提升至45%，且OOD检测带来的召回增益相当于使用20倍参数量的守卫模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全和对齐失败常源于分布外（OOD）情况，但现有监控管道对此类失败检测能力不足。

Method: 1) 构建MOOD基准，包含受限训练集和7个OOD测试集；2) 训练监控器并测试守卫模型在OOD场景的泛化能力；3) 提出将守卫模型与OOD检测器（马氏距离、困惑度等）组合的方法。

Result: 守卫模型在OOD场景下泛化失败；组合方法将召回率从39%提升至45%；OOD检测带来的增益相当于20倍参数量的守卫模型。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组件。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [192] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TO-Agents是一个将自然语言设计意图与迭代拓扑优化相结合的多智能体AI框架，通过视觉-语言推理和独立评判智能体自动调整求解器参数，实现从用户描述到3D打印原型的端到端设计，在两个案例中生成符合偏好设计的成功率比无视觉反馈的基线高6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化能生成高效结构，但设计者通常需要手动将定性设计意图（如视觉风格、产品体验或可制造性）转化为与这些偏好无直接关联的求解器设置，缺乏直接连接自然语言意图与优化过程的自动化方法。

Method: 提出TO-Agents多智能体框架，将用户自然语言设计描述转化为验证后的求解器输入，运行拓扑优化求解器，渲染生成的3D拓扑结构，利用多视角视觉-语言推理结合独立评判智能体对每个结果进行评估并修正求解器参数，最后通过制造智能体后处理最高排名设计以适配增材制造。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准和手机支架产品设计两个长周期任务中，系统在10次独立重复试验中每个案例均有60%的试验生成了符合偏好（分层分枝状树形形态）的设计，成功试验次数比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管道高出6倍。评判分数和人工评估表明管道能识别有效参数杠杆、从错误修正中恢复并扩展设计探索。

Conclusion: 智能体拓扑优化能使设计者从低层级参数调优转向更高层级的形式与功能规格说明，同时需注意过冲、选择性记忆、工具错放和参数推理错误等失效模式，为可靠的自主工程设计提供保障机制。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [193] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对USC犹太大屠杀基金会和耶鲁福图诺夫视频档案馆超过1600份证词的大规模计算分析，本文证实了两者在结构上存在差异，但也发现了显著重叠，从而挑战了简单"结构化vs.自由形式"的二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究领域长期以来认为USC基金会和耶鲁福图诺夫档案馆的证词在访谈风格上存在显著差异（结构化vs.自由形式），这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案建设。作者希望通过对这一基础性论断进行大规模量化检验，验证其准确性。

Method: 收集来自两个档案馆的超过1600份证词，采用话语分割、主题建模和基于大语言模型的分析方法，通过主题连贯性、采访者-幸存者互动动态以及问题类型分布来量化证词的"结构化程度"。

Result: 总体上证实了先前研究所指出的结构差异，但同时揭示了两个馆藏之间存在显著重叠——无论是在单个访谈内部还是跨叙事的常见模式中，这使简单的"结构化vs.自由形式"二分法变得复杂化。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅重新审视了大屠杀研究中的一个基础性论断，还提供了一个可扩展、可复制的比较语料库分析框架。作为概念验证，该方法可推广应用于数字口述历史、叙事分析以及公民科学标注平台的设计。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [194] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindLoom 是一个通过组合式思维模式工程来合成前沿推理数据的框架。它先将复杂问题的已验证解分解为思维模式链，再训练检索模型匹配问题状态与合适思维模式，以此迭代生成新问题，并通过分布对齐采样和难度标注来保证数据质量。在九个基准上的实验表明，微调后的模型在推理任务上优于基线和蒸馏方法，且具有良好的难度控制和推理模式覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法对问题难度背后的结构因素可见性有限，导致多样性不足和难度控制不稳定。

Method: 1) 将复杂问题解分解为思维模式链；2) 训练检索模型将问题状态与兼容思维模式匹配；3) 迭代应用检索到的思维模式组合新问题，并通过分布对齐采样保证多样性；4) 基于展开推演（rollout）的评判阶段标注难度并生成已验证的正确答案。

Result: 在涵盖五个STEM学科和四个数学推理任务的九个基准上，用MindLoom数据微调的模型表现优于基模型、知识蒸馏和外部数据基线。消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: MindLoom通过思维模式的组合工程有效提升了推理数据合成的多样性和难度可控性，为生成前沿级推理数据提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [195] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0，一个证据模型原型，旨在通过数据模型扩展、质量改进、FAIR/AI就绪性增强及与NAMs的整合，推动AOP-Wiki现代化，以支持计算生成AOP、定量AOP及下一代风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AOP-Wiki当前的数据模型和应用基础设施限制了其持续增长与发展，无法满足监管科学、生物医学和One Health背景下对AOP日益增长的需求。

Method: 提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0原型，通过数据模型扩展、内部质量改进、证据结构化以增强FAIR性和AI就绪性，以及改进AOP框架与NAMs的整合。

Result: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0具体展示了数据模型扩展，为支持计算生成AOP和定量AOP奠定了基础，并提升了对下一代风险评估的支撑能力。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0利用核心AOP原则和AI技术，展示了AOP-Wiki转型的方向，以更好地服务监管科学、生物医学和One Health应用。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [196] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过控制逻辑推理任务实验，发现AI使用强度与技能发展呈负相关：重度使用者表现弱于同龄人，而轻度使用者与不使用AI者相当。AI信息含量是关键中介因素——低信息AI既无短期收益又损害长期学习，高信息AI虽提升短期表现但对长期效果影响异质。AI既可增强独立推理（互补效应），也可能削弱推理能力（替代效应），需审慎监管AI使用以促进技能发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助决策对人类技能发展的影响，特别是AI使用强度和信息含量如何调节学习效果，以回答AI究竟是增强还是削弱人类能力这一核心问题。

Method: 采用控制实验方法，在逻辑推理任务中设置按需获取AI辅助的条件，通过比较不同AI使用强度（重度/轻度/不使用）和不同AI信息含量（高/低）条件下被试的即时表现和后续无AI时的表现，分析技能发展差异。

Result: (1) AI使用强度与技能发展负相关：重度使用者表现劣于可比同龄人，轻度使用者与不使用AI者表现相似；(2) AI信息含量是关键中介：低信息AI既不提升即时表现，移除后表现下降，整体学习更差；高信息AI提升短期表现，且平均不损害长期表现，但存在异质性效应。

Conclusion: AI对技能发展的影响取决于使用情境：高信息AI可与人类技能形成互补，增强独立推理；低信息AI则可能替代人类推理，削弱技能发展。因此需通过调节AI的访问和使用方式来促进而非阻碍人类能力成长。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [197] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttuneBench是一个基于真实多轮人机对话的情感智能基准测试，包含200段对话和逐轮情感标注。通过评估11个模型，发现情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和回复质量判断是相互独立的能力，其中偏好对齐和回复质量比情感标签准确度更能区分模型优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感智能基准依赖合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，未能直接衡量模型在真实对话过程中推理和响应用户情感状态的能力。

Method: 基于200段真实多轮人机对话，参与者与匿名LLM对话并提供逐轮的情感状态、模型行为和偏好回复标注；评估11个模型在情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和回复质量判断四个维度上的表现。

Result: 模型在四个能力维度上的排名基本独立，表明情感智能行为可分解为可分离的能力；偏好对齐和回复质量判断比情感标签准确度更具模型区分度。

Conclusion: 情感智能行为需要预测特定用户在特定语境中想要的回复类型，这一区别会被聚合评分掩盖，且单轮或合成格式无法直接跨轮捕获。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [198] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出基于AI平台对话日志测量的职业暴露度实际上反映的是平台用户构成而非真实劳动力结构，导致估计偏差：不同平台输入使ChatGPT后就业系数变化1.9倍，消费者与企业渠道估计符号相反，按BLS劳动力份额重新加权后估计值衰减42%-93%。作者形式化了非经典测量误差，推导了就业弹性概率极限和部分识别边界，发现偏差低估替代效应甚于增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛使用AI平台对话日志来衡量职业暴露度，但这类测量是否真实反映劳动力市场结构存在疑问。本文旨在揭示该测量方法存在平台用户选择偏差，并量化其因果推断的影响。

Method: 保持结果变量、样本、控制变量和估计量不变，仅改变平台输入来源，比较不同平台（消费者vs企业渠道）估计系数的差异；通过BLS劳动力份额重新加权调整；形式化非经典测量误差模型，推导概率极限和部分识别边界。

Result: 不同平台输入使ChatGPT后就业系数变化1.9倍；消费者与企业渠道估计符号相反；按BLS劳动力份额重新加权后估计值衰减42%-93%；偏差低估替代效应甚于增强效应。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在严重的平台用户选择偏差，不能简单等同于劳动力市场真实暴露度；研究者需谨慎使用此类测量，或通过加权调整等方法缓解偏差。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [199] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合因果关系与论证推理的可解释AI方法，先通过因果发现识别变量间因果关系，再转化为双极论证框架，利用半稳定语义找出解释模型预测原因的特征扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法能识别哪些特征对模型预测重要，但无法解释为什么模型会做出特定决策，缺乏因果层面的推理能力。

Method: 首先使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系；然后将因果结构转化为双极论证框架（BAF），表示特征间的支持与对立关系；最后采用半稳定语义（semi-stable semantics）找出能够解释模型为何选择特定结果的特征扩展。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上验证了该方法，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 通过因果发现与论证推理的结合，该方法能够提供比传统事后解释方法更丰富的解释信息，明确说明模型为何做出特定预测决策。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [200] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过综述70篇论文和调查106位专家，系统定义了AI谄媚行为（sycophancy）的概念，提出了一个包含两个维度的分类法：(1) 谄媚对象（用户的立场/信念 vs. 个人特质/情感），(2) 表现方式（显性直接 vs. 隐性微妙）。研究发现当前研究集中于对用户信念的显性谄媚，而隐性和针对人格的谄媚被忽视；专家虽普遍认为谄媚是重大问题（94.3%），但对具体行为是否构成谄媚存在严重分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI谄媚缺乏统一定义，不同研究使用同一术语描述不同行为，导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统对不同形式的谄媚表现不一致。迫切需要建立共享词汇表以协调研究和治理。

Method: (1) 综述70篇论文，建立行为定义和测量的分类法；(2) 调查106位AI谄媚及相关领域专家，分析共识与分歧。

Result: 分类法揭示了两个维度：谄媚对象（信念 vs. 人格/情感）和表达方式（显性 vs. 隐性）。现有研究集中于对用户信念的显性谄媚，隐性和人格导向行为研究不足。专家94.3%认为谄媚是重大问题，但对具体行为的界定存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI谄媚是一个广泛的行为家族，不同形式具有不同的测量挑战、干预需求和治理含义。所提出的分类法为理解和应对这些行为提供了共享词汇表。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [201] [Trace2Skill: Verifier-Guided Skill Evolution for Long-Context EDA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21810)
*Zijian Du,Nathaniel Pinckney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Skill是一个无需微调硬件专用模型、仅通过测试时扩展来提升硬件LLM Agent能力的框架。它通过挖掘Agent的反复执行轨迹，提取成功与失败模式，转化为稠密诊断和预言机经验，并利用预言机、变异器和选择器的循环生成任务专用技能，指导后续搜索、编辑、验证和恢复。结合有界运行时稠密验证器反馈，Trace2Skill在复杂Verilog设计任务上显著提升通过率，甚至取得此前未解决任务的突破性通过，无需高质量微调数据或模型权重更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂Verilog设计任务中，硬件LLM Agent需在大型仓库快照中定位验证器相关的RTL、测试平台、包含路径和构建依赖，进行精确编辑，并从稀疏隐藏验证器失败中恢复——这些挑战导致现有Agent表现不佳。现有方法依赖专用模型微调或简单增加采样，无法有效利用失败信息指导后续行为。

Method: Trace2Skill将Agent的自然语言技能视为可进化策略，通过三个核心组件运作：(1) 预言机（Oracle）——从成功/失败轨迹中提取稠密诊断和预言机经验；(2) 变异器（Mutator）——基于诊断变异生成新技能；(3) 选择器（Selector）——保留有效技能。同时支持有界运行时稠密验证器反馈，返回功能观察结果但隐藏参考实现，连接技能文本与验证证据以指导进化。

Result: 在击败种子CVDP Agent的困难任务上（包括也击败前沿编码Agent的任务），Trace2Skill配合稠密验证器反馈显著提升了任务通过率，并在先前未解决的任务上取得了突破性通过。该方法无需高质量微调数据、专用RTL模型训练或模型权重更新。

Conclusion: Trace2Skill提供了一种通用的测试时扩展策略，可推广至数字设计之外的其他可验证EDA任务，为硬件Agent能力的提升开辟了一条无需模型微调的可行路径。

Abstract: Complex Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) challenge hardware LLM agents because solving them requires localizing verifier-relevant RTL, testbenches, include paths, and build dependencies inside large repository snapshots, making precise edits, and recovering from sparse hidden-verifier failures. We present Trace2Skill, a test-time scaling framework that improves a hardware agent without RTL-specialized model fine-tuning. Rather than training a new model or only sampling more candidate solutions, Trace2Skill treats the agent's natural-language skill as an evolvable policy. It mines repeated rollout traces for success and failure modes, converts them into dense diagnostics and oracle lessons, and uses an oracle, mutator, and selector loop to produce task-specific skills that guide later search, editing, validation, and recovery. Because final pass/fail labels are often too coarse for hard failures, Trace2Skill also supports bounded runtime dense verifier feedback that returns sanitized functional observations while keeping hidden harnesses and reference solutions inaccessible to the agent. This feedback helps guide skill evolution and agent execution by connecting skill text, verifier evidence, and downstream behavior. Across hard CVDP tasks that defeat the seed CVDP agent, including tasks that also defeat frontier coding agents, Trace2Skill with dense verifier feedback substantially improves task pass rates and produces breakthrough passes on previously unsolved tasks, without requiring high-quality fine-tuning data, specialized RTL model training, or model weight updates. The same framework provides a general test-time scaling strategy that can extend beyond digital design to other verifiable EDA tasks.

</details>


### [202] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL框架，从人群偏好中提取安全对齐技能，通过分层策略组合以安全解决下游任务，在无显式安全奖励情况下降低安全成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中人群偏好数据隐含安全准则，但不同用户偏好存在差异，直接组合偏好学习奖励与任务奖励存在固有局限性，亟需一种能从众包偏好中发现共享安全准则并迁移到下游RL任务的方法。

Method: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL分层框架：从人群偏好中提取安全对齐的技能，通过高层策略组合这些技能以安全地解决下游任务。

Result: 在安全RL环境和LLM式任务中，该方法在不使用显式安全奖励的情况下显著降低安全成本，任务性能与使用真实安全信号训练的oracle方法相当。

Conclusion: 从人群偏好中提取共享安全准则并作为技能迁移是可行的，分层框架有效解决了直接奖励组合的局限性，为无显式安全信号下的安全RL提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can reveal implicit objectives such as safety considerations that go beyond task completion. In this work, we focus on the common safety criteria embedded in crowd preference datasets, where different users may express distinct preferences or objectives, yet follow similar safety principles. Our aim is to discover shared safety criteria from crowd preferences and then transfer them to downstream RL tasks to regularize agent behavior and enforce safety. We first show that direct reward combination-optimizing a preference-learned reward model together with downstream task rewards-has inherent limitations. Motivated by this, we propose Safe Crowd Preference-based RL, a hierarchical framework that extracts safety-aligned skills from crowd preferences and composes them via a high-level policy to safely solve downstream tasks. Experiments across safe RL environments and a preliminary LLM-style task with diverse user goals and shared safety constraints demonstrate that our approach substantially lowers safety costs without access to explicit safety rewards, while achieving task performance comparable to oracle methods trained with ground-truth safety signals.

</details>


### [203] [FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21832)
*Xinhang Yuan,Zexi Huang,Anjia Cao,Xudong Lu,Zikai Wang,Penghao Zhou,Chang Liu,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLUID提出了一种完全移除候选侧物品ID的直播推荐框架，通过多模态编码器生成离散层次化编码（LUCID），结合后融合无ID设计，在数十亿用户的工业场景下显著提升了冷启直播推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播推荐中，直播间通常仅持续数十分钟，其ID嵌入在传统协同过滤范式下长期处于冷启动状态，基于ID的排序模型难以泛化。

Method: 提出FLUID框架：1）跨领域多模态编码器，在短视频和直播上联合训练生成离散层次化编码LUCID；2）后融合、无ID设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立Token注入；3）在线增量训练下采用分段预热稳定训练。

Result: 在超十亿用户的工业直播推荐系统中部署，在线指标显著提升：质量观看时长+0.55%，冷启房间观看量+2.05%，活跃时长+0.05%。

Conclusion: FLUID是首个在生产级直播排序器中完全淘汰候选侧物品ID的框架，通过多模态离散编码有效解决了直播场景的冷启动问题。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely heavily on ID-based collaborative filtering: each item is represented by a unique ID embedding that accumulates collaborative signals from user interactions. Livestreaming recommendation, however, faces a unique challenge in this paradigm: a live room typically broadcasts for only tens of minutes, so its item ID remains poorly learned in a persistent cold-start state and ID-centric ranking models fail to generalize. We present FLUID, the first framework to fully retire the candidate-side item ID from a production-scale livestreaming ranker. FLUID couples a cross-domain multimodal encoder, jointly trained on short videos and livestreams to produce discrete hierarchical codes (LUCID), with a late-fusion, ID-free design that injects slice-level and room-level LUCID as independent tokens, stabilized by a staged warmup under online incremental training. Deployed on our industrial livestreaming recommenders with a cross-platform combined user base of over one billion globally, FLUID delivers significant online gains of +0.55% Quality Watch Duration, +2.05% Cold-Start Room Views, and +0.05% Active Hours.

</details>


### [204] [Planning in the LLM Era: Building for Reliability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21902)
*Michael Katz,Harsha Kokel,Kavitha Srinivas,Shirin Sohrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了LLM用于规划的研究转向：从单次生成计划、混合搜索方法，转向在解构造时生成符号求解器，以实现可靠高效且推理时少依赖LLM的规划器。文章分类讨论了三种生成方法、当前局限及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体受到关注，规划作为其核心能力备受重视。早期LLM规划方法存在不完备、资源消耗大且泛化差等问题，需要更可靠高效的规划器生成范式。

Method: 本文对LLM时代规划领域的研究转向进行综述分析，将规划器生成方法划分为三大类别，并讨论其局限性和未来研究步骤。

Result: 识别出研究趋势从单次生成/混合搜索转向在解构造时生成可验证的符号求解器；归纳了三类规划器生成方法；指出当前方法仍存在局限，需进一步研究提升可靠性和效率。

Conclusion: LLM用于规划的研究正在发生更广泛的重组，未来方向是生成更可靠、资源高效且推理时对LLM依赖最小的可维护规划器。

Abstract: Growing attention to intelligent agents has put a spotlight on one of their central capabilities: planning. Early attempts to leverage large language models (LLMs) for planning relied on single-shot plan generation, followed by hybrid approaches that coupled LLMs with limited external search. These methods, unsound and incomplete by their very nature, often require substantial resources without yielding better solutions on unseen problems. As the limitations of LLMs become clearer, recent work has shifted toward using them at solution construction time -- generating symbolic solvers for a family of problems that can be verified and then used efficiently at inference time. This trend reflects the growing need for agents that are both reliable and resource-efficient. It also offers a path towards generating maintainable planners with minimal dependence on language models at inference time. In this paper, we argue that this shift reflects a broader realignment of the planning field in the LLM era. We examine three major categories of planner-generation methods, discuss their current limitations, and outline research steps towards a more reliable and efficient LLM-based generation of planners.

</details>


### [205] [AI-Enabled Serious Games: Integrating Intelligence and Adaptivity in Training Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21962)
*Priyamvada Tripathi,Bill Kapralos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本章探讨了人工智能（尤其是大语言模型、强化学习和基于智能体的架构）如何通过动态场景变化、上下文反馈、自适应节奏和学习者状态建模来支持严肃游戏中的实时教学适应，同时分析了AI集成所面临的透明度、可解释性、验证和计算成本等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严肃游戏在静态场景设计、创作瓶颈、学习者建模有限以及难以实施有意义的实时教学适应等方面存在持续挑战，亟需借助AI技术突破这些限制。

Method: 通过历史综述追踪从早期计算机辅助教学到智能辅导系统、动态难度调整、创作平台、学习分析及最新AI架构的发展脉络，并分析LLM、RL和智能体架构如何贡献更整合的智能与适应能力。

Result: 区分了教学智能（系统推断学习者知识并推理教学响应的能力）与适应性（在交互中修改教学行动的能力）两个核心概念，并指出AI可支持动态场景变化、上下文反馈、自适应节奏和学习者状态建模等新能力。

Conclusion: AI赋能的严肃游戏在提升智能与适应性方面有巨大潜力，但仍面临可解释性、验证、计算成本及长期学习效果证据不足等挑战。

Abstract: Serious games are widely used for learning and training across domains such as healthcare, defense, and education. Persistent challenges remain, however, including static scenario design, authoring bottlenecks, limited learner modeling, and difficulty implementing meaningful real-time instructional adaptation. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) introduce novel capabilities such as dynamic scenario variation, contextual feedback, adaptive pacing, and learner-state modeling that may help address some of these limitations. At the same time, integrating AI into serious games raises important questions related to validity, transparency, system control, and learner trust. This chapter examines how contemporary AI approaches may support real-time instructional adaptation in serious games. It distinguishes between instructional intelligence, defined as a system's capacity to infer learner knowledge and reason about pedagogically appropriate responses, and adaptivity, defined as the ability to modify instructional actions during interaction. A historical synthesis of adaptive learning systems is presented, tracing developments from early computer-assisted instruction through intelligent tutoring systems (ITS), dynamic difficulty adjustment (DDA), authoring platforms, learning analytics, and recent AI-enabled architectures. Building on this perspective, the chapter discusses how large language models (LLMs), reinforcement learning (RL), and agent-based architectures may contribute to more integrated forms of intelligence and adaptivity in serious games. It also highlights practical and research challenges associated with AI-enabled systems, including explainability, validation, computational cost, and the limited empirical evidence regarding long-term learning outcomes in AI-enabled serious games.

</details>


### [206] [Format-Constraint Coupling in Knowledge Graph Construction from Statistical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21974)
*Jingxuan Qi,Zhiqiang Ye,Yuxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示在统计CSV数据中，序列化格式与模式约束之间存在超加性耦合效应（format-constraint coupling），其联合影响远超独立效应之和（最高+1.180），且不匹配的格式-模式组合会导致事实覆盖率大幅下降（低于无约束基线）。通过探针和词元消融实验，发现该现象源于以列名引用为中心的表层形式锚定。更关键的是，三种标准检索模式会掩盖构建质量缺陷（delta≤1pp），而直接图访问可暴露高达+47.6pp的差距（p<0.0001）。为支持保真度评估，发布了包含15个数据集、1,892个黄金标准事实的CSVFidelity-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提取模式本不应降低知识图谱的保真度，但在统计CSV数据中，由于序列化格式与模式约束的交互作用，保真度可能受损。现有研究缺乏对此类耦合效应的系统分析，尤其缺乏对格式-模式不匹配导致灾难性后果的深入理解。

Method: 采用2x2因子设计实验，在6个数据集上测量格式与模式的独立及联合效应；通过探针分析和词元消融实验验证表层形式锚定假设；在多种格式-模式配对、GraphRAG宿主和LLM家族中进行对照验证；比较四种检索模式（无约束基线+三种标准检索模式+直接图访问）下的事实覆盖率差异。

Result: 格式-模式联合效应超出独立效应之和最多+1.180（6个数据集中4个的Bootstrap 95% CI严格为正）；不匹配组合导致4/6数据集事实覆盖率低于无约束基线；标准检索模式掩盖构建质量（delta≤1pp），直接图访问暴露差距达+47.6pp（p<0.0001）；发布了包含15个数据集、1,892个事实的CSVFidelity-Bench基准。

Conclusion: 格式-模式耦合是统计CSV知识图谱构建中不可忽视的因素，直接图访问是评估构建保真度的有效手段。现有检索模式可能掩盖保真度问题，建议采用直接图访问进行保真度感知评估。

Abstract: An extraction schema should not reduce knowledge graph fidelity. On statistical CSV, however, it can. We study country-by-year time-series matrices, a common layout on open-data portals. In this setting, serialization format and schema constraints interact super-additively. Their joint effect exceeds the sum of independent effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial, 6 datasets). Bootstrap 95% CIs are strictly positive on 4/6 datasets, with strongest evidence on wide Type-II matrices. More critically, a schema applied to a mismatched format can trigger catastrophic mismatch. Fact coverage falls below the unconstrained baseline on 4/6 datasets through entity inflation or extraction refusal. We call this observed pattern format-constraint coupling. Probing and token ablation support a surface-form anchoring explanation centred on column-name references. Controlled variants across format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families show the same direction within the measured scope; one LLM family shows only partial activation. The observation also has a diagnostic consequence. Three standard retrieval modes largely mask construction quality (delta <= 1pp), whereas direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp (p < 0.0001). To support fidelity-aware evaluation, we release CSVFidelity-Bench. It contains 15 datasets, 11 Type-II matrices, 4 Type-III tables, and 1,892 Gold Standard facts across 6 domains.

</details>


### [207] [Echo: Learning from Experience Data via User-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21984)
*Hande Dong,Xiaoyun Liang,Jiarui Yu,Jiayi Lin,Changqing Ai,Feng Liu,Wenjun Zhang,Rongbi Wei,Chaofan Zhu,Linjie Che,Feng Wu,Xin Shen,Dexu Kong,Xiaotian Wang,Qiuyuan Chen,Bingxu An,Yueting Lei,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Echo提出了一个通用框架，通过利用用户对智能体输出的精炼过程（用户驱动修正），将智能体与环境交互产生的原始经验数据转化为高质量训练信号，从而突破静态人类数据的性能瓶颈。在代码补全生产环境中，该框架将采纳率从25.7%提升至35.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态人类数据存在扩展成本高且受限于创作者知识的固有局限；虽然智能体部署可产生大量交互经验数据，但原始交互日志噪声大、信息密度低，直接用于模型训练效率低下。需要一种方法将原始经验转化为可学习的知识。

Method: Echo框架通过捕捉用户对智能体输出的精炼序列——用户出于对结果负责而将缺陷方案转化为验证解决方案的过程——系统性地将这些用户驱动的精炼过程转化为高质量的训练信号，用于持续对齐智能体与真实世界需求。

Result: 在代码补全生产环境中的大规模验证表明，Echo有效利用了这一流水线，将采纳率从25.7%提升至35.7%，打破了静态性能天花板。

Conclusion: Echo框架成功地将智能体与环境交互产生的原始经验数据，通过用户精炼这一自然反馈机制转化为可学习的训练信号，实现了智能体的持续对齐与性能突破。

Abstract: Static "human data" faces inherent limitations: it is expensive to scale and bounded by the knowledge of its creators. Continuous learning from "experience data" - interactions between agents and their environments - promises to transcend these barriers. Today, the widespread deployment of AI agents grants us low-cost access to massive streams of such real-world experience. However, raw interaction logs are inherently noisy, filled with trial-and-error and low information density, rendering them inefficient for direct model training.
  We introduce Echo, a generalized framework designed to operationalize the transition from raw experience to learnable knowledge, effectively "echoing" environmental feedback back into the training loop for model optimization. In today's agent ecosystem, user refinement serves as a primary source of such feedback: driven by responsibility for the outcome, users rigorously transform flawed agent proposals into verified solutions. These user-driven refinement sequences inherently distill agents' crude attempts into high-quality training signals. Echo systematically harvests these signals to continuously align the agent with real-world needs. Large-scale validation in a production code completion environment confirms that Echo effectively harnesses this pipeline, breaking the static performance ceiling by increasing the acceptance rate from 25.7% to 35.7%.

</details>


### [208] [The Log is the Agent: Event-Sourced Reactive Graphs for Auditable, Forkable Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21997)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActiveGraph 是一个反转传统智能体框架设计的运行时系统，以仅追加的事件日志为真相来源，工作图是日志的确定性投影，行为通过共享图进行协调，实现了确定性重放、低成本分叉和端到端溯源三大特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架以语言模型为中心，将日志和可观测性作为附加层，导致状态管理复杂、重放困难、溯源不完整。作者旨在通过架构反转来解决这些问题。

Method: ActiveGraph 采用事件日志作为唯一真相来源，工作图为日志的确定性投影；行为（普通函数、类、LLM 例程或类型化边上的逻辑）通过响应图变化并发射新事件来协调，组件间不直接指令对方。

Result: 系统提供三种传统检索-摘要记忆系统无法实现的特性：(1) 从日志中确定性重放任意运行过程；(2) 低成本分叉，在任意事件处分支而不重新执行共享前缀；(3) 端到端溯源，从高层目标到产生每个工件的具体模型调用。

Conclusion: 作者认为该架构特别适合自改进智能体，并扩展了 BabyAGI 谱系和先前的图记忆研究，但尚未进行实证验证。

Abstract: Most agent frameworks are built around the language model: a conversation loop comes first, then tools, then rules, and finally a logging layer bolted on for observability, with state persisted as retrievable "memory." We describe ActiveGraph, a runtime that inverts this arrangement. The append-only event log is the source of truth; the working graph is a deterministic projection of that log; and behaviors--ordinary functions, classes, LLM-backed routines, or logic attached to typed edges--react to changes in the graph and emit new events. No component instructs another; coordination happens entirely through the shared graph. This single design decision yields three properties that retrieval-and-summarization memory systems do not provide: deterministic replay of any run from its log, cheap forking that branches a run at any event without re-executing the shared prefix, and end-to-end lineage from a high-level goal down to the individual model call that produced each artifact. We present the architecture, a determinism contract that makes replay sound, and a worked diligence example whose full causal structure is reconstructable from the log alone. We discuss--without claiming to demonstrate--why this substrate is unusually well suited to self-improving agents, and how it extends the BabyAGI lineage and prior graph-memory research.

</details>


### [209] [Active Evidence-Seeking and Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models for Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22047)
*Chen Zhan,Xihe Qiu,Xiaoyu Tan,Xibing Zhuang,Gengchen Ma,Yue Zhang,Shuo Li,Peifeng Liu,Xiaoxiao Ge,Liang Liu,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于OSCE（客观结构化临床考试）的标准病人模拟器和可重复的主动诊断查询基准。实验表明，多轮证据寻求比全上下文评估的诊断准确性降低12.75%，支持证据质量降低24.36%，错误分析归因于过早诊断闭合和低效提问，提示静态基准可能高估交互式诊断场景中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在静态医学考试中表现良好，但临床诊断通常需要在不确定性下进行迭代证据收集。现有评估多基于全上下文静态评估，缺乏对主动诊断查询能力的有效度量。

Method: 受OSCE启发，构建标准病人模拟器及可控可重复的主动诊断查询基准；在468个病例和15个模型上进行实验，比较多轮证据寻求与全上下文评估的性能差异。

Result: 多轮证据寻求相比全上下文评估，诊断准确性下降12.75%，支持证据质量下降24.36%；错误分析发现主要归因于过早诊断闭合和低效提问。

Conclusion: 静态全上下文基准可能高估交互式证据寻求场景中的模型性能，需要互补的交互式评估以实现更安全的临床决策支持。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on static medical examinations, yet clinical diagnosis often requires iterative evidence gathering under uncertainty. Building on prior interactive evaluation efforts, we introduce an OSCE-inspired standardized patient simulator and a controlled, reproducible benchmark for active diagnostic inquiry. Across 468 cases and 15 models in our protocol, we observe that multi-turn evidence seeking reduces diagnostic accuracy by 12.75% and lowers supporting-evidence quality by 24.36% relative to full-context evaluation; error analyses associate these drops with premature diagnostic closure and inefficient questioning. Together, these results suggest that static full-context benchmarks may overestimate performance in interactive evidence-seeking settings, motivating complementary interactive assessment for safer clinical decision support.

</details>


### [210] [Enhancing Visual Token Representations for Video Large Language Models via Training-Free Spatial-Temporal Pooling and Gridding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22078)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Hanqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-GridPool，一种无需训练的视觉令牌增强方法，通过金字塔时间网格化（PTG）和基于范数的空间池化（NSP），有效提升视频大语言模型的时空交互表征，且即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM（如LLaVA系列）使用简单的池化或插值技术处理视觉令牌，忽略了视觉令牌的复杂时空动态关系，导致视频理解效率低下。

Method: 提出ST-GridPool方法，包含两个核心模块：1) 金字塔时间网格化（PTG），通过层次化时间网格捕捉多粒度时空交互；2) 基于范数的空间池化（NSP），利用令牌范数与语义丰富度的相关性，保留高信息量的视觉区域。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的大量实验表明，ST-GridPool能够持续提升视频LLM的性能，且无需昂贵的重新训练。

Conclusion: ST-GridPool是一种高效、即插即用的视觉令牌表征改进方案，能有效提升视频理解任务性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced video understanding tasks, yet challenges remain in efficiently compressing visual tokens while preserving spatiotemporal interactions. Existing methods, such as LLaVA family, utilize simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens. To bridge this gap, we propose ST-GridPool, a novel training-free visual token enhancement method designed specifically for Video LLMs. Our approach integrates Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG), which captures multi-grained spatiotemporal interactions through hierarchical temporal gridding, and Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP), which preserves high-information visual regions by leveraging the correlation between token norms and semantic richness. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that ST-GridPool consistently enhances performance of Video LLMs without requiring costly retraining. Our method offers an efficient and plug-and-play solution for improving visual token representations. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-GridPool.

</details>


### [211] [A Camera-Cooperative ISAC Framework for Multimodal Non-Cooperative UAVs Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22090)
*Wenfeng Wu,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种相机协同的 ISAC（CC-ISAC）框架，通过视觉与回波多模态融合，实现无人机波束引导与跟踪，在 DeepSense 6G 数据集上平均降低 71% 波束引导开销和 1.69-11.15% 跟踪开销，同时保持高角度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作无人机检测面临单模态感知局限以及感知与通信共享资源竞争的双重挑战，亟需一种高效的多模态协同方案来提升 ISAC 系统性能。

Method: 提出 CC-ISAC 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) V2EDA 模型，通过交叉注意力机制对齐视觉与回波域特征；(2) MMFE 模型，融合历史多模态数据与当前观测进行鲁棒状态估计。相机负责粗粒度空域监测，ISAC 负责细粒度高精度感知，形成互补感知闭环。

Result: 在 DeepSense 6G 数据集上，CC-ISAC 框架平均降低波束引导开销 71%，跟踪开销降低 1.69-11.15%，同时保持高角度估计精度。

Conclusion: CC-ISAC 框架有效缓解了感知与通信之间的资源竞争，在实现可靠无人机监视的同时释放了大量系统资源用于额外通信任务，代表了 ISAC 系统设计的实用进展。

Abstract: The detection of non-cooperative unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) presents significant challenges for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems due to the inherent limitations of single-modal perception and the competition for shared communication and sensing resources. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC) framework that employs multimodal sensing to enable efficient UAV beam steering and tracking. The proposed framework employs cameras for coarse-grained airspace monitoring and utilizes ISAC for fine-grained, high-precision sensing, forming a complementary perception loop that enhances both sensing accuracy and resource efficiency. Within this framework, two key modules are developed: (1) a Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA) model that aligns visual and echo-domain features through cross-attention mechanisms, and (2) a Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE) model that integrates historical multimodal data with current observations for robust state estimation. Extensive evaluations conducted on the DeepSense 6G dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average reduction of 71% in beam steering overhead and 1.69-11.15% in tracking overhead while maintaining high angular estimation accuracy. The CC-ISAC framework effectively mitigates resource contention between sensing and communication, enabling reliable UAV surveillance while freeing substantial system resources for additional communication tasks, thereby representing a practical advancement in ISAC system design.

</details>


### [212] [Knowledge Graph Re-engineering Along the Ontological Continuum (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22093)
*Enrico Daga,Valentina Tamma,Terry Payne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"本体论连续统"这一理论框架，通过语义vs语用、属性vs可供性两个正交维度来刻画知识图谱的建模实践，从而为神经符号AI中知识图谱的重工程化提供理论支撑。通过一个溯源知识案例研究验证了该框架的有效性，并提出了五项开放研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱建模实践高度多样化（从轻量级词汇表到丰富公理化的本体），导致集成和重用成本高昂且脆弱。这一问题在神经符号AI中尤为突出，因为连接神经组件和符号组件依赖于对知识图谱进行重工程化以适配新需求。尽管生成式AI提供了前所未有的自动化能力，但缺乏对KG空间的原则性理解，使得这种自动化在概念上缺乏根基。

Method: 提出本体论连续统（ontological continuum）作为缺失的概念化工具。该框架由两个正交区分定义：语义vs语用（semantics vs pragmatics）和属性vs可供性（properties vs affordances）。方法论立场是经验性的——通过观察真实世界的KG工程实践来定义存在理论，其结构可以通过形式概念分析（FCA）等形式化方法显式表达。

Result: 通过一个溯源知识（provenance knowledge）案例研究，展示了同一个关注点如何在本体论连续统的不同位置以不同方式呈现。提出了五项开放研究挑战，并邀请社区将本体论连续统发展为一个共享的研究议程。

Conclusion: 本体论连续统为描述、比较、导航和转换跨越全范围建模实践的知识图谱提供了一个统一的词汇表，有望为神经符号AI中KG的重工程化提供概念基础和理论指导。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become the primary vehicle for data integration and are critical to the success of modern AI, but the diversity of KG modelling practices, from lightweight vocabularies to richly axiomatised ontologies, makes integration and reuse expensive and brittle. This challenge is particularly acute in neuro-symbolic AI, where bridging neural and symbolic components depends on the ability to reengineer KGs to fit new requirements; GenAI now offers unprecedented automation capability, but without a principled understanding of the KG space, such automation remains conceptually ungrounded. We introduce the ontological continuum as that missing conceptualisation, a theoretical construct a theoretical construct whose characterisation framework is defined by two orthogonal distinctions: semantics vs pragmatics, and properties vs affordances; together these define a vocabulary to describe, compare, navigate, and transform KGs across the full range of modelling practices. The methodological stance is empirical: rather than prescribing how KGs should be modelled, the continuum aims to define a theory of the existent, derived from observation of real-world KG engineering practices and whose structure can be made formally explicit, for example, through Formal Concept Analysis (FCA). We ground the vision through a case study on provenance knowledge, showing how a single concern manifests differently across the continuum. We articulate five open research challenges and invite the community to develop the ontological continuum as a shared research agenda.

</details>


### [213] [MPDocBench-Parse: Benchmarking Practical Multi-page Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22100)
*Bangbang Zhou,Hangdi Xing,Yifan Chen,Jianjun Xu,Qi Zheng,Feiyu Gao,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MPDocBench-Parse是一个面向真实场景的多页文档解析基准，包含433份文档、3246页、15种类型，覆盖中英文，支持文档级端到端评估，实验发现现有模型在语义连续性、视觉内容解析和层次结构恢复方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准存在三个主要不足：仅关注单一任务、仅评估单页文本中心场景、缺乏对语义连续性、层次结构恢复和视觉内容保真度的细粒度评估，无法满足实际多页文档解析需求。

Method: 构建了包含433份人工标注文档（3246页）的多页文档解析基准，覆盖15种文档类型（中英文），设计了一套涵盖文本、表格、公式识别、截断文本/表格合并、图形提取、阅读顺序和标题层次恢复的综合评估协议。

Result: 现有模型在基础文本提取上表现良好，但在语义连续性整合、视觉内容解析和层次结构恢复方面存在明显局限性。

Conclusion: MPDocBench-Parse为文档解析向更真实场景推进提供了统一基础，弥补了现有基准在多页、多类型、细粒度评估方面的空白。

Abstract: Document parsing converts visually rich documents into machine-readable structured representations, forming a crucial foundation for information systems. Although many benchmarks have been proposed for document parsing, they remain inadequate for realistic scenarios. Existing benchmarks either focus on specific tasks or assess only single-page, text-centric settings, making them insufficient for practical multi-page parsing. Moreover, they lack fine-grained evaluation of semantic continuity, hierarchical structure recovery, and visual content preservation. To address these gaps, we propose MPDocBench-Parse, a benchmark for multi-page document parsing in real-world applications. It contains 433 manually annotated documents with 3,246 pages, covering 15 document types in English and Chinese, with diverse layout styles, and supports document-level end-to-end evaluation. We further design a comprehensive protocol for content fidelity and logical structure, covering text, table, and formula recognition, truncated text and table merging, figure extraction, reading order, and heading hierarchy recovery. Experiments show that, while existing models perform well on basic text extraction, they still suffer clear limitations in semantic continuity integration, visual content parsing, and hierarchical structure recovery. MPDocBench-Parse provides a unified foundation for advancing document parsing toward more realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [214] [ExComm: Exploration-Stage Communication for Error-Resilient Agentic Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22102)
*Woomin Song,Beomjun Kim,Daewon Choi,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Saket Dingliwal,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExComm是一种针对长程智能体测试时扩展的通信协议，通过周期性审计智能体信念状态检测跨智能体事实冲突，利用工具验证循环解决冲突，并通过软信念更新和轨迹多样化模块提升错误恢复和性能，在多个基准测试中平均提升5.0%-5.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体测试时扩展中的常见失败模式是错误传播——中间步骤引入的事实错误或无效推理会持续存在于智能体信念状态中并污染后续推理。现有测试时扩展方法对此控制有限，依赖智能体自我检测错误、在错误轨迹中选择或在错误已影响推理路径后才优化。

Method: ExComm协议包括：1) 周期性审计智能体信念状态以检测跨智能体事实冲突；2) 通过专用工具验证循环解决冲突，并返回简洁、有针对性的反馈；3) 软信念更新（追加验证后的反馈而非覆盖原有信念）；4) 轨迹多样化模块，将冗余轨迹重定向至正交策略，防止通信导致轨迹多样性坍塌。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和GAIA上使用Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite和Qwen3.5-4B进行实验，ExComm在平均性能上分别超过最强基线5.7%和5.0%。进一步分析表明其具备更好的错误恢复能力、有利的扩展行为、优于适应通信基线的多样性，以及最佳的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: ExComm通过主动检测和解决跨智能体冲突，配合软信念更新和轨迹多样化，有效缓解了长程智能体推理中的错误传播问题，为测试时扩展提供了更可控且高效的方法。

Abstract: A common failure mode in long-horizon agentic test-time scaling is error propagation, where factual errors or invalid deductions introduced at intermediate steps persist in the agent's belief state and contaminate later reasoning. Existing test-time scaling methods provide limited control over this process, as they often rely on agents to detect their own mistakes, select among flawed trajectories, or refine solutions only after errors have already shaped the reasoning path. We propose ExComm, a communication protocol for exploration-stage agentic test-time scaling. ExComm is motivated by the empirical observation that the majority of intermediate errors in parallel agentic reasoning produce detectable cross-agent factual conflicts. Leveraging the iterative structure of agentic workflows, ExComm periodically audits agent belief states to detect such conflicts, resolves them through a dedicated tool-based verification loop, and returns concise, targeted feedback to the involved agents. Corrections are incorporated through soft belief updates, which append verified feedback rather than overwriting existing beliefs. Furthermore, to prevent collapsing trajectory diversity due to communication, ExComm further introduces a trajectory diversification module that redirects redundant trajectories toward orthogonal strategies. Experiments on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and GAIA with Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite and Qwen3.5-4B show that ExComm consistently outperforms strong test-time scaling baselines, achieving average performance gains of 5.7% and 5.0% over the best-performing baselines, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate improved error recovery, favorable scaling behavior, stronger diversity than adapted communication baselines, and the best performance-cost trade-off among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [215] [ArborKV: Structure-Aware KV Cache Management for Scaling Tree-based LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22106)
*Yeqiu Chen,Ziyan Liu,Zhenxin Huang,Runquan Gui,Hong Wang,Lei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对Tree-of-Thoughts推理中KV缓存内存瓶颈问题，提出ArborKV框架，通过结构感知的驱逐策略实现约4倍峰值内存降低，同时保持接近完整保留的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tree-of-Thoughts将推理组织成树结构搜索，但大量保留部分轨迹的KV状态造成内存瓶颈，限制了搜索深度和宽度，需要高效的内存管理方案。

Method: 提出ArborKV框架，包含三个核心组件：轻量级价值估计器、树感知的分配策略、以及纯token提取式驱逐配合惰性再水化机制，利用搜索动态中KV复用模式进行结构感知的内存管理。

Result: 在ToT风格推理基准上实现高达约4倍峰值KV内存降低，同时保持接近完整保留的准确率，使得在固定设备预算下能够运行更大搜索配置。

Conclusion: ArborKV通过利用搜索树的结构特性进行选择性KV驱逐，有效解决了ToT推理中的内存瓶颈问题，在保证准确率的前提下大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: Recent progress in LLM reasoning has increasingly shifted from single-pass generation to explicit search over intermediate reasoning states. Tree-of-Thoughts (ToT) organizes inference to tree-structured search with branching and backtracking, but it substantially amplifies the Key--Value (KV) cache: retaining KV states for a frontier of partial trajectories quickly becomes a memory bottleneck that limits throughput and constrains search depth and width under fixed hardware budgets. We address this challenge by observing that KV reuse in ToT-style inference is governed by search dynamics: near-term decoding depends primarily on the active branch and its ancestors, whereas inactive subtrees have low short-term reuse probability yet must remain recoverable for backtracking. Motivated by this, we propose ArborKV, a structure-aware eviction framework that couples a lightweight value estimator with a tree-aware allocation policy, and performs purely token-extractive eviction with lazy rehydration to support revisits. Experiments on ToT-style reasoning benchmarks show that ArborKV achieves up to ~4x peak KV-memory reduction while preserving near-full-retention accuracy, enabling larger search configurations under fixed device budgets that would otherwise run out of memory.

</details>


### [216] [Perception or Prejudice: Can MLLMs Go Beyond First Impressions of Personality?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22109)
*Caixin Kang,Tianyu Yan,Sitong Gong,Mingfang Zhang,Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Bo Zheng,Huchuan Lu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato,Yifei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出接地人格推理（GPR）任务、构建MM-OCEAN数据集（1,104视频，5,320 MCQ），并基准测试27个MLLM，发现51%的正确评分缺乏可观察线索支撑，整体接地率仅0-33.5%，揭露了模型在人格感知中"正确得分但错误推理"的偏见缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM人格感知评测仅关注大五人格分数预测的数值准确性，未检验模型是否真正通过行为理解感知人格，还是仅依赖表面模式匹配进行预判。

Method: (i) 形式化GPR任务，要求MLLM通过评分-推理-接地链将每个大五评分锚定于可观察证据；(ii) 构建MM-OCEAN数据集，采用多智能体流水线+人工验证，包含时间戳行为观察、证据接地特质分析及七类线索接地多选题；(iii) 设计三级评估（评分、推理、接地）和四种样本级失败模式指标：偏见率（PR）、虚构率（CR）、整合失败率（IR）、整体接地率（HR），基准测试27个MLLM。

Result: 发现显著的偏见缺口：整个领域中51%的正确评分并未基于检索到的线索接地，整体接地率仅0-33.5%。

Conclusion: 揭露了MLLM在人格感知中"正确评分但错误推理"的脱节现象，为构建接地社会认知能力指明了路线图。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in human-facing roles where personality perception is critical, yet existing benchmarks evaluate this capability solely on numerical Big Five score prediction, leaving open whether models truly perceive personality through behavioral understanding or merely prejudge through superficial pattern matching. We address this gap with three contributions. (i) A new task: we formalize Grounded Personality Reasoning (GPR), which requires MLLMs to anchor each Big Five rating in observable evidence through a chain of rating, reasoning, and grounding. (ii) A new dataset: we release MM-OCEAN (1,104 videos, 5,320 MCQs), produced by a multi-agent pipeline with human verification, with timestamped behavioral observations, evidence-grounded trait analyses, and seven categories of cue-grounding MCQs. (iii) Benchmark and analysis: we design a three-tier evaluation (rating, reasoning, grounding) plus four sample-level failure-mode metrics: Prejudice Rate (PR), Confabulation Rate (CR), Integration-failure Rate (IR), and Holistic-grounding Rate (HR), and benchmark 27 MLLMs (13 closed, 14 open). The analysis uncovers a striking Prejudice Gap: across the field, 51% of correct ratings are not grounded in retrieved cues, and the Holistic-Grounding Rate spans only 0-33.5%. These findings expose a disconnect between getting the right score and reasoning for the right reason, charting a roadmap for grounded social cognition in MLLMs.

</details>


### [217] [Ratchet: A Minimal Hygiene Recipe for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22148)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Ratchet，一种让冻结LLM通过自然语言技能的自写、检索、策展和退役来管理技能生命周期的单智能体循环，在MBPP+和SWE-bench上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于冻结LLM的技能库方法（如Voyager）中，LLM撰写的技能对性能提升为0，而人类策展的技能可提升16.2%，说明瓶颈在于技能的生命周期管理而非技能编写本身。

Method: Ratchet采用单智能体循环，包含四种候选卫生机制：结果驱动的技能退役机制、有界活跃技能容量上限、元技能编写指导以及模式规范化。通过八组消融实验（A1-A8）验证各组件有效性。

Result: 在MBPP+ hard-100上，Claude Opus 4.7将pass@1从0.258基线提升至0.584滚动均值（峰值0.658），增益+0.328，远超无技能控制的+0.002；在SWE-bench Verified上峰值提升+0.22。最小有效配置仅需退役机制和元技能先验。

Conclusion: 技能生命周期管理是冻结LLM技能库的核心瓶颈；退役机制和元技能编写指导是核心组件，显式去重可被元技能自身替代；有界容量和退役阈值共同保证性能不会低于无技能基线。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries, pioneered by Voyager, let frozen LLM agents accumulate reusable knowledge without weight updates, yet recent evaluation shows that LLM-authored skills deliver $+0.0$pp over no-skill baselines while human-curated ones deliver $+16.2$pp: the bottleneck is not skill authoring but lifecycle management. We introduce \textbf{Ratchet}, a single-agent loop in which a frozen LLM writes, retrieves, curates, and retires its own natural-language skills. Ratchet integrates four candidate hygiene mechanisms: outcome-driven retirement, a bounded active-cap, meta-skill authoring guidance, and pattern canonicalisation. On MBPP+ hard-100 with Claude Opus 4.7, Ratchet lifts held-out pass@1 from a $0.258 \pm 0.047$ baseline to a late-window rolling mean of $0.584$ (peak $0.658 \pm 0.042$) across 100 rounds and 3 seeds, a $+0.328 \pm 0.018$ rolling-mean gain where the no-skill control drifts at $+0.002 \pm 0.005$; the same recipe transfers to an agentic solver on SWE-bench Verified ($+0.22$ peak lift over 20 rounds). Eight ablations (A1--A8) reveal that the minimal working recipe is smaller than our design suggests: retirement and the meta-skill authoring prior are load-bearing, while explicit deduplication (canonicalisation, cover-guard) is subsumed by the meta-skill itself. A non-divergence proposition shows that bounded cap and retirement threshold together prevent expected performance from drifting below the no-skills floor.

</details>


### [218] [IdleSpec: Exploiting Idle Time via Speculative Planning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22154)
*Daewon Choi,Kyunghyun Park,Woomin Song,Saket Dingliwal,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IdleSpec方法，利用智能体在等待环境观测结果时的空闲时间进行计划候选生成，通过渐进式和恢复式两种互补策略的采样与聚合，在不增加延迟的前提下提升LLM智能体性能，在GAIA、FRAMES和MLE-Bench等基准上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在执行多步推理时存在大量空闲等待时间，现有工作要么将其视为不可避免的开销，要么提出受限的解决方案，忽视了不同工具调用间计算预算的差异以及未来观测的不确定性，导致空闲时间利用率低。

Method: IdleSpec在空闲期间迭代生成计划候选，观测到达后聚合这些候选以指导下一步推理。为应对观测不确定性，通过后验反馈更新的学习分布，在渐进式（progressive）和恢复式（recovery）两种互补草稿策略之间进行采样。

Result: 在GAIA和FRAMES上，IdleSpec结合Gemini-2.5-Flash达到55.6%平均准确率，比未利用空闲时间的基线提升5.1%；在MLE-Bench上，Any Medal率提升高达9.1%。

Conclusion: IdleSpec是一种可扩展且通用的推理方法，能有效利用空闲计算提升智能体性能并最小化延迟开销，尤其在长周期任务中表现突出。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents solve complex tasks by leveraging multi-step reasoning with iterative tool calls and environment interactions, which incur idle time while waiting for observations. Despite the prevalence of idle time in most agentic scenarios, existing works treat it as an unavoidable overhead or propose restricted solutions that overlook varying computational budgets across different tool calls and future observation uncertainty, thereby leading to suboptimal utilization of idle time. In this paper, we introduce IdleSpec, a scalable and generic inference approach that leverages idle-time computation to improve agent performance while minimizing latency overhead. Specifically, IdleSpec iteratively generates plan candidates during idle periods and, once observations become available, aggregates them to guide the next reasoning step. For effective plan generation under observation uncertainty, IdleSpec samples between complementary drafting strategies (i.e., progressive and recovery) from a learned distribution that is updated via posterior feedback. Our experiments demonstrate that IdleSpec significantly improves agent performance in various agentic scenarios by effectively utilizing idle time. In particular, on the GAIA and FRAMES, IdleSpec achieves 55.6% average accuracy with Gemini-2.5-Flash, surpassing the vanilla baseline without idle-time usage by 5.1%. Furthermore, for MLE-Bench, which involves substantial delay from code executions, IdleSpec achieves performance gains of up to 9.1% on the Any Medal rate, highlighting its generalizability to long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [219] [ST-SimDiff: Balancing Spatiotemporal Similarity and Difference for Efficient Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22158)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Chaoqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-SimDiff框架，通过构建时空图并采用基于相似性的社区检测（保留静态信息）和基于时序差异的关键事件检测（保留动态信息）的双重选择策略，在不需训练的情况下大幅压缩视频视觉令牌数量，显著提升多模态大模型处理长视频的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs处理长视频时面临大量视觉令牌导致的高计算开销问题，而当前的令牌剪枝或合并方法主要基于重要性或相似性来减少冗余，忽略了视频内容中的变化和转折点这一关键维度，且缺乏对时空关系的协同建模。

Method: 提出无需训练的ST-SimDiff框架：1) 从视觉令牌构建时空图以统一建模复杂关联；2) 采用并行双选策略——基于相似性的选择利用社区检测保留代表性令牌以压缩静态信息，基于时序差异的选择精确定位内容变化点以保留捕捉关键动态变化的令牌。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在显著降低计算成本的同时，性能大幅超越当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: ST-SimDiff通过相似性与差异性的互补视角，结合时空图建模和双选策略，能用最少的令牌同时保留静态和动态内容，为长视频MLLM效率优化提供了有效且无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face significant computational overhead when processing long videos due to the massive number of visual tokens required.
